Result: 在腔QED环境中识别了多种光子-引力子散射通道（上转换、下转换、光子产生）；在半经典极限下观察到自发参量光子放大和双模压缩；在引力场量子化情况下发现放大饱和和光子子系统纯度损失；特征时间尺度与引力子占据数呈集体增强关系；在受激区域观察到类似Dicke型超辐射发射的增强耦合。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于腔体的引力子转换器理论框架，为探测引力波的量子特性提供了新途径，揭示了在量子引力背景下电磁-引力相互作用的丰富物理现象，包括集体增强效应和放大饱和行为。

Abstract: We develop a quantum description of the resonant interaction between electromagnetic (EM) and gravitational waves (GW). We first show that Lorentz invariance together with polarization selection rules forbids any photon-graviton mixing in free space. We demonstrate that confining the EM field within a cavity quantum electrodynamics (cavity-QED) environment breaks translational symmetry and isotropy, leading to non-vanishing mode coupling between EM and gravitational degrees of freedom. Within this framework, we identify multiple photon-graviton scattering channels, including photon up- and down-conversion and photon creation. In the semiclassical limit of the trilinear interaction where GW acts as a classical pump and the EM field is in a vacuum, spontaneous parametric photon amplification and two-mode squeezing occur. When the gravitational field is quantized, however, the back-action and energy exchange between photons and gravitons result in saturation of amplification, in contrast to exponential growth, and the loss of purity in the photonic subsystem. The characteristic timescale scales as $t_{\text{sp}}\sim (g\sqrt{n_g})^{-1}$, where $g$ and $n_g$ refer to the coupling strength and the mean graviton number, demonstrating collective enhancement of the interaction with the graviton occupation number. In the stimulated regime, where one EM mode is initially populated, the effective coupling is further enhanced, analogous to Dicke-type superradiant emission. This work introduces a cavity-based graviton transducer for probing quantum aspects of GWs.

</details>


### [95] [Digital Predistortion of Optical Fields for Fast and High-Fidelity Entangling Gates in Trapped-Ion Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27761)
*Jovan Markov,Yotam Shapira,Ayelet Hasson,Meir Alon,Avraham Gross,Nitzan Akerman,Roee Ozeri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种数字预失真技术，用于校正声光调制器的非线性幅度响应，从而提高离子阱量子处理器中多音纠缠门波形的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子门需要精确的经典控制信号，但模拟硬件（如声光调制器）的非线性失真会降低门性能。这些失真产生虚假的互调产物，限制了线性工作范围和衍射效率。

Method: 采用数字预失真技术：首先测量声光调制器的静态非线性幅度响应，然后通过前馈校正进行反转。该方法对设备和平合无关，适用于量子处理器经典控制链中的任何非线性元件。

Result: 1. 光谱分析显示主要互调音调抑制了3-5 dB；2. 在10^{-3}门误差阈值下，可用衍射效率大约翻倍；3. 直接双量子比特贝尔态保真度测量证实预失真持续改善纠缠门性能。

Conclusion: 数字预失真技术有效扩展了声光调制器的线性工作范围，抑制了互调失真，显著提高了离子阱量子处理器中纠缠门的性能。该方法具有通用性，可应用于量子处理器控制链中的各种非线性元件。

Abstract: High-fidelity quantum gates require precise classical control signals, yet the analog hardware delivering these signals introduces nonlinear distortions that degrade gate performance. We demonstrate digital predistortion of an acousto-optic modulator used to generate multi-tone entangling-gate waveforms in a trapped-ion processor based on $^{88}$Sr$^+$. By measuring and inverting the static nonlinear amplitude response of the modulator, we apply a feed-forward correction that extends its linear operating range and suppresses spurious intermodulation products. Spectral analysis of the gate beam shows 3--5 dB suppression of the dominant intermodulation tones, approximately doubling the usable diffraction efficiency at a $10^{-3}$ estimated gate-error threshold. Direct two-qubit Bell-state fidelity measurements confirm that predistortion consistently improves entangling-gate performance. The calibrate-and-invert methodology is device and platform agnostic, applicable to any nonlinear element in the classical control chain of a quantum processor.

</details>


### [96] [Benchmarking simulation of hybrid decoding scheme for parity-encoded spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27776)
*Yoshihiro Nambu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 比较SLHZ模型与minor embedding在奇偶编码自旋系统中的混合解码性能，发现SLHZ方案结合经典比特翻转解码后更高效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于片上超导技术的量子退火设备开发，研究适用于奇偶编码自旋系统的实用混合解码方案，比较两种实现全连接模拟的嵌入方案性能

Method: 采用经典基准模拟，比较SLHZ模型和minor embedding两种嵌入方案，结合基于经典比特翻转算法的后读出经典解码，评估寻找最优解的性能

Result: SLHZ方案本身效率显著低于ME方案，但与基于经典比特翻转算法的后读出经典解码结合后，整体性能比ME方案更高效

Conclusion: 对于奇偶编码自旋系统的混合解码，SLHZ嵌入方案结合经典后处理解码比传统minor embedding方案更具优势，为量子退火设备开发提供了有价值的参考

Abstract: This paper presents classical benchmark simulations of a practical hybrid decoding scheme for parity-encoded spin systems, which is well-suited to the development of quantum annealing devices based on on-chip superconducting technology. We compared the performance of finding the optimal solution using two embedding schemes for emulating all-to-all connectivity from local interactions: the SLHZ model, proposed by Sourlas, Lechner, Hauke, and Zoller, and the commonly used minor embedding (ME) scheme. We found that the SLHZ scheme is more efficient than the ME scheme when combined with postreadout classical decoding based on the classical bit-flipping algorithm, although the SLHZ scheme itself is substantially less efficient than the ME scheme.

</details>


### [97] [Q-Bridge: Code Translation for Quantum Machine Learning via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27836)
*Runjia Zeng,Priyabrata Senapati,Ruixiang Tang,Dongfang Liu,Qiang Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Q-Bridge：首个LLM引导的代码翻译框架，可将经典机器学习代码系统转换为可执行的量子机器学习变体，并构建了CML-2-QML数据集


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准化、高质量的数据集和稳健的翻译框架，限制了经典机器学习与量子机器学习之间的桥梁构建

Method: 采用自迭代管道将已验证的种子代码库扩展为大规模数据集CML-2-QML，包含可验证和不可验证的代码对；使用监督LoRA适应进行微调，实现可扩展且内存高效的训练

Result: 实证分析证实了直接CML-to-QML翻译的可行性，揭示了经典与量子范式之间的一致结构对齐；案例研究显示Q-Bridge既能保持确定性正确性，又能支持创造性架构探索

Conclusion: 建立了首个可复现的LLM驱动量子代码翻译框架和数据集，为可扩展量子AI开发奠定了基础

Abstract: Large language models have recently shown potential in bridging the gap between classical machine learning and quantum machine learning. However, the lack of standardized, high-quality datasets and robust translation frameworks limits progress in this domain. We introduce Q-Bridge, an LLM-guided code translation framework that systematically converts CML implementations into executable QML variants. Our approach builds on a self-involving pipeline that iteratively expands a verified seed codebase into a large-scale dataset, CML-2-QML, integrating verifiable and unverifiable code pairs. The Q-Bridge model is fine-tuned using supervised LoRA adaptation for scalable and memory-efficient training, achieving faithful and interpretable quantum code generation across diverse architectures. Empirical analysis confirms the feasibility of direct CML-to-QML translation and reveals consistent structural alignment between classical and quantum paradigms. Case studies further demonstrate that Q-Bridge can maintain deterministic correctness and also enable creative architectural exploration. This work establishes the first reproducible framework and dataset for LLM-driven quantum code translation, offering a foundation for scalable quantum AI development.

</details>


### [98] [Exponentially cheaper coherent phase estimation via uncontrolled unitaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27858)
*Mirko Amico*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种改进的相位反冲技术，通过利用受控酉算子的信息，用非受控酉算子替代受控酉算子，代价是引入受控态制备，从而在量子相位估计算法中实现指数级减少两量子比特门数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位反冲是量子相位估计等许多量子算法的基本原语。传统方法需要受控酉算子，这会引入大量两量子比特门。作者旨在减少量子相位估计中的门复杂度，特别是当目标本征态的制备过程已知时。

Method: 提出改进的相位反冲技术：利用受控酉算子的信息，用非受控酉算子替代受控酉算子，同时引入受控态制备。将此技术应用于量子相位估计算法，当本征态制备过程已知时，可以显著减少门复杂度。

Result: 证明了在相关极限下，对于m比特的相位估计，该方法能实现两量子比特门数量的指数级减少。提供了具体应用示例，展示了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 改进的相位反冲技术为量子相位估计提供了更高效的方法，特别适用于本征态制备过程已知的情况。该方法可推广到任何使用相位反冲现象且满足假设的算法中，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: Phase kickback is a fundamental primitive that is used in many quantum algorithms, such as quantum phase estimation. Here we observe that by using information about the controlled unitary, we can replace the controlled unitary with an uncontrolled one at the cost of introducing controlled state preparations. We then show how this modified phase kickback can be used as part of the quantum phase estimation algorithm when the goal is to estimate the phase of an eigenstate whose preparation procedure is known. We prove that this yields an exponential reduction in the number of two-qubit gates for an m-bit phase estimation in the relevant limit. Examples of applications are also presented. Naturally, this can be adapted to any algorithm that uses the phase kickback phenomenon and satisfies the assumptions.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Robust Control using Geometric Optimal Control Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27894)
*Francesca Albertini,Domenico D'Alessandro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于几何最优控制的量子鲁棒控制方法，通过最小化灵敏度函数和能量来设计鲁棒量子控制，针对单量子比特和双量子比特系统获得解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统对参数扰动敏感，需要开发鲁棒控制方法来应对实际模型与名义模型之间的偏差，确保量子操作在存在不确定性的情况下仍能可靠执行。

Method: 采用几何最优控制工具，定义灵敏度函数作为轨迹对未知小参数的泰勒展开系数，构建最优控制问题以最小化灵敏度大小和控制场能量的加权和，针对单量子比特退相哈密顿量获得椭圆积分形式的解析解。

Result: 在灵敏度权重极大时获得零灵敏度且最小化控制场能量的最优控制，得到特别简单且光滑的显式解，避免了其他方法中的不连续性；将结果扩展到双量子比特系统，证明串扰污染最小化问题可解耦为两个独立的单量子比特问题。

Conclusion: 几何最优控制方法为量子鲁棒控制提供了系统框架，能够获得解析解并处理灵敏度最小化问题，该方法可扩展到多量子比特系统，为量子计算中的鲁棒控制设计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we demonstrate an approach to quantum robust control based on the tools of geometric optimal control. The central objects of interest are the sensitivity functions defined as the coefficients in the Taylor expansion of the trajectory with respect to the (unknown, small) parameters which describe the deviation of the actual model from nominal one. In terms of these quantities, we formalize an optimal control problem where one searches for the optimal nominal trajectory which minimizes the size of the sensitivity while taking into account other aspects of the control design such as the energy of the control field.
  We consider in detail the case of a single qubit with a dephasing Hamiltonian term, and the optimal control problem of obtaining a state transfer by minimizing the weighted sum of the energy of the controlling field and the first order sensitivity. At the limit of a very large weight on the sensitivity, we obtain the optimal control which zeros the sensitivity and minimizes the control field energy. This problem has a rich mathematical structure which enables its solution in terms of elliptic integrals. For this problem, we obtain an explicit solution which is particularly simple and also smooth, avoiding discontinuities which are present in other approaches.
  We extend the results to the robust control of two quantum bits minimizing cross-talk contamination, as we show that such a problem decouples in two independent one qubit problems.

</details>


### [100] [High PDMR contrast in single NV centres and related photocurrent properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27911)
*Michael Petrov,Boo Carmans,Josef Soucek,Akhil Kuriakose,Ottavia Jedrkiewicz,Emilie Bourgeois,Milos Nesladek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了对氮空位中心光电磁共振检测机制的理解，报道了光电流产生和电荷循环的新见解，开发了定位光电流相关特征的技术，发现界面态作为放大器可显著增强PDMR对比度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展对氮空位中心光电磁共振检测机制的理解，这对于固态量子计算平台的发展尤为重要。需要深入理解光电流产生和电荷循环机制，以解释PDMR对比度达到50%及以上的现象。

Method: 开发了定位光电流相关特征的技术；在共聚焦装置中引入第二束激光驱动界面态；建立模型解释观察到的放大效应；研究单氮空位中心的光电流产生和电荷循环过程。

Result: 发现电子在氮空位中心产生后存储在界面陷阱能级；确定界面态可作为放大器，通过第二束激光驱动；证明控制这些界面态可显著增强PDMR对比度；建立的模型能一致解释观察到的放大效应，即使在不施加偏压的情况下。

Conclusion: 界面态在氮空位中心的光电磁共振检测中起到关键放大作用，通过控制这些界面态可显著增强PDMR对比度，这为固态量子计算平台的发展提供了重要技术手段和理论理解。

Abstract: This paper aims to extend the understanding of the mechanism of photo-electrical detection of magnetic resonance (PDMR) in nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centres. This technique is particularly important for development of solid-state quantum computing platforms. In particular, we report on the new insight in the photocurrent (PC) generation and charge cycling in the single NV centre, which is related to PDMR contrast reaching 50\% and above. We develop a technique to locate PC related features. We find that electrons generated at the NV centre are stored in interface trap levels and establish that the interface states serve as an amplifier that can be driven by introducing a second laser into our confocal setup. We show that controlling these interface states allows one to significantly enhance the PDMR contrast. We develop a model that consistently explains observed amplification effects even without the application of a bias voltage.

</details>


### [101] [Contextuality of quantum non-demolition measurement via state discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27917)
*Min Namkung,Ilhwan Kim,Hyang-Tag Lim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过理论分析揭示了量子非破坏性测量中的上下文性特征，这些特征不仅展示了无歧义态分辨的非经典性，还扩展到涉及后测量态的序列无歧义分辨和概率量子克隆，并在噪声场景中保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子非破坏性测量在量子技术中具有重要作用，但其操作结构可以被经典的非上下文模型复制。因此，识别哪些特征阻止了经典模型再现相应的量子测量至关重要，这有助于揭示量子系统的非经典性本质。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示量子非破坏性测量结构中的上下文性特征，并将分析扩展到涉及后测量态的序列无歧义分辨和概率量子克隆，同时考虑噪声场景以评估实际实现的潜力。

Result: 发现了量子非破坏性测量中固有的上下文性特征，这些特征不仅揭示了无歧义态分辨的非经典性，还能扩展到序列无歧义分辨和概率量子克隆。在噪声场景中这些特征仍然存在，表明其在实际实现中的潜在相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究结果扩展了在量子系统中观察非经典性的范围，最终有助于推动各种量子技术的发展，特别是在量子通信等应用领域。

Abstract: Quantum non-demolition measurements facilitate various quantum technologies, including quantum communication. Notably, their operational structure can be replicated by a classical model--referred to as a noncontextual model--making it crucial to identify which features prevents such models from reproducing the corresponding quantum measurements. In this work, we theoretically demonstrate contextual features inherent in the structure of quantum non-demolition measurements. These features not only reveal the nonclassicality of unambiguous state discrimination, but also extend to sequential unambiguous discrimination and probabilistic quantum cloning, both of which involve post-measurement states. Moreover, our analysis extends to noisy scenarios, highlighting its potential relevance for practical implementations. We believe that our results broaden the scope of observing nonclassicality in quantum systems and ultimately contribute to the advancement of various quantum technologies.

</details>


### [102] [Hybrid QPE-Ansatz Strategy for Reliable Excited-State Variational Quantum Deflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27978)
*Young Kyun Ahn,Young Min Rhee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出sfVQD方案：结合自旋z分量守恒的对称性保持变分波函数和浅层量子相位估计，用于NISQ时代激发态计算，通过辅助量子比特编码自旋信息并筛选自旋污染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NISQ设备上进行激发态计算时，自旋污染问题会降低计算精度。传统方法需要显式计算总自旋期望值⟨Ŝ²⟩，这在量子电路中成本高昂。需要一种NISQ兼容的方法来确保激发态计算具有物理意义。

Method: 1. 设计自旋z分量守恒的对称性保持变分波函数；2. 开发浅层量子相位估计(Sx)程序；3. 将两者结合到自旋筛选变分量子紧缩(sfVQD)方案中；4. 通过受控旋转exp(iθŜx)将自旋信息编码到小型辅助寄存器；5. 利用编码信息筛选自旋污染，避免显式计算⟨Ŝ²⟩。

Result: 在LiH和BeH₂分子不同几何构型上应用sfVQD，相比传统VQD，显著改善了单重态和三重态能级的分离。自旋筛选模块独立于变分波函数，可与其他基于变分量子本征求解器的激发态计算方案结合使用。

Conclusion: 辅助量子比特辅助的对称性筛选提供了一种模块化且NISQ兼容的途径，确保激发态计算具有物理意义。该方案可自然扩展到计算其他守恒量，为NISQ时代的量子化学计算提供了新方法。

Abstract: We introduce a spin $z$-component ($S_{z}$) conserving symmetry-preserving ansatz and a shallow quantum phase estimation (QPE) routine of spin $x$ ($S_x$), and combine them into a spin-filtering variational quantum deflation (sfVQD) scheme for noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) computing era excited state calculations. The scheme encodes the spin information into a small ancilla register through controlled rotations under $\mathrm{exp} (iθ\hat{S}_{x})$ with only modest circuit overhead. The encoded information is then utilized to suppress spin contamination by screening, avoiding costly explicit evaluation on the total spin $\langle\hat{S}^{2}\rangle$. Because the screening module operates independently of the variational ansatz, it can also be employed with other excited-state calculation schemes based on variational quantum eigensolvers. As a demonstration, we apply sfVQD to LiH and BeH$_2$ with varying geometries to show markedly improved separation of singlet and triplet manifolds over conventional VQD without QPE-derived screening. These results suggest that ancilla-assisted symmetry screening provides a modular and NISQ-compatible route to securing excited state calculations of physically meaningful properties. We discuss how our scheme may naturally be extended to computing other conserved quantities.

</details>


### [103] [Adiabatic dressing of quantum enhanced Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28076)
*Wen Ting Hsieh,Alev Orfi,Dries Sels*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过控制量子提议者的离域化程度，采用绝热修饰的淬火协议可以显著增强量子增强马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛算法在自旋玻璃模型中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强MCMC算法面临一个基本矛盾：量子动力学需要使系统离域化以提出超越经典更新的配置，但过度离域化会导致配置接受率降低，从而减慢收敛速度。需要解决这一矛盾以提升算法性能。

Method: 采用绝热修饰的淬火协议来控制量子提议者的离域化程度，在量子增强MCMC框架中，通过量子系统提出配置，然后由经典算法接受或拒绝。

Result: 在典型自旋玻璃模型中，通过控制离域化程度可以显著增强马尔可夫间隙，表明该方法能有效平衡离域化与接受率之间的矛盾。

Conclusion: 通过绝热修饰淬火协议控制量子提议者的离域化程度是提升量子增强MCMC算法性能的有效策略，为解决该算法的核心矛盾提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantum-enhanced Markov chain Monte Carlo, a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm in which configurations are proposed by a quantum proposer and accepted or rejected by a classical algorithm, has been introduced as a possible method for robust quantum speedup. Previous work has identified competing factors that limit the algorithm's performance: the quantum dynamics should delocalize the system across a range of classical states to propose configurations beyond the reach of simple classical updates, whereas excessive delocalization produces configurations unlikely to be accepted, slowing the chain's convergence. Here, we show that controlling the degree of delocalization by adiabatically dressing the quench protocol can significantly enhance the Markov gap in paradigmatic spin-glass models.

</details>


### [104] [Entanglement generation of arbitrary squeezed Fock states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28077)
*Qin-Ru Cheng,Ke-Xiong Yan,Yuan Qiu,Yi-Tong Shi,Yan Xia,Ye-Hong Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种高效稳健的协议，用于在超导量子比特和压缩腔之间生成纠缠。通过参数驱动和绝热通道，系统被引导至最大纠缠态，数值模拟证实了高保真度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子计算和量子计量学提供生成复杂非高斯纠缠态的实际途径，这些态对于容错量子计算和超越标准量子极限的量子计量具有重要意义。

Method: 1. 对耦合到量子比特的腔施加参数驱动，系统动力学在压缩参考系中精确描述为各向异性拉比模型。2. 利用高阶时间平均方法解析推导高阶三光子过程的共振条件和有效拉比频率。3. 通过绝热通道缓慢调节腔频率穿过共振，将系统引导至最大纠缠态（如压缩图像中的|g,3⟩和|e,0⟩态）。

Result: 数值模拟结果证实了所提协议的高保真度和鲁棒性。该方法能够生成最大纠缠态，例如在压缩图像中量子比特基态与三光子态|g,3⟩和激发态与真空态|e,0⟩之间的纠缠。

Conclusion: 该方法为生成复杂非高斯纠缠态提供了实用途径，这些态对于容错量子计算和超越标准量子极限的量子计量具有重要价值，为实现超越标准量子极限的量子计量和容错量子计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: We propose an efficient and robust protocol for the generation of entanglement between a superconducting qubit and a squeezed cavity. By applying a parametric drive to the cavity coupled to the qubit, the dynamical evolution of the system is precisely described by an anisotropic Rabi model within a squeezed reference frame. Utilizing high-order time-averaging methods, we analytically derive the resonance conditions and the effective Rabi frequency for the high-order three-photon process. By implementing an adiabatic passage, slowly tuning the cavity frequency across the resonance, the system is steered into a maximally entangled state, e.g., between the three-photon state $\ket{g,3}$ and the qubit excited state $\ket{e,0}$ in the squeezed picture. Numerical simulation results confirm the high fidelity and robustness of the proposed protocol. Our method provides a practical pathway for generating complex non-Gaussian entangled states, which are of significant value for fault-tolerant quantum computation and quantum metrology beyond the standard quantum limit.

</details>


### [105] [Device independent quantum key distribution with robust self-tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28085)
*Andreas Bluhm,Gereon Koßmann,René Schwonnek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出通过本地自测试将设备无关量子密钥分发协议提升为设备相关协议的方法，利用路由贝尔测试设置进行本地自测试，并建立严格的数学框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关量子密钥分发虽然减少了设备模型的详细假设，但引入了量子实验有效性的基本假设。本研究旨在通过本地自测试将DIQKD协议提升为设备相关协议，从而在保持安全性的同时减少假设

Method: 在密钥生成双方实验室中进行本地自测试，采用路由贝尔测试设置作为自测试手段，建立严格的数学框架证明底层优化问题可以转移到设备相关QKD设置中

Result: 开发了完整的数学框架，证明了优化问题的可转移性，并通过路由BB84协议的案例研究展示了相关技术

Conclusion: 成功建立了将设备无关QKD协议提升为设备相关协议的框架，通过本地自测试减少了假设要求，为实际量子密钥分发系统提供了更实用的安全模型

Abstract: Device-independent quantum key distribution (DIQKD) provides a model of quantum key distribution with minimal assumptions and highly abstract theoretical building blocks. Although DIQKD frees us from detailed discussions of specific device models and associated error parameters, it replaces them with fundamental assumptions about the validity of quantum experiments. In this work, we propose a way to lift a protocol based on DIQKD-style assumptions to a device-dependent QKD protocol by performing local self-tests in the laboratories of the two key-generating parties. In particular, we consider routed Bell-test setups as a means of self-testing the local parties in earnest and develop a rigorous mathematical framework showing that the underlying optimization problems can indeed be transferred to the device-dependent QKD setting. As an application, we illustrate many of the relevant techniques through the case study of a routed BB84 protocol.

</details>


### [106] [Q-DIVER: Integrated Quantum Transfer Learning and Differentiable Quantum Architecture Search with EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28122)
*Junghoon Justin Park,Yeonghyeon Park,Jiook Cha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Q-DIVER：结合预训练EEG编码器与可微分量子架构搜索的混合框架，在脑电信号分类任务上实现与经典方法相当性能，但参数量减少50倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子电路集成到深度学习流程中的启发式设计限制问题，探索量子迁移学习在高维生物信号处理中的参数效率优势

Method: 提出Q-DIVER混合框架：1) 使用大规模预训练EEG编码器DIVER-1提取特征；2) 采用可微分量子架构搜索自动发现任务最优的量子电路拓扑结构；3) 在端到端微调中优化量子分类器

Result: 在PhysioNet运动想象数据集上，量子分类器达到与经典多层感知机相当的预测性能（测试F1：63.49%），同时任务特定头部参数减少约50倍（2.10M vs. 105.02M）

Conclusion: 量子迁移学习是高维生物信号处理中一种参数高效的策略，可微分量子架构搜索能够自主发现任务最优的量子电路拓扑，为量子-经典混合深度学习提供了新范式

Abstract: Integrating quantum circuits into deep learning pipelines remains challenging due to heuristic design limitations. We propose Q-DIVER, a hybrid framework combining a large-scale pretrained EEG encoder (DIVER-1) with a differentiable quantum classifier. Unlike fixed-ansatz approaches, we employ Differentiable Quantum Architecture Search to autonomously discover task-optimal circuit topologies during end-to-end fine-tuning. On the PhysioNet Motor Imagery dataset, our quantum classifier achieves predictive performance comparable to classical multi-layer perceptrons (Test F1: 63.49\%) while using approximately \textbf{50$\times$ fewer task-specific head parameters} (2.10M vs. 105.02M). These results validate quantum transfer learning as a parameter-efficient strategy for high-dimensional biological signal processing.

</details>


### [107] [Entanglement of minimal dimension and a class of local state discrimination problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28284)
*Saronath Halder,Suchetana Goswami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了无法通过LOCC完美区分的二部正交纯态小集合，并证明最小维度的纠缠资源态可实现完美区分，且任何纯纠缠态都可用作资源进行非零概率的明确区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LOCC区分能力的局限性，探索纠缠资源在量子态区分中的关键作用，特别是在无法通过LOCC完美区分的态集合中，纠缠资源如何提供区分优势。

Method: 构建二部正交纯态的小集合，这些集合无法通过LOCC完美区分，但至少包含一个无法通过LOCC非零概率明确识别的纠缠态。然后证明使用最小维度的纠缠资源态可以实现完美LOCC区分。

Result: 1. 在所有二量子比特希尔伯特空间中存在这样的态集合；2. 最小维度纠缠资源态可实现完美LOCC区分；3. 任何纯纠缠态都可用作资源进行非零概率的明确区分；4. 可以降低集合的平均纠缠度或增加集合基数而不改变其性质。

Conclusion: 纠缠资源在量子态区分中具有普遍优势，即使对于包含非纠缠态的集合，纠缠资源也能提供区分能力。这一发现深化了对LOCC局限性和纠缠资源价值的理解。

Abstract: In this work, we construct small sets of bipartite orthogonal pure states that cannot be perfectly distinguished by local operations and classical communication (LOCC). We mention that not all the states within the constructed sets are necessarily entangled. However, such a set contains at least one entangled state which cannot be conclusively identified by LOCC (with nonzero probability). Then, we show that the states of any such set can be perfectly distinguished by LOCC using a minimal-dimensional entangled resource state. Clearly, here the entangled resource state provides an advantage irrespective of the dimension of the given set. Using this result, we also prove that any pure entangled state is useful as a resource to distinguish the states of any present set unambiguously with nonzero probability under LOCC. These sets exist in all two-qudit Hilbert spaces. Furthermore, it is possible to decrease the average entanglement content of such a set or to increase the cardinality of the set without changing certain properties of the same.

</details>


### [108] [Learning from imperfect quantum data via unsupervised domain adaptation with classical shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28294)
*Kosuke Ito,Akira Tanji,Hiroshi Yano,Yudai Suzuki,Naoki Yamamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于量子数据学习的无监督域适应框架，通过经典阴影表示量子态，在经典计算流程中处理量子测量数据，以解决实际场景中量子数据不完美和域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子机器学习应用中，通常无法获得目标域的干净、完全标记的量子数据，模型必须在与部署条件不同的数据上进行训练，这种不匹配需要超越先前工作假设的新方法。

Method: 采用无监督域适应框架，利用通过经典阴影获得的量子态经典表示，在量子态测量执行后，完全在经典计算流程中进行无监督域适应。

Result: 在量子物质相和纠缠分类任务上，在现实域偏移条件下进行数值评估。该方法在两项任务中都优于仅源域的非自适应基线和仅目标域的无监督学习方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了域适应在现实量子数据学习中的实际适用性，为处理不完美量子数据提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Learning from quantum data using classical machine learning models has emerged as a promising paradigm toward realizing quantum advantages. Despite extensive analyses on their performance, clean and fully labeled quantum data from the target domain are often unavailable in practical scenarios, forcing models to be trained on data collected under conditions that differ from those encountered at deployment. This mismatch highlights the need for new approaches beyond the common assumptions of prior work. In this work, we address this issue by employing an unsupervised domain adaptation framework for learning from imperfect quantum data. Specifically, by leveraging classical representations of quantum states obtained via classical shadows, we perform unsupervised domain adaptation entirely within a classical computational pipeline once measurements on the quantum states are executed. We numerically evaluate the framework on quantum phases of matter and entanglement classification tasks under realistic domain shifts. Across both tasks, our method outperforms source-only non-adaptive baselines and target-only unsupervised learning approaches, demonstrating the practical applicability of domain adaptation to realistic quantum data learning.

</details>


### [109] [Local robust shadows on a trapped ion computer -- a case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28307)
*Jadwiga Wilkens,Milena Guevara-Bertsch,Marwa Marso,Mederika Zangerl,Albert Frisch,Florian Girtler,Juris Ulmanis,Ingo Roth,Richard Kueng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在离子阱设备上实验演示了局部鲁棒阴影协议，通过交替校准和估计阶段以及泡利-X旋转来对称化错误率，成功缓解了因缩短测量脉冲时间而增加的测量错误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子计算中的测量错误问题，特别是在离子阱设备上，通过缩短测量脉冲时间来提高实验速度但会增加错误率，需要开发有效的错误缓解协议

Method: 采用局部鲁棒阴影协议，交替进行校准阶段和阴影估计阶段，并在两个阶段引入泡利-X旋转来对称化错误率；在离子阱量子计算机上通过人为缩短测量脉冲持续时间来测试协议

Result: 在三种示例量子态上进行了基准测试：局部Haar随机态、标准QAOA态和泡利相关编码QAOA态。在所有三种情况下，局部鲁棒阴影协议都成功缓解了因缩短测量脉冲持续时间而增加的错误率

Conclusion: 局部鲁棒阴影协议在离子阱设备上有效缓解了测量错误，特别是在需要快速实验但会引入更高错误率的场景中，为量子态表征提供了实用的错误缓解解决方案

Abstract: We experimentally demonstrate local robust shadows on a trapped-ion device, a protocol developed to counteract measurement errors. We alternate between a calibration stage and the shadow estimation stage and also introduce Pauli-X-twirling before measurements in both stages to symmetrize error rates. We then demonstrate the protocol on a trapped-ion quantum computer with artificially shortened measurement pulse duration. This yields faster experiments at the cost of increased error rates which are subsequently mitigated by the robust shadow protocol. We benchmark this approach on three exemplary quantum states: a local Haar random state, as well as standard and Pauli-correlation encoded QAOA states. In all three cases, the local robust shadow protocol succeeds at mitigating the increased error rates hailing from shorter measurement pulse durations.

</details>


### [110] [Compact Continuous-Variable Quantum Key Distribution System Employing Monolithically Integrated Silicon Photonic Transceiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28310)
*Denis Fatkhiev,João dos Reis Frazão,Alireza H. Derkani,Kadir Gümüş,Menno van den Hout,Aaron Albores-Mejia,Chigo Okonkwo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次展示基于单片硅光子双偏振收发器的CV-QKD系统，采用PS-64-QAM调制，在25公里标准单模光纤上实现1.9 Mbit/s的密钥率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索电子-光子集成技术在实用化量子密钥分发中的应用潜力，解决传统分立器件系统在尺寸、成本和稳定性方面的限制

Method: 设计并制造单片硅光子双偏振收发器，采用连续变量量子密钥分发架构，使用PS-64-QAM（概率整形64-QAM）调制方案

Result: 在25公里标准单模光纤上实现了1.9 Mbit/s的密钥率，这是首次基于单片硅光子集成收发器的CV-QKD系统演示

Conclusion: 该工作证明了电子-光子集成技术在实用化量子密钥分发中的巨大潜力，为实现紧凑、低成本、高性能的QKD系统提供了重要技术路径

Abstract: We demonstrate the first CV-QKD system featuring a custom-designed monolithic silicon photonic dual-polarisation transceiver. Leveraging PS-64-QAM, we achieved 1.9 Mbit/s secret key rate across 25 km of standard single-mode fibre, highlighting the potential of electronic-photonic integration for practical QKD.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Analyzing Formic Acid Using Classical and Quantum Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28343)
*Omer Gurevich,Tal Mor,Ido Ram*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出结合量子、量子启发和经典工具来模拟碳固定过程，特别引入基于相互无偏基的离散量子穷举搜索方法，分析二氧化碳、氢气和甲酸的最简单非催化过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 催化碳固定对减少碳足迹和研究生命起源具有重要意义。量子工具在分析分子电子结构方面有优势，但面临希尔伯特空间中贫瘠高原的障碍，需要开发新方法来克服这些限制

Method: 结合量子、量子启发和经典工具进行模拟，特别引入基于相互无偏基的离散量子穷举搜索方法，分析二氧化碳、氢气和甲酸的最简单非催化过程

Result: 提出了一个集成多种计算方法的框架，特别展示了离散量子穷举搜索在分析简单碳固定过程中的应用潜力

Conclusion: 通过融合量子、量子启发和经典工具，特别是离散量子穷举搜索方法，能够更好地模拟碳固定等相关过程，为解决量子计算中的贫瘠高原问题提供了新思路

Abstract: Catalytic carbon fixation to formic acid is important for studying the reduction of carbon footprint and the emergence of life. Can discrete quantum exhaustive search merged with other methods help reduce the carbon footprint? We suggest merging quantum, quantum inspired, and classical tools for a better simulation of various relevant processes. Quantum tools are often used for analyzing the electronic structure of molecules, sometimes because this problem is not scalable (in the number of orbitals) on classical computers while it is potentially approximately scalable on (future) quantum computers. It is potentially even solvable in the near future using variational quantum eigensolvers (VQE) yet a major obstacle to such analysis is the appearance of barren plateaus in the Hilbert space describing the problem.
  Here we make use of the basic (standard) tools while also including a novel one -- the discrete quantum exhaustive search, which relies on mutually unbiased bases, for analyzing the simplest non-catalytic process involving carbon dioxide, hydrogen and formic acid.

</details>


### [112] [Resource-efficient quantum approximate optimization algorithm via Bayesian optimization and maximum-probability evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28413)
*Siran Zhang,Shuming Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种资源高效的QAOA框架，使用最可能测量比特串的割值作为优化目标，结合贝叶斯优化和基于模式置信度与归一化割值方差的双准则自适应分配测量次数，在有限测量预算下实现与传统期望值目标相当的离散解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子近似优化算法（QAOA）的性能严重依赖于目标设计、参数搜索和测量次数分配。传统基于期望值的目标需要大量测量，在有限测量预算下效率低下。需要开发资源高效的QAOA框架，在减少总测量次数的同时保持解的质量。

Method: 1) 使用最可能测量比特串的割值作为优化目标，而非传统的期望值；2) 结合贝叶斯优化进行参数搜索；3) 采用基于模式置信度和归一化割值方差的双准则自适应分配测量次数。

Result: 在3-正则MaxCut问题（包括加权和非加权实例）上的数值实验表明，该方案在达到相同最终模式精度时，通常需要比传统期望值目标更少的总测量次数，同时获得相当的离散解质量。

Conclusion: 围绕最大概率比特串重新组织QAOA，为在有限测量预算下提高实际性能提供了有效途径。这种资源高效框架在保持解质量的同时显著减少了测量开销。

Abstract: The quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) is a leading variational approach to combinatorial optimization, but its practical performance depends strongly on objective design, parameter search, and shot allocation. We present a resource-efficient QAOA framework that uses the cut value of the most probable measured bitstring as the optimization objective, combines it with Bayesian optimization, and adaptively allocates shots using dual criteria based on mode confidence and normalized cut-value variance. Numerical experiments on 3-regular MaxCut show that, for both unweighted and weighted instances, the proposed scheme achieves discrete-solution quality comparable to that of the conventional expectation-based objective while typically requiring fewer total shots to reach the same final mode accuracy. These results indicate that reorganizing QAOA around the maximum-probability bitstring provides an effective route to improving practical performance under limited measurement budgets.

</details>


### [113] [Learning unified control of internal spin squeezing in atomic qudits for magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28421)
*C. Z. Cao,J. Z. Han,M. Xiong,M. Deng,L. Wang,X. Lv,M. Xue*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 使用强化学习控制非线性塞曼效应，将多能级原子中的噪声转化为自旋压缩资源，实现超越标准量子极限的磁测量灵敏度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多能级原子在低场区工作时，非线性塞曼效应既是产生自旋压缩态的资源，也是限制测量精度的因素。它会产生时间依赖的压缩轴和有效非线性作用，导致测量相关正交分量畸变，限制了可达到的计量增益。

Method: 采用物理信息强化学习方法，仅使用实验可访问的低阶自旋矩，在161Dy原子的f=21/2能级流形中训练智能体，识别统一的控制策略。该策略能快速制备强压缩内部态，并在持续的非线性塞曼演化中稳定超过4dB的固定轴自旋压缩。

Result: 学习到的协议实现了13.9 pT/√Hz的单原子磁灵敏度，对应约3dB超越标准量子极限的优势。包括态制备开销在内，该协议将非线性塞曼动力学从读出退化源转化为持续的计量资源。

Conclusion: 基于学习的控制为将多级量子传感器中不可避免的内在非线性动力学转化为可操作的计量优势提供了实用途径，建立了强化学习在量子增强原子磁力计中的应用价值。

Abstract: Generating and preserving metrologically useful quantum states is a central challenge in quantum-enhanced atomic magnetometry. In multilevel atoms operated in the low-field regime, the nonlinear Zeeman (NLZ) effect is both a resource and a limitation. It nonlinearly redistributes internal spin fluctuations to generate spin-squeezed states within a single atomic qudit, yet under fixed readout it distorts the measurement-relevant quadrature and limits the accessible metrological gain. This challenge is compounded by the time dependence of both the squeezing axis and the effective nonlinear action. Here we show that physics-informed reinforcement learning can transform NLZ dynamics from a source of readout degradation into a sustained metrological resource. Using only experimentally accessible low-order spin moments, a trained agent identifies, in the $f=21/2$ manifold of $^{161}\mathrm{Dy}$, a unified control policy that rapidly prepares strongly squeezed internal states and stabilizes more than $4\,\mathrm{dB}$ of fixed-axis spin squeezing under always-on NLZ evolution. Including state-preparation overhead, the learned protocol yields a single-atom magnetic sensitivity of $13.9\,\mathrm{pT}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$, corresponding to an advantage of approximately $3\,\mathrm{dB}$ beyond the standard quantum limit. Our results establish learning-based control as a practical route for converting unavoidable intrinsic nonlinear dynamics in multilevel quantum sensors into operational metrological advantage.

</details>


### [114] [Phase statistics of a single qubit emission as a direct probe of its coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28433)
*A. Sultanov,E. Mutsenik,L. Kaczmarek,M. Schmelz,G. Oelsner,R. Stolz,E. Il'ichev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过单次外差检测测量超导transmon量子比特在开放波导配置中的发射相位分布，证明量子叠加态编码在发射的相位统计中，并建立了开放量子系统退相干动力学与其辐射场统计特性之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单个原子发射光子的相位如何编码其初始量子态的相位信息，探索量子叠加态在辐射场相位统计中的表现，建立量子系统退相干过程与辐射场统计特性之间的直接关联。

Method: 采用单次外差检测技术，测量超导transmon量子比特在开放波导配置中发射光子的相位分布，跟踪其随时间演化。通过分析发射包络衰减和相位分布展宽来分别提取能量弛豫率和纯退相位速率。

Result: 量子叠加态的存在确实编码在发射的相位统计中，即使在较高噪声水平下仍可分辨。发射包络衰减随积分时间增加揭示了发射波包的能量弛豫率，而相位分布展宽则追踪了纯退相位过程。

Conclusion: 建立了开放量子系统退相干动力学与其辐射场统计特性之间的直接联系，相位统计可作为量子比特相干性的定量探针，为通过辐射场测量研究量子系统退相干提供了新方法。

Abstract: The emission of photon from an individual atom encodes the phase of its initialized quantum state. Using single-shot heterodyne detection, we measure the phase distribution of the emission from a superconducting transmon qubit in an open waveguide configuration and track its evolution over time. We demonstrate that the presence of a quantum superposition is encoded in the phase statistics of the emission and remains resolvable despite a high noise level. These phase statistics serve as a quantitative probe of the qubit coherence. The decay of the emission envelope with increasing integration time reveals the energy relaxation rate of the emitted wavepacket, while phase distribution broadening tracks pure dephasing processes. We thereby establish a direct link between the decoherence dynamics of an open quantum system and the statistical properties of its radiated field.

</details>


### [115] [Mixed-register Stabilizer Codes: A Coding-theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28459)
*Himanshu Dongre,Lane G. Gunderman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合寄存器量子设备中的信息保护，构建了编码理论最优的混合寄存器稳定子码，这些码的逻辑子空间不直接对应于任何组成局部维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合寄存器量子设备中的信息保护，这种设备包含不同局部结构的量子位置，能更准确地反映实际量子硬件的结构，同时减少需要保护的单个量子位置数量，并具有潜在的基础理论优势。

Method: 首先提供混合寄存器Pauli算子的一般结果，识别被禁止的稳定子编码信息形式。然后基于这些见解，从定义在互质局部维度上的码集合构建编码理论最优的混合寄存器稳定子码。

Result: 构建了混合寄存器稳定子码，这些码的逻辑子空间不直接对应于任何组成局部维度，实现了编码理论最优的混合寄存器量子码构造。

Conclusion: 混合寄存器量子设备中的信息保护具有实际硬件结构和理论优势，通过构建编码理论最优的混合寄存器稳定子码，为这类设备提供了有效的量子纠错方案。

Abstract: Protecting information in systems that have more than two basis states (qudits) not only offers a promising route for reducing the number of individual quantum locations that must be protected, while more accurately reflecting the structure of realistic quantum hardware, but also has some possibly enticing foundational strengths. While work in the past has largely focused on protecting information in quantum devices with locations that are some consistent local structure, this work considers coding-theoretic constraints on devices constructed from locations which may vary in their local structures -- these are mixed-register quantum devices. In this work we provide some general results for mixed-register Pauli operators, then identify some stabilizer encoded information forms that are forbidden. Building on these insights, we construct coding-theoretically optimal mixed-register stabilizer codes from sets of codes defined on coprime local-dimensions. The construction of such codes results in codes with logical subspaces that do not directly correspond to any of the constituent local-dimensions.

</details>


### [116] [$1/N^2$ Precision Interferometry with Collectively Enhanced Atomic Mirror](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28471)
*Yuan Liu,Ke-Mi Xu,Hong-Bo Sun,Linhan Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需制备纠缠态的量子计量新范式，利用集体增强量子镜（CEAM）实现超越海森堡极限的测量精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计量协议依赖制备脆弱的量子态来获取量子资源，这给扩展性和鲁棒性带来重大挑战。需要一种无需纠缠态制备的实用化量子计量方案

Method: 引入集体增强量子镜（CEAM）范式，即耦合到半无限波导的N个原子介观阵列。通过向波导注入单光子并从反射相位估计CEAM-边界距离，利用合作光学响应获得量子资源

Result: 获得了1/N²的精度标度，超越了海森堡极限。该方案对位置和耦合无序具有鲁棒性，无需纠缠态制备

Conclusion: CEAM范式为集成光子系统中的超灵敏量子计量提供了实用化途径，通过合作光学响应而非纠缠态制备获取量子资源，解决了传统方案的扩展性和鲁棒性挑战

Abstract: Quantum metrology exploits quantum resources to enhance measurement precision beyond the classical limit. Conventional protocols normally rely on the preparation of delicate quantum states to acquire these resources, posing a major challenge for scaling and robustness. Here we introduce a paradigm that circumvents this requirement with a collectively enhanced quantum mirror (CEAM), i.e., a mesoscopic array of $N$ atoms coupled to a semi-infinite waveguide. When injecting single photons into the waveguide and estimating the CEAM-boundary distance from the reflection phase, a $1/N^2$ precision scaling can be obtained, which surpasses the Heisenberg limit. In this protocol, the quantum resource stems from the cooperative optical response, requiring no entangled state preparation. Our scheme is robust against positional and coupling disorder, offering a practical route to ultra-sensitive quantum metrology in integrated photonic systems.

</details>


### [117] [Open-System Adiabatic Quantum Search under Dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28506)
*Afaf El Kalai,Peter J. Eder,Christian B. Mendl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文重新分析了开放系统中受退相干影响的绝热量子算法，特别针对绝热Grover搜索框架，推导了最优演化时间表、最小运行时间和可达保真度的闭式表达式，并识别了退相干的临界阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热量子算法需要在抑制非绝热跃迁和保持实际可行性之间取得平衡，而在开放系统中，这种权衡被退相干效应重新塑造。先前研究[1]表明，对于受退相Lindblad算符影响的哈密顿量，存在唯一的时间表能最大化目标态的保真度。本研究旨在将这一分析应用于绝热Grover搜索框架，探索退相干如何影响量子搜索协议的性能边界。

Method: 重新审视Avron等人的分析框架，将其应用于绝热Grover搜索问题。通过数学推导获得最优演化时间表、最小运行时间和可达保真度的闭式表达式。利用能量-时间不确定性原理论证，识别退相干的临界阈值。

Result: 获得了绝热Grover搜索在退相干影响下的最优演化时间表、最小运行时间和可达保真度的精确解析表达式。发现了一个临界退相干阈值，超过该阈值后噪声辅助加速被禁止，从而定义了基于退相干的绝热量子搜索协议的物理可实现边界。

Conclusion: 该研究为开放系统中受退相干影响的绝热量子算法提供了理论框架，特别针对Grover搜索问题给出了完整的解析解。识别出的临界退相干阈值定义了噪声辅助加速的物理极限，为实际量子计算中退相干效应的管理提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Adiabatic quantum algorithms must evolve slowly enough to suppress non-adiabatic transitions while remaining fast enough to be practical. In open systems, this trade-off is reshaped by decoherence. For Hamiltonians subject to dephasing Lindbladians, Avron et al. [1] showed that a unique timetable exists that maximizes the fidelity with a target state. This optimal schedule is characterized by a constant tunneling rate along the adiabatic path. In this work, we revisit their analysis and apply it to the adiabatic Grover search framework, obtaining closed-form expressions for the optimal evolution schedule, the minimum runtime, and the resulting achievable fidelity. Moreover, by invoking an energy-time uncertainty argument, we identify a critical dephasing threshold, beyond which further noise-assisted acceleration is prohibited, thereby defining the physically realizable boundaries for dephasing-based adiabatic quantum search protocols.

</details>


### [118] [Photon-triplets for quantum optics generated by a phase-matched third-order difference-frequency mixing in a KTiOPO4 bulk crystal pumped at 532 nm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28519)
*Gaspar Mougin-Trichon,Veronique Boutou,Corinne Felix,David Jegouso,Benoit Boulanger*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在KTP晶体中通过差频混频实现高效光子三重态生成，最大通量达11.6个/秒，实验数据与基于真空量子涨落的半经典模型吻合


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高效光子三重态生成方法，通过差频混频过程产生三光子态，为量子光学应用提供可靠的光子源

Method: 使用532nm和1491nm皮秒光束在II类相位匹配KTP晶体中进行差频混频，通过符合计数协议测量光子三重态通量随入射光束能量的变化

Result: 实现了最大11.6光子三重态/秒的通量，实验数据与基于真空量子涨落和经典非线性光学方程的半经典模型描述一致

Conclusion: 成功实现高效光子三重态生成，验证了基于真空量子涨落的半经典模型能够准确描述该非线性光学过程

Abstract: We report implementation and modelling of an efficient photon-triplets generation experiment based on a difference-frequency-mixing of two picosecond beams at 532 nm and 1491 nm in a type II phase-matched KTP crystal. The photon-triplets flux was measured as a function of the energy of the two incident beams using a coincidence protocol. A maximal flux of 11.6 photon-triplets per second was achieved. These experimental data were satisfactorily described by a semiclassical model based on the quantum fluctuations of vacuum and the classical equations of nonlinear optics.

</details>


### [119] [SesQ: A Surface Electrostatic Simulator for Precise Energy Participation Ratio Simulation in Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28524)
*Ziang Wang,Shuyuan Guan,Feng Wu,Xiaohang Zhang,Qiong Li,Jianxin Chen,Xin Wan,Tian Xia,Hui-Hai Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SesQ：一种基于表面积分方程的超导量子比特仿真器，通过2D表面离散化、多层格林函数和边界网格细化，高效精确计算能量参与比（EPR），相比传统FEM加速约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特设计需要准确评估介质损耗，传统有限元方法（FEM）在处理纳米级材料界面奇异电场时面临计算成本高、内存需求大的多尺度挑战。

Method: 提出SesQ表面积分方程仿真器：采用2D表面离散化、推导半解析多层格林函数、实施专用非共形边界网格细化方案，避免未知数爆炸性增长。

Result: 验证显示SesQ比商业FEM工具电容提取加速约100倍；EPR计算精度更高；实际transmon量子比特仿真表明FEM方法显著低估EPR；高效性支持快速布局优化。

Conclusion: SesQ通过表面积分方程方法有效解决了超导量子比特EPR计算的多尺度挑战，为低损耗超导量子电路的自动化设计提供了强大工具。

Abstract: An accurate and efficient numerical electromagnetic model for superconducting qubits is essential for characterizing and minimizing design-dependent dielectric losses. The energy participation ratio (EPR) is the commonly adopted metric used to evaluate these losses, but its calculation presents a severe multiscale computational challenge. Conventional finite element method (FEM) requires 3D volumetric meshing, leading to prohibitive computational costs and memory requirements when attempting to capture singular electric fields at nanometer-thin material interfaces. To address this bottleneck, we propose SesQ, a surface integral equation simulator tailored for the precise simulation of the EPR. By applying discretization on 2D surfaces, deriving a semi-analytical multilayer Green's function, and employing a dedicated non-conformal boundary mesh refinement scheme, SesQ accurately resolves singular edge fields without an explosive growth in the number of unknowns. Validations with analytically solvable models demonstrate that SesQ accelerates capacitance extraction by roughly two orders of magnitude compared to commercial FEM tools. While achieving comparable accuracy for capacitance extraction, SesQ delivers superior precision for EPR calculation. Simulations of practical transmon qubits further reveal that FEM approaches tend to significantly underestimate the EPR. Finally, the high efficiency of SesQ enables rapid iteration in the layout optimization, as demonstrated by minimizing the EPR of the qubit pattern, establishing the simulator as a powerful tool for the automated design of low-loss superconducting quantum circuits.

</details>


### [120] [Quantized Dissipation from the Inverse-Square Anomaly in a Non-Hermitian Klein-Gordon Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28525)
*Mansour Haghighat,Ali Nouri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构建了一个可精确求解的相对论模型，将反常反平方相互作用嵌入到非厄米Klein-Gordon场论中，通过纯虚数、尺度不变的标量势实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究尺度反常、边界条件诱导的非厄米性以及相对论开放量子系统中的量子化耗散，为这些现象提供最小解析框架。

Method: 构造了包含纯虚数尺度不变标量势的非厄米Klein-Gordon场论，将定态场方程简化为具有二次谱参数的反平方薛定谔型问题。在奇点处施加严格出射边界条件（解释为不可逆吸收），选择唯一的物理实现。

Result: 将向中心跌落的不稳定性转化为离散的、对数周期性的复能量谱。衰变率呈现普适的几何间距，仅由反常标度指数决定，对微观短距离正则化不敏感。结构定义了一个控制耗散动力学的新兴运动学能量尺度。

Conclusion: 该模型为研究尺度反常、边界条件诱导的非厄米性和相对论开放量子系统中的量子化耗散提供了一个最小解析框架，揭示了耗散动力学的普适几何结构。

Abstract: We construct an exactly solvable relativistic model that embeds the anomalous inverse-square interaction into a non-Hermitian Klein-Gordon field theory through a purely imaginary, scale-invariant scalar potential. The stationary field equation reduces to an inverse-square Schrodinger-type problem with a quadratic spectral parameter. Imposing a strictly outgoing boundary condition at the singularity-interpreted as irreversible absorption-selects a unique physical realization and converts the fall-to-the-center instability into a discrete, log-periodic spectrum of complex energies. The resulting decay rates exhibit universal geometric spacing, determined solely by the anomalous scaling exponent and insensitive to microscopic short-distance regularization. This structure defines an emergent kinematic energy scale that controls dissipative dynamics and provides a minimal analytic framework for studying scale anomaly, boundary-condition-induced non-Hermiticity, and quantized dissipation in relativistic open quantum systems.

</details>


### [121] [Tunable Nonlocal ZZ Interaction for Remote Controlled-Z Gates Between Distributed Fixed-Frequency Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28526)
*Benzheng Yuan,Chaojie Zhang,Haoran He,Yangyang Fei,Chuanbing Han,Shuya Wang,Huihui Sun,Qing Mu,Bo Zhao,Fudong Liu,Weilong Wang,Zheng Shan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双transmon耦合器的分布式超导量子计算架构，通过同步控制实现模块间高保真度CZ门操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单片超导量子处理器面临布线密度、串扰和制造良率的限制，模块化平台虽然提供可扩展替代方案，但实现远距离模块间高保真度纠缠门仍是核心挑战，特别是对于高相干性的固定频率量子比特

Method: 采用一对双transmon耦合器(DTCs)的分布式硬件架构，通过同步控制位于宏观电缆两端的两个DTC，强烈抑制残余静态模块间耦合，同时按需激活非局域交叉克尔相互作用

Result: 该机制可实现超过10^6的开/关比，通过包含实际硬件参数的系统级数值模拟，证明可在25厘米同轴电缆连接的独立封装固定频率transmon之间实现保真度超过99.99%的远程受控-Z(CZ)门

Conclusion: 这些结果为高性能分布式超导处理器建立了一条高度可行且硬件高效的路径

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires large-scale superconducting processors, yet monolithic architectures face increasing constraints from wiring density, crosstalk, and fabrication yield. Modular superconducting platforms offer a scalable alternative, but achieving high-fidelity entangling gates between distant modules remains a central challenge, particularly for highly coherent fixed-frequency qubits. Here, we propose a distributed hardware architecture designed to overcome this bottleneck by employing a pair of double-transmon couplers (DTCs). By synchronously controlling the two DTCs stationed at opposite ends of a macroscopic cable, our scheme strongly suppresses residual static inter-module coupling while enabling on-demand activation of a non-local cross-Kerr interaction with an on/off ratio exceeding $10^6$. Through comprehensive system-level numerical simulations incorporating realistic hardware parameters, we demonstrate that this mechanism can realize a remote controlled-Z (CZ) gate with a fidelity over 99.99\% between fixed-frequency transmons housed in separate packages interconnected by a 25 cm coaxial cable. These results establish a highly viable, hardware-efficient route toward high-performance distributed superconducting processors.

</details>


### [122] [The Power of Power-of-SWAP: Postselected Quantum Computation with the Exchange Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28527)
*Jędrzej Burkat,Sergii Strelchuk,Michał Studziński*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: XQP电路是仅使用计算基SPAM和各向同性海森堡交换相互作用的量子计算模型，位于BPP和BQP之间，其高效模拟会导致多项式层级塌陷，为近期硬件上的量子优势演示提供了有前景的平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一种适合近期量子硬件的子通用量子计算模型，该模型仅使用计算基状态制备和测量以及各向同性海森堡交换相互作用，探索其在量子计算优势演示中的潜力。

Method: 引入XQP电路模型，分析其计算能力与经典模拟难度，研究仅由√SWAP门组成的受限电路族的特性，建立与对称群Gelfand-Tsetlin基、六顶点模型和Potts模型配分函数的联系。

Result: XQP位于BPP和BQP之间，其乘法误差高效模拟会导致多项式层级塌陷至第三层；仅由√SWAP门组成的受限电路族对乘法误差模拟仍然困难；这些电路是半通用的，生成SU(2)不变酉算符的t-设计，并在XQP内最大化纠缠能力。

Conclusion: XQP电路天然适合近期量子硬件，为实验演示量子计算优势提供了有前景的平台，其输出概率与统计物理模型配分函数的结构联系为理解其计算能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce Exchange Quantum Polynomial Time (XQP) circuits, which comprise quantum computation using only computational basis SPAM and the isotropic Heisenberg exchange interaction. Structurally, this sub-universal model captures decoherence-free subspace computation without access to singlet states. We show that XQP occupies an intermediate position between BPP and BQP, as its efficient multiplicative-error simulation would collapse the polynomial hierarchy to its third level. We further provide evidence that additive-error simulation of XQP would enable efficient additive-error simulation of arbitrary BQP computations. Remarkably, the restricted family of XQP circuits consisting solely of $\sqrt{\mathrm{SWAP}}$ gates remains hard to simulate to multiplicative error. We additionally prove that circuits generated by $\sqrt{\mathrm{SWAP}}$ gates are semi-universal, generate $t$-designs for the uniform distribution over $SU(2)$-invariant unitaries, and maximise the entangling power within XQP. Finally, we derive structural results linking computational basis states in XQP to the Gelfand-Tsetlin basis of the symmetric group, and expressing XQP output probabilities as partition functions of the six-vertex and Potts models. Our findings indicate that XQP circuits are naturally suited to near-term hardware and provide a promising platform for experimental demonstrations of quantum computational advantage.

</details>


### [123] [Average Equilibration Time for Gaussian Unitary Ensemble Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28587)
*Emanuel Schwarzhans,Alessandro Candeloro,Felix C. Binder,Maximilian P. E. Lock,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究分析了随机矩阵理论（GUE）中量子系统的平衡时间，发现GUE预测的平衡时间随系统尺寸减小并在热力学极限下消失，表明真实混沌多体系统的平衡时间由GUE未捕获的物理特征主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解封闭量子系统的平衡时间对于描述其向平衡态的演化至关重要。混沌多体系统是这一研究中的典型系统，它们预期会按照本征态热化假说热化，并表现出可由随机矩阵理论（RMT）良好描述的光谱特性。然而，尽管RMT成功捕捉了光谱相关性，但其为平衡时间尺度提供定量预测的能力在很大程度上尚未被探索。

Method: 使用高斯酉系综（GUE）框架研究平衡化过程，将平衡视为退相干过程。推导了GUE平均平衡时间的解析表达式，并通过数值模拟验证该表达式与GUE真实平均平衡时间的吻合程度。

Result: 推导出的解析表达式近似描述了GUE的平均平衡时间，该时间与初始状态和可观测量选择无关（这是GUE旋转不变性的结果）。数值模拟证实了该解析表达式，并显示平衡时间随系统尺寸减小，在热力学极限下消失。

Conclusion: GUE预测的平衡时间在热力学极限下消失这一非物理结果表明，真实混沌多体系统的实际平衡时间尺度必须由随机矩阵系综（特别是GUE）未捕获的物理特征主导。

Abstract: Understanding equilibration times in closed quantum systems is essential for characterising their approach to equilibrium. Chaotic many-body systems are paradigmatic in this context: they are expected to thermalise according to the eigenstate thermalisation hypothesis and exhibit spectral properties well described by random matrix theory (RMT). While RMT successfully captures spectral correlations, its ability to provide quantitative predictions for equilibration timescales has remained largely unexplored. Here, we study equilibration within RMT using the framework of equilibration as dephasing, focusing on closed systems whose Hamiltonians are drawn from the Gaussian unitary ensemble (GUE). We derive an analytical expression that approximates the average equilibration time of the GUE and show that it is independent of both the initial state and the choice of observable, a consequence of the rotational invariance of the GUE. Numerical simulations confirm our analytical expression and demonstrate that our approximation is in close agreement with the true average equilibration time of the GUE. We find that the equilibration time decreases with system size and vanishes in the thermodynamic limit. This unphysical result indicates that the true equilibration timescale of realistic chaotic many-body systems must be dominated by physical features not captured by random matrix ensembles -- the GUE in particular.

</details>


### [124] [Lindbladian Simulation with Commutator Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28602)
*Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou,Xiaoyang Wang,Yi-Cong Zheng,Shengyu Zhang,Tongyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导了Lindbladian模拟的基于对易子的Trotter误差界，对局域相互作用系统实现了O(√N)的步数缩放，并通过Richardson外推法获得多对数精度，同时仅需O(1)辅助比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然哈密顿量模拟中的Trotter误差已建立在对易子关系上，但Lindbladian动力学的类似关系仍不清楚。需要为Lindbladian模拟建立基于对易子的误差界，以改进开放量子系统的模拟方法。

Method: 推导Lindbladian模拟的基于对易子的Trotter误差界；应用Richardson外推法估计可观测量平均值；开发绕过收敛问题的Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff展开截断界；对局域Lindbladians进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 对N个位点的局域相互作用系统，获得了O(√N)的Trotter步数缩放；通过外推法实现多对数精度；Trotter方法在系统尺寸缩放上优于先前技术，仅需O(1)辅助比特；数值模拟验证了预测的系统尺寸和精度缩放。

Conclusion: 该工作为Lindbladian模拟建立了基于对易子的Trotter误差理论框架，证明了Trotter方法在开放量子系统模拟中的优越性，为高效模拟提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Trotter decomposition provides a simple approach to simulating open quantum systems by decomposing the Lindbladian into a sum of individual terms. While it is established that Trotter errors in Hamiltonian simulation depend on nested commutators of the summands, such a relationship remains poorly understood for Lindbladian dynamics. In this Letter, we derive commutator-based Trotter error bounds for Lindbladian simulation, yielding an $O(\sqrt{N})$ scaling in the number of Trotter steps for locally interacting systems on $N$ sites. When estimating observable averages, we apply Richardson extrapolation to achieve polylogarithmic precision while maintaining the commutator scaling. To bound the extrapolation remainder, we develop a general truncation bound for the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff expansion that bypasses common convergence issues in physically relevant systems. For local Lindbladians, our results demonstrate that the Trotter-based methods outperform prior simulation techniques in system-size scaling while requiring only $O(1)$ ancillas. Numerical simulations further validate the predicted system-size and precision scaling.

</details>


### [125] [First-Click Time Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28623)
*Mafalda Pinto Couto,Lorenzo Maccone,Lorenzo Catani,Simone Roncallo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在Page-Wootters形式下构建了量子首次到达时间分布，通过引入记忆机制记录有限时间分辨率下的连续检测结果，发现条件化于未检测到会重新分配概率到更早到达时间，产生更窄更尖锐的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子到达时间问题存在两种不同视角：一种是粒子在给定时间被检测到的概率（无论之前是否被检测过），另一种是粒子首次被检测到的概率。本研究旨在分析后者，即构建条件化于粒子在更早时间未被检测到的首次到达时间分布。

Method: 采用Page和Wootters形式体系，将时间作为量子可观测量处理。引入记忆机制来记录由探测器有限时间分辨率分隔的连续检测尝试结果。将该框架应用于单个高斯波包和两个重叠波包的叠加态。

Result: 条件化于未检测到会将概率重新分配到更早的到达时间，与标准的无条件情况相比产生更窄更尖锐的分布。这种效应在量子干涉存在时仍然持续，但更粗糙的时间分辨率会使分布变宽并使其向更晚时间偏移。

Conclusion: 通过Page-Wootters形式体系和记忆机制，成功构建了量子首次到达时间分布（第一点击分布）。条件化于非检测显著改变了到达时间分布的特征，使其更早、更尖锐，这对量子测量理论和时间相关量子现象的理解具有重要意义。

Abstract: There are two distinct perspectives on the quantum time-of-arrival: one can ask for the probability that a particle is found at the detector at a given time, regardless of whether it was previously detected, or for the probability that the particle is detected there for the first time. In this work, we analyze the latter by constructing the time-of-arrival distribution conditioned on the particle not having been detected at earlier times -- the first-click distribution. We work within the Page and Wootters formalism, where time is treated as a quantum observable, and introduce a memory mechanism that records the outcomes of successive detection attempts separated by the detector's finite time resolution. We apply this framework to a single Gaussian wave packet and to a superposition of two overlapping wave packets. We find that conditioning on non-detection redistributes probability toward earlier arrival times, producing narrower and sharper distributions compared with the standard unconditioned case. This effect persists in the presence of quantum interference, though coarser time resolutions broaden the distribution and shift it toward later times.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Riemannian Hamiltonian Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28624)
*Yoshihiko Abe,Ryo Nagai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子黎曼哈密顿下降算法（QRHD），将量子哈密顿下降（QHD）扩展到黎曼流形上的连续优化问题，通过位置依赖的度量引入几何结构，并分析了量子修正、收敛时间界限和量子电路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子优化算法扩展到具有几何结构的参数空间，利用黎曼流形的几何信息改进优化性能，解决经典优化算法在复杂流形上的局限性。

Method: 在算符和路径积分形式下构建QRHD，在哈密顿量中引入位置依赖的度量项，推导量子运动方程，分析量子修正项，建立半经典方程并估计收敛时间下界，设计基于时变哈密顿模拟的量子电路实现。

Result: 量子修正项出现在作用量积分中，但在后期被时变耗散因子抑制；最优点附近的收敛由经典势能控制，量子效应主要影响早期动力学；通过数值实验验证了QRHD作为量子优化算法的有效性，并估算了查询复杂度。

Conclusion: QRHD成功将量子优化扩展到黎曼流形，量子效应在早期动力学中起重要作用，后期收敛行为由经典势能主导，为复杂几何结构下的量子优化提供了理论框架和实现方案。

Abstract: We propose Quantum Riemannian Hamiltonian Descent (QRHD), a quantum algorithm for continuous optimization on Riemannian manifolds that extends Quantum Hamiltonian Descent (QHD) by incorporating geometric structure of the parameter space via a position-dependent metric in the kinetic term. We formulate QRHD at both operator and path integral formalisms and derive the corresponding quantum equations of motion, showing that quantum corrections appear in the action integral but they are suppressed at late times by the time-dependent dissipation factor. This implies that convergence near optimal points is controlled by the classical potential while quantum effects influence early-time dynamics. By analyzing the semiclassical equation, we estimate a lower bound on the convergence time and numerically demonstrate whether QRHD work as a quantum optimization algorithm in some examples. A quantum circuit implementation based on time-dependent Hamiltonian simulation is also discussed and the query complexity is estimated.

</details>


### [127] [Shor's algorithm is possible with as few as 10,000 reconfigurable atomic qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28627)
*Madelyn Cain,Qian Xu,Robbie King,Lewis R. B. Picard,Harry Levine,Manuel Endres,John Preskill,Hsin-Yuan Huang,Dolev Bluvstein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过优化量子纠错码、逻辑指令集和电路设计，将实现Shor算法所需的物理量子比特数从数百万减少到仅10,000个可重构原子量子比特，使密码学相关规模的量子计算成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机在密码学相关任务（如整数分解和离散对数）上具有超越经典计算机的潜力，但现有资源估计需要数百万物理量子比特，量子纠错的高开销成为主要障碍。需要探索更高效的实现方案。

Method: 利用高码率量子纠错码、高效逻辑指令集和电路设计的进展，结合中性原子量子比特架构，通过可重构原子量子比特系统优化Shor算法的实现。分析不同物理量子比特数量对运行时间的影响。

Result: 仅需10,000个可重构原子量子比特即可执行密码学相关规模的Shor算法。26,000个物理量子比特系统可在几天内完成P-256椭圆曲线离散对数计算，RSA-2048整数分解的运行时间则长1-2个数量级。中性原子实验已展示低于纠错阈值的通用容错操作和6,000+量子比特阵列。

Conclusion: 尽管仍存在工程挑战，但理论分析表明，适当设计的中性原子架构能够支持密码学相关规模的量子计算。这些结果凸显了中性原子在容错量子计算中的潜力，具有广泛的科学和技术应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum computers have the potential to perform computational tasks beyond the reach of classical machines. A prominent example is Shor's algorithm for integer factorization and discrete logarithms, which is of both fundamental importance and practical relevance to cryptography. However, due to the high overhead of quantum error correction, optimized resource estimates for cryptographically relevant instances of Shor's algorithm require millions of physical qubits. Here, by leveraging advances in high-rate quantum error-correcting codes, efficient logical instruction sets, and circuit design, we show that Shor's algorithm can be executed at cryptographically relevant scales with as few as 10,000 reconfigurable atomic qubits. Increasing the number of physical qubits improves time efficiency by enabling greater parallelism; under plausible assumptions, the runtime for discrete logarithms on the P-256 elliptic curve could be just a few days for a system with 26,000 physical qubits, while the runtime for factoring RSA-2048 integers is one to two orders of magnitude longer. Recent neutral-atom experiments have demonstrated universal fault-tolerant operations below the error-correction threshold, computation on arrays of hundreds of qubits, and trapping arrays with more than 6,000 highly coherent qubits. Although substantial engineering challenges remain, our theoretical analysis indicates that an appropriately designed neutral-atom architecture could support quantum computation at cryptographically relevant scales. More broadly, these results highlight the capability of neutral atoms for fault-tolerant quantum computing with wide-ranging scientific and technological applications.

</details>


### [128] [Qubit-efficient embedding of parity-encoded Hamiltonians in quantum annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28667)
*Ryoji Miyazaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对Zephyr连接量子退火器的量子比特高效嵌入方案，用于实现基于奇偶编码的SLHZ量子退火方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLHZ奇偶编码量子退火方案具有优势但尚未在大规模量子退火器上实现，需要高效的嵌入方法将奇偶编码模型映射到现有硬件图结构中

Method: 提出针对Zephyr连接量子退火器的量子比特高效嵌入方案，通过显式构造方法将中间哈密顿量的相互作用图嵌入Zephyr图，每个自旋映射到两量子比特链，使用系统化的链分配规则

Result: 嵌入方案验证了链间连接性，每个自旋仅需3个量子比特，比已知的Pegasus图嵌入方案更少，且具有实际灵活性，辅助自旋链可简化为单个物理量子比特

Conclusion: 该嵌入方案为在现有量子退火器上实现SLHZ奇偶编码量子退火提供了可行的技术路径，具有量子比特效率和实际部署优势

Abstract: The Sourlas-Lechner-Hauke-Zoller (SLHZ) scheme for quantum annealing uses the parity to encode logical variables and has several advantages, but it has not been implemented for large-scale quantum annealers. If the SLHZ-based approach can be implemented on currently available quantum annealers, we can evaluate its performance. An efficient method to embed the parity-encoded model into the hardware graphs of available quantum annealers is one of the key elements for this approach. We propose a qubit-efficient embedding scheme for parity-encoded Hamiltonians on quantum annealers with the Zephyr connectivity. We give an explicit constructive embedding of the interaction graph of an intermediate Hamiltonian, which contains only one- and two-body interactions, into the Zephyr graph. Our embedding maps each spin to a two-qubit chain using systematic chain-assignment rules. Its validity is verified via the resulting chain-to-chain connectivity. Our embedding also offers practical flexibility. Chains assigned to ancillary spins allow reduction to a single physical qubit, leading to options to avoid inactive qubits. The number of required qubits per spin in the parity Hamiltonian is three, which is fewer than that for a known embedding scheme for the Pegasus graph.

</details>


### [129] [Efficient and Practical Black-Box Verification of Quantum Metric Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28687)
*Ahmed Shokry,Movahhed Sadeghi,Mahmut Kandemir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种实用的黑盒验证协议，用于审计量子度量学习模型的性能，无需了解模型实现细节即可验证不同数据组之间的角度分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度量学习将经典数据映射到量子希尔伯特空间以实现类间最大分离，但在当前NISQ硬件上，映射过程本身容易出错且可能根本错误。验证量子嵌入模型是否成功实现其承诺的分离对于确保正确性和可靠性至关重要。

Method: 提出黑盒验证协议，包含两个参与方：强大的不可信证明者（声称拥有参数化酉电路，能以保证的角度分离嵌入不同组数据）和能力有限的验证者（仅能执行基本测量）。验证算法克服两个关键挑战：1) 验证者不了解证明者的实现细节；2) 量子测量的破坏性阻碍直接估计分离角度。算法使验证者能准确估计不同组之间的真实分离角度。

Result: 实现了所提出的协议并部署用于验证QAOAEmbedding模型。理论和实际实现的结果表明，该方案能有效评估嵌入质量，并在对抗性设置中保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的黑盒验证协议为量子度量学习模型提供了一种实用的性能审计方法，无需了解模型实现细节即可验证数据组之间的角度分离，增强了量子机器学习系统的可靠性和可信度。

Abstract: Quantum metric learning enhances machine learning by mapping classical data to a quantum Hilbert space with maximal separation between classes. However, on current NISQ hardware, this mapping process itself is prone to errors and could be fundamentally incorrect. Verifying that a quantum embedding model successfully achieves its promised separation is essential to ensure the correctness and reliability. In this paper, we propose a practical black-box verification protocol to audit the performance of quantum metric learning models. We define a setting with two parties: a powerful but untrusted prover, who claims to have a parameterized unitary circuit that embeds classical data from different groups with a guaranteed angular separation, and a limited verifier, whose quantum capabilities are restricted to performing only basic measurements. The verifier has no knowledge of the implementation of the prover, including the structure of the model, its parameters, or the details of the prover measurement setup. To verify the separation between different data groups, the proposed algorithm must overcome two key challenges. First, the verifier is ignorant of the prover's implementation details, such as the optimization cost function and measurement setup. Consequently, the verifier lacks any prior information about the expected quantum embedding states for each group. Second, the destructive nature of quantum measurements prevents direct estimation of the separation angles. Our algorithm successfully overcomes these challenges, enabling the verifier to accurately estimate the true separation angles between the different groups. We implemented the proposed protocol and deployed it to verify the QAOAEmbedding models. The results from both theoretical analysis and practical implementation show that our proposal effectively assesses embedding quality and remains robust in adversarial settings.

</details>


### [130] [Modeling Quantum Optomechanical STIRAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28692)
*Ian Hedgepeth,Youqiu Zhan,Vitaly Fedoseev,Dirk Bouwmeester*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子光机械STIRAP用于两个机械模式与一个光学模式耦合的系统，可在无损耗情况下从单声子Fock态生成机械Bell态，从相干态生成乘积态，相对相位由初始态的声子数宇称决定。数值研究表明通过分数STIRAP可在现有低温冷却技术下实现高保真纠缠，并提出了基于时间反转分数STIRAP的干涉协议来量化纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子光机械系统中的STIRAP技术，旨在实现机械模式之间的高保真纠缠生成和量化，探索在存在耗散的实际系统中实现量子态操控的可能性。

Method: 采用分数STIRAP（f-STIRAP）技术，分析两个机械模式与一个光学模式耦合的系统。首先在无损耗情况下进行解析分析，然后数值研究耗散效应，最后提出基于时间反转分数STIRAP的干涉协议。

Result: 解析表明：无损耗系统中，分数STIRAP可从单声子Fock态生成机械Bell态，从相干态生成乘积态；相对相位由初始态声子数宇称决定。数值分析显示：利用现有低温冷却和机械器件，分数STIRAP可实现高保真纠缠。提出的干涉协议可量化机械模式间的纠缠。

Conclusion: 量子光机械STIRAP是生成和量化机械模式纠缠的有效方法，在现有实验条件下可实现高保真操作，为机械量子系统的量子信息处理提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Quantum optomechanical STIRAP (Stimulated Raman Adiabatic Passage) is investigated for a system of two mechanical modes coupled to an optical mode. We show analytically that in a system without loss, fractional STIRAP can generate a mechanical Bell state from a single phonon Fock state of one of the mechanical modes with the other mechanical mode in the vacuum state, and a product state from a coherent state. Relative phases between Fock basis components in the final state of STIRAP are determined by the phonon-number parity of the initial state. Furthermore, the system is numerically studied to determine the effects of dissipation, and it is concluded that high-fidelity entanglement can be achieved via fractional STIRAP using state-of-the-art cryogenic cooling and mechanical devices. Finally, an interferometric protocol using time-reversed fractional STIRAP is proposed to quantify entanglement between two mechanical modes.

</details>


### [131] [Construction and characterization of measures in block coherence resource theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28712)
*Xiangyu Chen,Qiang Lei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究块相干性资源理论中的相干性度量构建与比较，提出了基于α-z Rényi相对熵和Tsallis相对算子熵的相干性度量族，并建立了不同度量间的数值不等式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块相干性资源理论通过正交投影算子定义相干性，扩展了传统相干性框架。需要系统研究块相干性度量的构造方法，并比较不同度量间的数值关系和排序特性，以深化对相干性作为量子资源的理解。

Method: 1. 提出两种通用的相干性度量构造方法；2. 基于α-z Rényi相对熵构建双参数度量族；3. 基于Tsallis相对算子熵构建度量族；4. 通过理论证明和数值反例比较不同度量的排序关系；5. 建立约束度量值的通用数值不等式；6. 使用C_{α,1}度量分析Kominis主方程复杂动力学演化中的相干性作用。

Result: 1. 成功构造了基于α-z Rényi相对熵和Tsallis相对算子熵的块相干性度量族；2. 揭示了不同度量间的排序关系，并提供了数值反例；3. 建立了一系列约束度量值的通用数值不等式；4. 展示了相干性在包含复合反应的Kominis主方程复杂动力学演化中的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究系统发展了块相干性资源理论中的度量构造框架，建立了不同度量间的数值关系，为相干性作为量子资源的量化提供了新工具，并展示了相干性在量子动力学演化中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum coherence, as a direct manifestation of the quantum superposition principle, is a crucial resource in quantum information processing. Block coherence resource theory generalizes the traditional coherence framework by defining coherence via a set of orthogonal projectors. Within this framework, we investigates the construction and comparison of block coherence measures. First, we propose two universal methods for constructing coherence measures and introduce a two-parameter family of measures based on the $α$-$z$ Rényi relative entropy and a family of measures based on the Tsallis relative operator entropy. Second, through theoretical proofs and numerical counterexamples, we compares the ordering relations and numerical magnitudes among different block coherence measures and establishes a series of universal numerical inequalities to constrain their values. Besides, we also use $C_{α,1}$ to show the role of coherence in complex dynamic evolution of the Kominis master equation that includes recombination reactions.

</details>

### [132] [Anomalous phonon dispersion near yielding in athermal crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26825)
*Fumiaki Nakai,Michio Otsuki,Kuniyasu Saitoh,Hiroaki Katsuragi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文研究了有序非热固体在屈服点附近的振动特性，发现屈服由方向性扩展的多模软化控制，而非单一局域失稳，在波数空间形成十字形低频区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序非热固体在屈服点附近的振动特性仍未被充分理解，需要揭示屈服过程的微观机制和标度行为。

Method: 通过分析剪切晶体中的振动模式，研究屈服点附近的频率-波矢关系，推导标度律，并分析振动态密度和长度尺度的变化。

Result: 发现屈服由方向性扩展的多模软化控制，在波数空间形成十字形低频区域；在软方向上，声学色散从ω∼k变为ω∼k²；振动态密度从德拜标度过渡到非德拜标度，并出现发散的长度尺度。

Conclusion: 有序非热固体的屈服不是由单一局域失稳控制，而是由方向性扩展的多模软化机制主导，这一发现为理解屈服过程提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Vibrational properties of ordered athermal solids near yielding remain poorly understood. We show that yielding in a sheared crystal is governed not by a single localized instability but by directionally extended multimode softening that forms a cross-shaped low-frequency region in wave number space. Near yielding, the acoustic dispersion $ω\sim k$ is replaced by $ω\sim k^2$ along the soft direction, and the vibrational density of states crosses over from Debye to non-Debye scaling, with a diverging length scale. We analytically derive these scaling laws.

</details>


### [133] [Bubble-induced versus thermodynamic voltage losses during pressurized alkaline water electrolysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26855)
*Hannes Rox,Feng Liang,Robert Baumann,Mateusz M. Marzec,Krystian Sokołowski,Xuegeng Yang,Andrés F. Lasagni,Roel van de Krol,Kerstin Eckert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究气泡对水电解效率的影响，发现高压下气泡尺寸减小可降低过电位，补偿热力学电压损失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解气泡如何影响水电解效率对于实现可再生能源制氢的经济竞争力至关重要。高压电解可降低氢气机械压缩成本，因此需要研究绝对压力对电化学性能和气泡尺寸的影响。

Method: 使用直接激光写入技术制备不同尺寸的柱状图案镍电极，在1-6 bar压力范围内研究气泡尺寸变化。通过电流密度变化（-25 mA/cm²到100 mA/cm²）测量阴极电位变化，并应用白金汉π定理推导无量纲数。

Result: 气泡尺寸随压力增加而减小；柱状尺寸增大时气泡在所有压力下都变大。在-25 mA/cm²时，阴极电位随压力增加符合能斯特方程的热力学电压损失（约23 mV）。但在100 mA/cm²时，过电位降低约60 mV，表明气泡尺寸减小补偿了热力学电压损失。

Conclusion: 高压下气泡尺寸减小可最小化气泡引起的损失，从而补偿热力学电压损失。通过无量纲数可表征气泡诱导损失与热力学电压损失的比例，为优化高压水电解系统提供理论指导。

Abstract: Understanding how bubbles influence the efficiency of water electrolysis is crucial to achieve economically competitive hydrogen, generated by renewable energy sources, such as wind and solar power. Water electrolysis is typically performed at high pressures to reduce the cost of energy-intensive mechanical compression of the produced H2. Thus, a better understanding of how the absolute pressure affects electrochemical performance and bubble size is necessary. In general, bubble sizes decrease as the pressure increases. Using different-sized pillar-patterned Ni electrodes generated by Direct Laser Writing, the detached bubble sizes can be modified even at elevated pressures. As the pillar size increases, the bubbles become larger at all pressures investigated from 1 to 6 bar. At a current density of -25 mA/cm2, the cathodic potential increases with pressure according to the thermodynamic voltage losses given by the Nernst equation (~ 23 mV at p = 6 bar). Surprisingly, increasing the current density to 100 mA/cm2 leads to a reduction of the overpotential by up to ~ 60 mV. Reduced bubble sizes at increased pressures minimize the losses caused by the bubbles, thereby compensating for the thermodynamic voltage penalty. Applying the Buckingham Π-theorem enables the derivation of dimensionless numbers to characterize the ratio of bubble-induced and thermodynamic voltage losses

</details>


### [134] [Giant Magnetostriction by Design: A First-Principles Screening of Co-based Heusler Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26864)
*Pengju Wu,Jie Du,Liang Yao,Hang Li,Xiaodong Zhou,Tao Zhu,Wenhong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理筛选出10种具有大磁致伸缩性能的Co基Heusler合金，其中Co₃Si达到-966 ppm，并提出两种增强策略：费米能级调控和自旋轨道耦合放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高性能、无稀土磁致伸缩材料对传感、驱动和微机电系统技术发展至关重要。Heusler合金作为有前景但尚未充分探索的材料类别，需要系统研究其磁致伸缩性能。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法系统研究了25种Co基全Heusler合金Co₂YZ（Y=V、Cr、Mn、Fe、Co；Z=Al、Ga、Si、Ge、Sn）的磁致伸缩性能，并提出两种工程化策略：费米能级调控（通过Sb掺杂）和自旋轨道耦合放大（通过Re取代）。

Result: 筛选出10种预测磁致伸缩|λ₀₀₁|>100 ppm的化合物，其中Co₃Si达到-966 ppm。通过Sb掺杂将Co₃Sn磁致伸缩提升至-905 ppm，通过Re取代将Co₂CrGa磁致伸缩提升至-1008 ppm。发现磁致伸缩与Y位过渡金属选择之间存在线性关系的预测规则。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅识别了新型磁致伸缩候选材料，还建立了基于物理的设计原则，为加速发现新型功能磁性材料提供了清晰指导。

Abstract: The pursuit of high-performance, rare-earth-free magnetostrictive materials is crucial for advancing technologies in sensing, actuation, and microelectromechanical systems. Heusler alloys represent a promising, yet underexplored, class of materials for this purpose. In this work, we perform a systematic first-principles investigation of the magnetostrictive properties of 25 Co-based full Heusler alloys, Co$_2$YZ (Y = V, Cr, Mn, Fe, Co; Z = Al, Ga, Si, Ge, Sn). Our screening identifies 10 compounds with large predicted magnetostriction ($|λ_{001}| > 100$~ppm), highlighted by Co$_3$Si with a giant value of -966~ppm. Furthermore, we demonstrate two effective strategies for engineering magnetostriction: (i) tuning the Fermi level, which enhances the magnetostriction of Co$_3$Sn to -905~ppm via Sb doping, and (ii) amplifying the spin-orbit coupling, which boosts the magnetostriction of Co$_2$CrGa to a colossal -1008~ppm through Re substitution. Our analysis reveals a general predictive rule, uncovering a linear relationship between the magnetostriction and the choice of the Y-site transition metal. This work not only identifies novel candidates for magnetostrictive applications but also establishes clear, physically-grounded design principles to accelerate the discovery of new functional magnetic materials.

</details>


### [135] [Electrostatic Effects of Self Trapped Holes in Gallium Oxide Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26958)
*Nathan Wriedt,Joe McGlone,Davide Orlandini,Siddharth Rajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了氧化镓器件中光生自陷空穴通过福勒-诺德海姆隧穿机制影响静电学和电流传导的新机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氧化镓作为超宽带隙半导体在功率电子和UV-C光电子领域具有优异性能，但其在光照下的行为尚不清楚，特别是光生自陷空穴如何影响器件静电学和电流传导机制

Method: 采用具有半透明Ni阳极的垂直肖特基光电二极管，在黑暗和带隙以上光照条件下进行电容-电压、电流-电压和温度依赖性I-V测量，并应用福勒-诺德海姆隧穿理论分析

Result: 传统镜像力势垒降低模型需要不切实际的高界面电场，而福勒-诺德海姆隧穿理论能够合理解释测量的光电流和光电容结果，量化了自陷空穴的二维浓度，证实光照诱导电荷显著改变器件静电学

Conclusion: 理解这种基于隧穿的光电流增益机制对于设计用于UV-C探测器和功率电子学的氧化镓器件至关重要，揭示了光生自陷空穴通过隧穿机制影响器件性能的新物理机制

Abstract: Gallium oxide is an ultra-wide bandgap semiconductor with exceptional properties for power electronics and UV-C optoelectronics, but its behavior under illumination remains poorly understood. In this work, we investigate how optically generated self-trapped holes influence electrostatics and current conduction in gallium oxide devices. Using a vertical Schottky photodiode with a semi-transparent Ni anode, we performed capacitance-voltage, current-voltage, and temperature-dependent I-V measurements under dark and above-bandgap illumination. Analysis of photocurrent gain reveals that conventional image-force barrier-lowering models require unrealistically high interfacial electric fields, suggesting the presence of an alternative mechanism. By applying Fowler-Nordheim tunneling theory, we reconcile measured photocurrents and photo-capacitance results with physically plausible fields and quantify the two-dimensional concentration of self-trapped holes. Our findings demonstrate that illumination-induced charge significantly alters device electrostatics. Understanding this tunneling-based photocurrent gain mechanism is critical for designing gallium oxide devices for UV-C detectors and power electronics.

</details>


### [136] [Heterointerface-Engineered Electrochemically Exfoliated MoS2/WS2 2D-Layered Nanocomposite for Efficient Visible-Light Photocatalytic Degradation of Sorafenib](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26960)
*I. Agnes Felicia Roy,Kuo Yuan Hwa,Aravindan Santhan,Slava V Rotkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过电化学剥离法制备MoS2/WS2异质结复合材料，用于可见光催化降解抗癌药物索拉非尼，在2小时内达到92%降解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水环境中抗癌药物等药物污染物日益增多，传统降解方法难以有效去除索拉非尼等持久性药物，需要开发高效的光催化降解技术。

Method: 采用电化学剥离法制备超薄MoS2/WS2二维/二维层状纳米复合材料，通过FE-SEM、AFM、拉曼光谱和紫外-可见光谱进行结构表征，评估其在可见光下对索拉非尼的光催化降解性能。

Result: MoS2/WS2异质结复合材料相比纯MoS2和WS2纳米片表现出显著增强的光催化效率，在可见光照射2小时内对索拉非尼的降解率达到约92%。

Conclusion: 电化学剥离的MoS2/WS2异质结复合材料通过形成Type-II能带排列促进电荷分离和转移，抑制电子-空穴复合，增强可见光吸收和活性氧物种生成，为去除持久性药物污染物提供了可行的催化平台。

Abstract: The increasing prevalence of pharmaceutical contaminants within the aquatic environment has generated considerable environmental concerns, especially regarding persistent anticancer medications like the kinase inhibitor sorafenib (SRF), which are inadequately eliminated by standard degradation methods. A heterointerface-engineered MoS2/WS2, 2D/2D layered nanocomposite was fabricated using an electrochemical exfoliation method to facilitate effective visible-light-driven photocatalytic degradation of SRF. The electrochemical exfoliation method yielded ultrathin 9.62-layer thickness MoS2/WS2 nanosheets with numerous exposed edge sites and an increased specific surface area, facilitating the development of well-interconnected van der Waals heterointerfaces. Comprehensive structural and morphological examinations utilizing field emission scanning electron microscopy (FE-SEM), atomic force microscopy (AFM), Raman spectroscopy, and UV-visible spectroscopy validated the effective synthesis of few-layer nanosheets and their heterostructure interfaces. In contrast to the pure MoS2 and WS2 nanosheets, the MoS2/WS2 heterostructure composite demonstrated significantly enhanced photocatalytic efficacy, attaining roughly 92 % degradation of SRF within 2h under visible-light exposure. The enhanced catalytic efficiency is mainly due to the establishment of a Type-II band alignment at the MoS2/WS2 interface, facilitating effective charge separation and directional charge transfer while inhibiting electron-hole recombination. The robust interfacial interaction among the transition metal dichalcogenide layers accelerates visible-light absorption and the production of reactive oxygen species.
