This study illustrates that electrochemically exfoliated MoS2/WS2 heterostructure composites serve as a viable catalytic platform for the successful removal of persistent pharmaceutical pollutants.

</details>


### [137] [Channeling-in channeling-out revisited: selected area electron channeling and electron backscatter diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27082)
*T. Ben Britton,M. Haroon Qaiser,Ruth M. Birch*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合选区电子通道花样（SA-ECP）和电子背散射衍射（EBSD），直接探测了通道效应（channeling-in）对EBSD信号的影响，发现多种质量指标均受晶体学调制影响，这对常规EBSD分析有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描电子显微镜中的电子通道效应（channeling-in和channeling-out）在定量分析中常被简化或忽略，但其对EBSD信号的相互影响尚未得到充分研究，需要直接实验验证通道效应对EBSD数据质量指标的影响。

Method: 使用单晶硅片，在记录每个入射束方向的EBSD花样同时获取选区电子通道花样（SA-ECPs），直接探测通道效应对EBSD信号的影响。分析多种质量指标：基于Hough变换的质量指标（花样质量、带对比度、带斜率）、花样匹配互相关系数以及基于傅里叶变换的信噪比。

Result: 所有质量指标均表现出强烈的晶体学调制，遵循底层ECP结构，这种调制在原始和背景校正后的花样中都存在。类似的宽角度通道特征在常规低倍率EBSD图中也可见，表明通道效应在常规映射条件下相关，而不仅限于专门的ECP实验。

Conclusion: 通道效应会显著偏倚基于质量的EBSD数据解释，影响花样模糊分析、高分辨率应变映射以及依赖衍射花样细微变化的统计或机器学习方法。提出的SA-ECP与EBSD结合策略为可视化和控制SEM中的通道效应耦合提供了实用框架，为设计实验和探测器配置以减轻或利用这些动力学效应提供了新途径。

Abstract: Scanning electron microscopy combined with electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) and electron channeling provides rich crystallographic contrast, but the mutual influence of channeling-in and channeling-out is often simplified or neglected in quantitative analyses. In this work, we use selected-area electron channeling patterns (SA-ECPs) acquired from a single-crystal silicon wafer while recording an EBSD pattern at every incident beam direction, thereby directly probing how channeling-in affects the EBSD signal. We show that common Hough-based quality metrics (pattern quality, band contrast, and band slope), pattern-matching cross-correlation coefficients, and Fourier-based signal-to-noise ratios all exhibit strong crystallographic modulations that follow the underlying ECP, in both raw and background-corrected patterns. Similar wide-angle channeling features are also visible in conventional, low-magnification EBSD maps, indicating that channeling-in effects are relevant under routine mapping conditions and not only in specialized ECP experiments. These observations highlight that channeling-in can significantly bias quality-based interpretation of EBSD data, with consequences for methods such as pattern blurring analysis, high-resolution strain mapping, and emerging statistical or machine-learning approaches that rely on subtle variations in diffraction patterns. The combined SA-ECP and EBSD strategy presented here offers a practical framework to visualize and potentially control channeling-in/channeling-out coupling in the SEM, suggesting new routes to design experiments and detector configurations that either mitigate or intentionally exploit these dynamical effects.

</details>


### [138] [The switching of bipolar and unipolar magnetostriction in polycrystalline ZnO film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27246)
*Suman Guchhait,Saumen Chaudhuri,A. K. Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究利用自主开发的光学悬臂梁磁强计，在室温下研究了多晶ZnO薄膜在面内磁场旋转0-90度时的磁致伸缩特性，观察到双极性和单极性磁致伸缩的切换行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多晶ZnO薄膜在面内旋转磁场下的磁致伸缩特性，探索其在微纳电子器件中作为传感器和执行器的应用潜力。

Method: 使用自主开发的光学悬臂梁磁强计（CBM）装置，在室温下测量多晶ZnO薄膜在面内磁场旋转0-90度时的磁致伸缩响应。

Result: ZnO薄膜表现出双极性（拉伸和压缩）和单极性（拉伸/压缩）磁致伸缩特性，且随着面内磁场旋转角度变化，观察到双极性和单极性磁致伸缩的切换行为，这种切换归因于材料的晶体各向异性。

Conclusion: ZnO薄膜的双极性和单极性磁致伸缩特性使其成为制造微纳电子器件中传感器和执行器的合适候选材料。

Abstract: We report the investigation of magnetostrictive property of polycrystalline ZnO film at room temperature on rotating a magnetic field in the plane of the film from 0 deg to 90 deg by an indigenously developed optical cantilever beam magnetometer (CBM) setup. In this study, the film exhibits bipolar (tensile & compressive) and unipolar (tensile/compressive) nature of magnetostriction with the angle of rotation of in-plane magnetic field. Moreover, we observe switching behavior of bipolar and unipolar magnetostriction as the applied field is rotated in the plane of the film. The switching of magnetostriction is attributed to the crystal anisotropy of the material. The appearance of bipolar and unipolar magnetostriction has made the ZnO film a suitable candidate for the fabrication of sensors as well as actuators applicable in micro and nano-electronic devices.

</details>


### [139] [Low-scaling \textit{GW} calculation of quasi-particle energies within numerical atomic orbital framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27292)
*Min-Ye Zhang,Peize Lin,Rong Shi,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数值原子轨道（NAO）基组框架的时空算法，将GW近似中极化函数和自能的计算复杂度从O(N⁴)降低到O(N²)或更低，通过局部分辨率恒等式（LRI）技术在实空间实现低标度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW近似是描述真实材料电子能带结构的广泛应用方法，但其高计算成本阻碍了在大尺度系统中的应用。传统平面波基组中的时空算法将标度从O(N⁴)降低到O(N³)，但仍存在较大的前置系数和内存成本。

Method: 在数值原子轨道（NAO）基组框架内开发了时空算法，通过在实空间计算极化函数和自能，利用局部分辨率恒等式（LRI）技术实现低标度算法。该方法已在LibRPA库中实现，并与FHI-aims代码包接口。

Result: 对晶体固体的基准计算表明，低标度实现产生的准粒子能量与FHI-aims中先前实现的传统O(N⁴)k空间形式非常一致。对于所研究的系统，观察到的总体标度相对于规范方法显著降低，低标度实现在包含少于100个原子的系统中已经具有优势。

Conclusion: 基于NAO的LRI增强时空GW算法成功地将计算复杂度降低到O(N²)或更好，为大规模系统的GW计算提供了高效解决方案，已在LibRPA库中实现并与FHI-aims集成。

Abstract: The many-body perturbation theory within the $GW$ approximation is a widely used method for describing the electronic band structures in real materials. Its application to large-scale systems is, however, impeded by its high computational cost. The rate-limiting steps in a typical $GW$ implementation are the evaluation of the polarization function under the random phase approximation (RPA) and the evaluation of the $GW$ self-energy, both of which have a canonical $O(N^4)$ scaling with $N$ being the system size. The conventional space-time algorithm within the plane-wave basis sets reduces the scaling from $O(N^4)$ to $O(N^3)$, albeit with a large prefactor and increased memory cost. Here, we present a space-time algorithm within the numerical atomic orbital (NAO) basis-set framework, for which the evaluation of the polarization function and self-energy is formally reduced to $O(N^2)$ or better with respect to system size. This is achieved by computing these quantities in real space, where low-scaling algorithms can be formulated by leveraging the localized resolution of identity (LRI) technique. The resulting NAO-based, LRI-enhanced space-time $GW$ algorithm has been implemented in the LibRPA library interfaced with the FHI-aims code package. Benchmark calculations for crystalline solids show that the low-scaling implementation yields quasi-particle energies in close agreement with the conventional $O(N^4)$ k-space formalism previously implemented in FHI-aims. For the systems studied here, the observed overall scaling is substantially reduced relative to the canonical approach, and the low-scaling implementation becomes advantageous already for systems containing fewer than 100 atoms.

</details>


### [140] [Current-tunable room temperature ferromagnetism and current-driven phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27298)
*Jianping Guo,Peng Rao,Xinhao Huang,Tailai Xu,Yuxuan Guo,Jian Shao,Cheng Sun,Anton Orekhov,Thomas N. G. Meier,Johannes Knolle,Christian H. Back,Lin Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 电荷电流通过轨道磁化产生的有效磁场增强二维铁磁体Fe3Ge2Te的居里温度，使其超过室温，而非通过焦耳热抑制磁性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为电荷电流通过焦耳热抑制铁磁有序，本文旨在探索电流能否增强磁性有序，为二维铁磁体在室温下集成到自旋电子器件提供新途径

Method: 使用WTe2/Fe3Ge2Te异质结作为模型系统，研究WTe2中的电荷电流通过轨道磁化产生的有效磁场对相邻FGT铁磁性能和磁相变的调控作用

Result: 电荷电流显著增强FGT的居里温度，使其超过室温；同时能够可控调节FGT的相变，验证了铁磁体-顺磁体相变的标度行为

Conclusion: 这项工作为二维铁磁体在技术相关温度下集成到自旋电子功能器件提供了新途径，并为探索铁磁系统中电流驱动的新现象开辟了道路

Abstract: It is generally assumed that the application of a charge-current in ferromagnetic metals suppresses their ferromagnetic order through trivial Joule heating. Here, we demonstrate that a charge current can instead enhance magnetic ordering. Using a WTe2/Fe3Ge2Te (FGT) stack as a model system, we show that a charge current flowing in WTe2 controls the ferromagnetic properties and magnetic phase transition of the adjacent FGT via a current-induced effective magnetic-field arising from orbital magnetization. Remarkably, the charge current drives a substantial enhancement of the Curie temperature, boosting it well above room temperature. Furthermore, we show that the charge-current enables controlled tuning of the phase transitions in FGT, which confirms the scaling behaviour of a ferromagnet-paramagnet phase transition. This work provides a pathway for integrating two-dimensional ferromagnets into spintronic functionalities at technologically relevant temperatures and for exploring novel current-driven phenomena in ferromagnetic systems.

</details>


### [141] [Alloying Controlled Tuning of Interfacial Spin Orbit Interaction and Magnetic Damping in Crystalline FeCo Alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27307)
*Hongrui Lao,Matthias Kronseder,Zhe Yuan,Thomas Narr,Thomas N. G. Meier,Nadine Mundigl,Christian H. Back,Lin Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过合金化调控FeCo薄膜界面自旋轨道相互作用，实现超低阻尼和自旋轨道场连续可调


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非中心对称铁磁体中的本征自旋轨道场在基础研究和应用方面都很重要，但一旦合成后其强度难以调控，因为这是主要体性质。需要寻找调控界面自旋轨道相互作用的方法。

Method: 在GaAs(001)衬底上生长单晶FeCo薄膜，通过改变Co浓度进行合金化。使用自旋轨道铁磁共振技术测量Lande g因子、吉尔伯特阻尼(α)和界面自旋轨道场。

Result: Lande g因子、吉尔伯特阻尼和界面自旋轨道场都表现出对Co浓度的共同非单调依赖性。在x~0.2处出现显著最小值，实现超低阻尼α~0.0015。观察到α与(g-2)^2之间的线性比例关系，建立了界面自旋轨道相互作用与磁弛豫之间的直接关联。

Conclusion: 合金化是调控单晶铁磁体-半导体异质结构中界面自旋轨道相互作用和阻尼的有效手段，为自旋电子学器件设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: The discovery of intrinsic spin orbit fields in noncentrosymmetric ferromagnets has attracted considerable interest for both fundamental studies and technological applications. However, once such materials are synthesized, the strength of the spin orbit fields is difficult to tune because it is primarily a bulk property. Here, we demonstrate that the interfacial spin orbit interaction (SOI) in single crystalline FeCo thin films grown on GaAs(001) can be continuously tuned via alloying. Using spin orbit ferromagnetic resonance, we find that the Lande g factor, the Gilbert damping (alpha), and the interfacial spin orbit fields exhibit a common nonmonotonic dependence on Co concentration. A pronounced minimum occurs near x ~ 0.2 where an ultra low damping alpha ~ 0.0015 is achieved. Furthermore, we observe linear scaling between alpha and (g-2)^2, establishing a direct correlation between interfacial SOI and magnetic relaxation. These results identify alloying as an effective knob to engineer interfacial SOI and damping in single crystalline ferromagnet semiconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [142] [Identification and Prediction of Photoplasticity in Semiconductors Using Feature Engineering and Machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27316)
*Huicong Chen,Mingqiang Li,Zheyuan Ji,Yu Zou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过高通量纳米压痕测量和机器学习方法，建立了可解释的光塑性预测框架，识别了影响半导体光诱导塑性变形的最关键物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光塑性（光诱导的塑性变形变化）对半导体材料的机械耐久性和制造至关重要，但其控制机制因多物理场耦合作用而尚未完全理解，需要建立可解释的预测框架。

Method: 1. 进行高通量纳米压痕测量，收集暗条件和光照条件下的硬度配对数据集；2. 构建涵盖电学、力学和光学特性的物理信息描述符；3. 识别十个最具信息量的特征（包括带隙、击穿场强、折射率等）；4. 建立可解释的机器学习框架，推导可迁移的设计规则。

Result: 成功识别了影响光塑性的十个关键物理特征，建立了能够预测半导体材料光塑性的可解释机器学习模型，并提取了可迁移的设计规则，为调控光响应机械行为提供了实用路径。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合实验测量和物理信息机器学习，为理解和预测半导体材料的光塑性提供了系统框架，能够提取与机制相关的可迁移指导原则，用于设计光响应半导体材料和器件。

Abstract: Photoplasticity, the light-induced change in plastic deformation, plays a pivotal role in the mechanical durability and manufacturing of semiconductor materials. Yet, its governing mechanisms remain incompletely understood, owing to the interplay of coupled multiphysics factors. Here, we conduct high-throughput nanoindentation measurements to compile a dataset of paired hardness values in dark and light conditions. Then, we engineer physics-informed descriptors spanning electrical, mechanical, and optical properties, and identify the ten most informative features, including bandgap, breakdown field, and refractive index, to enable an interpretable machine learning framework that yields transferable design rules for light-tunable semiconductor mechanics. By identifying and predicting photoplasticity in semiconductors, this work provides a practical pathway for extracting mechanism-linked, transferable guidelines to engineer light-responsive mechanical behavior in semiconductor materials and devices.

</details>


### [143] [Spin waves and instabilities in the collinear four component antiferromagnetic materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27394)
*Pavel A. Andreev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究四组分反铁磁材料中自旋的小振幅扰动，重点分析up-up-down-down平衡态，发现自旋波色散关系，揭示负频率平方解的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多组分反铁磁材料中自旋波的基本特性，特别是四组分up-up-down-down构型，为理解复杂反铁磁系统的动力学行为提供理论基础。

Method: 采用经典自旋的一维链模型，在最近邻相互作用近似下分析自旋波色散关系；同时考虑连续介质极限下的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程，并指出标准形式已超越最近邻近似。

Result: 当平衡自旋平行于各向异性轴时，系统存在两个自旋波模式；当垂直时，可推导出四个自旋波的色散方程。在所有可能的各向异性常数符号和大小下，至少存在一个负频率平方解。

Conclusion: 四组分反铁磁材料中的自旋波行为复杂，存在负频率平方解表明系统可能存在不稳定性；标准Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程已超越最近邻近似，需注意其适用条件。

Abstract: The small amplitude perturbations of spins are considered in the four component antiferromagnetic materials with the equilibrium state of form up-up-down-down (uniaxial samples). Other configurations for the four component antiferromagnetic materials and two component antiferromagnetic materials are briefly considered for the comparison with main regime. Dispersion dependencies of two spin waves existing in the system are found if equilibrium spins are parallel to the anisotropy axis. Dispersion equation leading to a possibility of four spin waves is derived if equilibrium spins are perpendicular to the anisotropy axis. It is found that at least one solution has negative frequency square for all possible modules and signs of the anisotropy constants. Calculations are made for the one dimensional chain of classical spins in the approximation of the nearest neighbours interaction. Next, we also addressed the nearest neighbours interaction approximation in the limit of the continuous medium (for the Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation). Mostly applied form of the Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert equation goes beyond the nearest neighbours interaction approximation. The difference is described. Required assumptions are described.

</details>


### [144] [Twist-Angle Engineering of Moiré Potentials for High-Performance Ionics in Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27453)
*Gen Fukuzawa,Yebin Lee,Teruyasu Mizoguchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 扭曲双层石墨烯(tBLG)通过特定扭转角(Σ37, 9.43°)同时实现了有利的锂插层能量(-2.39 eV)和低扩散势垒(0.14 eV)，解决了传统AA/AB堆叠的插层稳定性与扩散速度之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双层石墨烯在锂离子传输中存在固有权衡：AA堆叠提供稳定插层但扩散势垒高，AB堆叠扩散快但插层稳定性差。扭曲双层石墨烯(tBLG)有望克服这一限制，但不同扭转角下的系统理解仍然有限。

Method: 使用第一性原理密度泛函理论(DFT)研究tBLG中的锂插层，通过势能面(PES)映射评估多个扭转角下的插层能量和扩散势垒。采用平滑原子位置重叠(SOAP)描述符分析PES，并建立基于有限结构的模型预测未测试构型的PES。

Result: Σ37结构(9.43°)在所有测试结构中同时实现了最有利的插层能量(-2.39 eV)和最低扩散势垒(0.14 eV)。SOAP分析表明PES由局部原子环境控制，基于有限结构训练的模型能高精度预测未测试构型的PES。

Conclusion: 扭曲角工程为二维层状材料中的离子传输提供了系统框架，特定扭转角能同时优化插层稳定性和扩散动力学。基于描述符的模型可高效筛选构型，避免耗时的第一性原理计算，为先进能量存储材料设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Controlling ion transport is a fundamental challenge for advanced energy storage. Bilayer graphene offers a unique platform for modulating ion diffusion via twist-angle-dependent moire superlattices, yet conventional stacking configurations face an inherent trade-off: AA stacking provides stable Li intercalation but high diffusion barriers, while AB stacking enables fast diffusion but poor intercalation stability. Twisted bilayer graphene (tBLG) offers potential to overcome this limitation, yet systematic understanding across different twist angles remains limited. Here, we investigate Li intercalation in tBLG using first-principles density functional theory, evaluating intercalation energies and diffusion barriers across multiple twist angles through potential energy surface (PES) mapping. The Sigma 37 structure (9.43 degrees) simultaneously achieves the most favorable intercalation energy (-2.39 eV) and the lowest diffusion barrier (0.14 eV) among all structures examined, resolving the conventional stacking trade-off. Furthermore, using the Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP) descriptor, we demonstrate that the PES is governed by local atomic environments and that a model trained on limited structures predicts the PES of untested configurations with high accuracy. This transferability enables efficient screening without exhaustive first-principles calculations, establishing a systematic framework for twist-angle engineering of ion transport in two-dimensional layered materials.

</details>


### [145] [The Shape of Chocolate: A Topological Perspective on Food Microstructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27854)
*Matteo Rucco*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于拓扑数据分析（TDA）的计算框架，用于表征黑巧克力调温过程中可可脂的分子自组织行为，通过持久同调熵等拓扑指标识别最优调温晶型（Form V）的独特特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统巧克力调温过程质量控制依赖于经验判断和有限的分析方法，缺乏对可可脂分子自组织过程的定量表征。研究旨在开发基于拓扑数据分析的非侵入式质量指标，为工业巧克力调温过程提供更精确的监控手段。

Method: 采用物理启发的粒子模拟对N=100个甘油三酯分子在15-60°C全温度范围内进行建模，涵盖可可脂的六种晶型（Form I-VI）以及熔融和过热状态。在每个温度点构建Vietoris-Rips过滤，计算持久同调群H0、H1和H2，并通过持久熵E = -∑p_i log₂(p_i)分析持久图，其中p_i = l_i/∑l_j，l_i = death_i - birth_i。

Result: 研究发现最优调温晶型Form V（29.5-34°C）具有独特的拓扑特征：H0持久熵局部最小值（E0 = 5.74±0.04比特）、第一贝蒂数β₁显著降低（1562±35）以及H2熵全局最小值（E2 = 12.29±0.25比特），反映了层间空腔的有序排列。通过Rucco（2026）的定理1和推论1，证明持久熵能在相变诱导宏观尺度拓扑质量变化时，以渐近非零间隙区分有序和相无序。

Conclusion: 拓扑数据分析指标可作为工业巧克力调温过程的非侵入式质量指示器，通过识别Form V晶型的独特拓扑特征，为巧克力制造提供更精确的过程监控和质量控制方法。

Abstract: We present a computational framework for characterizing the molecular self-organization of cocoa butter (Theobroma cacao) during dark chocolate tempering through the lens of Topological Data Analysis (TDA). A physics-inspired particle simulation models N=100 triglyceride molecules across the full temperature range 15--60 degrees C, spanning all six crystalline polymorphs of cocoa butter (Forms I--VI) as well as the melt and superheating regimes. At each temperature tick, we construct a Vietoris-Rips filtration and compute the persistent homology groups H0 (connected components), H1 (independent cycles), and H2 (3D voids). The resulting persistence diagrams are analyzed via persistent entropy E = -sum_i p_i log2(p_i), where p_i = l_i / sum_j l_j and l_i = death_i - birth_i denotes feature lifetime; essential classes are assigned death = m+1 (m = eps_max) following the standard persistent entropy convention (Rucco 2026, arXiv:2602.09058). Our results demonstrate that Form V (the optimal tempering polymorph, 29.5--34 degrees C) is characterized by a distinctive topological signature: a local minimum in the H0 persistent entropy (E0 = 5.74 +/- 0.04 bits), a pronounced depression in the first Betti number beta_1 (1562 +/- 35), and a global minimum in the H2 entropy (E2 = 12.29 +/- 0.25 bits) reflecting coherent inter-bilayer lamellar cavities. Via Theorem 1 and Corollary 1 of Rucco (2026), persistent entropy is proven to separate the ordered and disordered phases by an asymptotically non-vanishing gap whenever a phase transition induces the creation or destruction of topological mass at macroscopic scales -- a condition we verify empirically across all eight cocoa butter regimes. These findings suggest that TDA-based metrics could serve as non-invasive quality indicators for industrial chocolate tempering processes.

</details>


### [146] [The rise of unconventional magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27505)
*Xiaobing Chen,Weizhao Chen,Qihang Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述文章系统探讨了非常规磁性的动量空间特性，包括自旋织构、量子几何和涌现准粒子，从对称性分析角度阐释时间反演奇物理响应机制，并展望与铁电性、超导性和莫尔工程的耦合应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非常规磁性代表了凝聚态物理的范式转变，它结合了反铁磁体的快速高密度优势和铁磁体的易读写能力。通过自旋空间群理论的发展，可以系统地将磁几何与相对论自旋轨道耦合解耦，从而探索具有补偿磁化但展现时间反演奇响应的非常规磁体。

Method: 采用对称性分析方法，从动量空间的三个关键方面（自旋织构、量子几何和涌现准粒子）系统综述非常规磁性。基于自旋空间群理论框架，阐明时间反演奇物理响应的机制，包括非相对论自旋分裂、反常和非线性霍尔效应，以及奇异的电子和磁子拓扑相。

Result: 文章系统阐述了非常规磁性的对称性原理和物理响应机制，揭示了其在动量空间中的丰富物理现象，包括非相对论自旋分裂、多种霍尔效应以及拓扑相的形成，为理解非常规磁体的功能特性提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 通过对称性驱动的洞察，该综述突出了非常规磁体在开发下一代高速、高能效自旋电子器件中的功能潜力。文章还展望了将非常规磁性与铁电性、超导性和莫尔工程耦合的前沿研究方向，为未来多功能量子材料设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Unconventional magnetism represents a paradigm shift in condensed matter physics, effectively bridging the fast, high-density advantages of antiferromagnets with the facile read-write capability of ferromagnets. Recent developments in spin space group theory have established a systematic methodology to decouple magnetic geometry from relativistic spin-orbit coupling, driving the exploration of unconventional magnets that exhibit compensated magnetization with time-reversal-odd responses. Here, we review unconventional magnetism across three pivotal facets in momentum space: spin textures, quantum geometry, and emergent quasiparticles. From the perspective of symmetry analysis, we elucidate the mechanisms underlying time-reversal-odd physical responses, including non-relativistic spin splitting, anomalous and nonlinear Hall effects, and exotic electronic and magnonic topological phases. Finally, we provide a forward-looking perspective on coupling unconventional magnetism with ferroelectricity, superconductivity, and moiré engineering. By exploiting symmetry-driven insights, this review highlights the functional potential of unconventional magnets in developing next-generation, high-speed, and energy-efficient spintronic devices.

</details>


### [147] [Light-Tunable Giant Anomalous Hall Effect in the Flat-Band Magnetic Weyl Semimetal $\mathrm{AlFe_2O_4}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27512)
*Tingyan Chen,Shengpu Huang,Jing Fan,Dong-Hui Xu,Rui Wang,Da-Shuai Ma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文发现AlFe₂O₄作为铁磁半金属平台，集成了三维平带和Weyl物理，具有398 S·cm⁻¹的巨大反常霍尔电导，并通过Floquet工程实现了光控拓扑输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现巨大的反常霍尔效应及其有效调控是拓扑自旋电子学的基本目标。磁性Weyl半金属中的平带为实现最大反常霍尔效应提供了有前景的途径，但现实材料候选稀少，且Weyl节点动量分离的主动调控仍是关键挑战。

Method: 通过综合第一性原理计算，建立了反尖晶石AlFe₂O₄作为铁磁半金属平台；构建了对称约束紧束缚模型，揭示了微观电子耦合与宏观反常霍尔效应之间的确定性关系；利用圆偏振光进行Floquet工程，动态抑制有效耦合。

Result: 自旋轨道耦合诱导单对Weyl节点，产生398 S·cm⁻¹的巨大本征反常霍尔电导；光调制可控制地增大Weyl节点动量分离κ，缩短拓扑费米弧，并显著定量抑制反常霍尔电导。

Conclusion: AlFe₂O₄作为集成了三维平带和Weyl物理的现实铁磁半金属平台，通过Floquet工程实现了反常霍尔效应的光控调制，为超快光控拓扑输运提供了实用蓝图。

Abstract: Achieving a giant anomalous Hall effect (AHE) and enabling its effective tuning are fundamental goals for topological spintronics. Magnetic Weyl semimetals hosting flat bands offer a promising route to maximize the AHE. However, while theoretical models are well-established, realistic material candidates remain scarce. Since the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) is topologically dictated by the momentum separation ($κ$) between Weyl nodes, actively manipulating remains a key challenge. Here, through comprehensive first-principles calculations, we establish the inverse spinel $\mathrm{AlFe_2O_4}$ as a realistic ferromagnetic half-metallic platform integrating three-dimensional flat bands and Weyl physics. Spin-orbit coupling induces a single pair of Weyl nodes, yielding a giant intrinsic AHC of $398\ \mathrm{S}\cdot\mathrm{cm}^{-1}$. By constructing a symmetry-constrained tight-binding model, we uncover a deterministic relationship between microscopic electronic couplings and the macroscopic AHE. Exploiting this via Floquet engineering with circularly polarized light, we demonstrate that the effective couplings are dynamically suppressed. This optical modulation controllably enlarges $κ$, shortens the topological Fermi arcs, and drives a dramatic, quantitative suppression of the AHC, providing a practical blueprint for ultrafast, light-controlled topological transport.

</details>


### [148] [Incipient magnetic instability in RuO$_2$ with random phase approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28355)
*Diana Csontosová,Kyo-Hoon Ahn,Jan Kuneš*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用Hartree-Fock和随机相位近似研究RuO₂的不稳定性，基于三轨道Hubbard模型（无自旋轨道耦合），发现自旋磁化率是主导响应通道，在低温和化学计量条件下出现共线反铁磁有序，高温或空穴掺杂时出现非共线波矢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究RuO₂中的磁不稳定性，特别是探索其可能的altermagnetic（交替磁性）有序，并理解altermagnets与antiferromagnets之间的本质区别。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock近似结合随机相位近似，使用三轨道Hubbard模型（排除自旋轨道耦合），分析静态磁化率的特征值和特征向量，研究不同局域相互作用参数（U、J_H）和空穴掺杂浓度（n）下的非磁性相。

Result: 自旋磁化率是主导响应通道；在无自旋轨道耦合的化学计量系统中，低温下共线altermagnetic有序是主要不稳定性；高温或有限空穴掺杂时出现非共线波矢；通过交错Weiss场引起的能带分裂分析揭示了altermagnets与antiferromagnets的定性差异。

Conclusion: RuO₂在低温和化学计量条件下倾向于形成共线altermagnetic有序，而温度升高或掺杂会诱导非共线磁结构；研究阐明了altermagnets与常规反铁磁体的本质区别，为理解这类新型磁性材料提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study the instability in RuO$_2$ using the Hartree-Fock approximation followed by the random phase approximation. We employ a three-orbital Hubbard model without spin-orbit coupling. An analysis of the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the static susceptibility in the non-magnetic phase for various local interaction parameters $U$, $J_H$, and hole doping $n$ shows that the spin susceptibility is the dominant response channel. In the stoichiometric system without spin-orbit coupling, commensurate altermagnetic order is identified as the leading instability at sufficiently low temperatures, whereas at higher temperatures or finite hole doping, incommensurate wave vectors emerge. To elucidate the origin of the magnetic instability, we analyze the band spitting by the staggered Weiss field and discuss the qualitative difference between altermagnets and antiferromagnets.

</details>


### [149] [Anomalous Hall Conductivity as an Effective Means of Tracking the Floquet Weyl Nodes in Quasi-One-Dimensional $β$-Bi$_4$I$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27514)
*Qingfeng Huang,Shengpu Huang,Tingyan Chen,Jing Fan,Dong-Hui Xu,Xiaozhi Wu,Da-Shuai Ma,Rui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用反常霍尔效应作为全电学探针，追踪Floquet外尔半金属中外尔节点的动态演化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet工程为调控拓扑相（特别是Floquet外尔半金属）提供了强大工具，但建立实验可行的策略来追踪这些态的动力学演化仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: 在准一维材料β-Bi₄I₄上，结合第一性原理计算和对称性分析，利用圆偏振光打破时间反演对称性，通过连续调节驱动场的偏振相位φ来控制外尔节点的轨迹。

Result: 圆偏振光驱动系统从平庸绝缘体转变为具有非零贝里曲率通量的Floquet外尔半金属相；通过调节偏振相位φ，外尔节点可在高对称点处迁移并最终湮灭；反常霍尔电导率直接映射到这种拓扑演化上。

Conclusion: 反常霍尔效应可作为敏感的全电学探针，用于追踪Floquet外尔节点的产生和动力学演化，为实验探测Floquet拓扑相提供了可行策略。

Abstract: While Floquet engineering offers a powerful paradigm for manipulating topological phases, particularly Floquet Weyl semimetals, establishing an experimentally feasible strategy for tracking the dynamic evolution of such states remains a significant challenge. Here, we propose that the anomalous Hall effect (AHE), as a sensitive, all-electrical probe, can be used to track Floquet Weyl nodes. Using first-principles calculations and symmetry analysis on the quasi-one-dimensional material $β$-Bi$_4$I$_4$, we demonstrate that circularly polarized light breaks time-reversal symmetry, driving the system from a trivial insulator into a Floquet Weyl semimetal phase characterized by a nonzero Berry curvature flux. Crucially, by continuously tuning the polarization phase $\varphi$ of the driving field, we show that the trajectory of the induced Weyl nodes is highly controllable, leading to their migration and eventual annihilation at high-symmetry points. We reveal that the anomalous Hall conductivity maps directly onto this topological evolution, serving as a definitive fingerprint for the generation and dynamics of Weyl nodes.

</details>


### [150] [First-order polarization process as an alternative to antiferroelectricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27590)
*Louis Bastogne,Lukas Korosec,Evgenios Stylianidis,Daniel G. Porter,Gareth Nisbet,Clémentine Thibault,Jean-Marc Triscone,Marios Hadjimichael,Philippe Ghosez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文发现了一种通过一级极化过程在非极性CaTiO₃薄膜中实现双电滞回线的新机制，为获得类似反铁电体的双电滞回线提供了替代途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁电体因其典型的双电滞回线(P-E曲线)而备受关注，但自然界中反铁电材料稀少。磁学系统中存在类似的双磁滞回线现象，不仅出现在反铁磁体中，也出现在通过一级磁化过程实现场诱导磁化重定向的系统中。这启发研究者探索是否存在类似的"一级极化过程"来实现双电滞回线。

Method: 研究聚焦于非极性的CaTiO₃材料，该材料在拉伸应变下可转变为铁电体。通过在具有不同取向的NdGaO₃衬底上生长外延薄膜，结合理论计算和实验技术，研究了薄膜的电学性能。特别关注了特定构型下出现的双电滞回线现象。

Result: 研究发现，在特定构型的CaTiO₃薄膜中观察到了双电滞回线P-E曲线。通过分析证实，这种现象源于电场诱导的极化矢量突然旋转，即一种"一级极化过程"。这种机制与磁学中的一级磁化过程类似，但首次在电学系统中实现。

Conclusion: 一级极化过程为实现双电滞回线提供了一种有前景的替代途径，扩展了获得类似反铁电体电学响应的材料范围，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Antiferroelectrics generate significant interest since their polarization versus electric field (PE) curves show typical double-hysteresis loops appealing for various applications. Unfortunately, antiferroelectrics are rare. In magnetic compounds, magnetization versus magnetic field (M-H) curves can show analogous double hysteresis loops not only in antiferromagnets but also in systems exhibiting field-induced first-order reorientation of the magnetization through a so-called first-order magnetization process. Here, we show that appealing double-hysteresis P-E loops can also appear from an unprecedented first-order polarization process. Focusing on non-polar CaTiO3, which can be turned ferroelectric under tensile strain, we study epitaxial thin films on differently-oriented NdGaO3 substrates using a combination of theoretical and experimental techniques. We uncover that a certain configuration exhibits double-hysteresis P-E loops that we rationalize from a field-induced abrupt rotation of the polarization. Such a first-order polarization process establishes a promising alternative pathway to achieve double hysterisis P-E loops appealing for practical applications.

</details>


### [151] [Solving the inverse problem of X-ray absorption spectroscopy via physics-informed deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27684)
*Suyang Zhong,Boying Huang,Pengwei Xu,Fanjie Xu,Yuhao Zhao,Jun Cheng,Fujie Tang,Weinan E,Zhong-Qun Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SPT是一个物理信息深度学习框架，通过傅里叶对偶性连接理论模拟与实验数据，解决X射线吸收光谱反演问题，实现毫秒级结构解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非晶或动态环境中瞬态原子构型解析的基本瓶颈，克服X射线吸收光谱反演中固有的"多对一映射"病态问题。

Method: 利用光谱能量振荡与空间散射路径之间的傅里叶对偶性，将光谱分解到频域，从实验噪声中分离出稳健的结构配位信号，基于大规模理论数据集训练深度学习模型。

Result: 在电池阴极连续相变解析和非晶材料局部有序性识别方面达到最先进精度，实现毫秒级延迟，消除自主材料发现的主要计算障碍。

Conclusion: SPT建立了理论数据集与实验现实之间的稳健桥梁，为闭环机器人化学提供了噪声鲁棒性引擎，推动了自主材料发现的发展。

Abstract: Resolving transient atomic configurations in non-crystalline or dynamic environments remains a fundamental bottleneck in the physical sciences. While X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) is a premier probe of local structure, inverting spectra into structural descriptors is a notoriously ill-posed problem due to inherent many-to-one mapping. Here, we present the Spectral Pattern Translator (SPT), a physics-informed deep learning framework that establishes a robust bridge between large-scale theoretical datasets and experimental reality. Our strategy exploits the Fourier duality between spectral energy oscillations and spatial scattering paths to overcome the "simulation-to-experiment" gap. By decomposing spectra into frequency domains, SPT effectively isolates robust structural coordination signals from the destabilizing noise inherent in experimental data. Trained on a massive library of diverse atomic environments, this approach achieves state-of-the-art accuracy in resolving continuous phase transitions in battery cathodes and deciphering local order in amorphous materials. With millisecond-scale latency, SPT removes the primary computational barrier to autonomous materials discovery, establishing a robust, noise-resilient engine for closed-loop robotic chemistry.

</details>


### [152] [Electrically and Magnetically Tunable Charge-Density-Wave Transport in Quasi-2D h-BN/1T-TaS2 Thin-Film Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27731)
*Jonas O. Brown,Maedeh Taheri,Nicholas R. Sesing,Tina T. Salguero,Alexander A. Balandin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了垂直电场和磁场对二维材料1T-TaS2中电荷密度波输运的调控，发现了电场对脱钉扎阈值的非单调影响，以及磁场对相变和脱钉扎的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制低维材料中的集体电子相是开发基于电荷密度波技术的关键挑战。研究旨在探索电场和磁场对二维电荷密度波系统的调控能力，为低功耗器件和极端环境电子学提供新策略。

Method: 使用h-BN封装的1T-TaS2薄膜异质结构，采用顶栅和底栅配置，通过施加垂直电场和磁场，研究电荷密度波的输运特性、脱钉扎阈值变化以及相变行为。

Result: 电场调控产生脱钉扎阈值的非单调变化，与一维系统行为不同；垂直磁场能增加脱钉扎阈值电压，并能驱动近公度-非公度电荷密度波相变，实现了对二维电子-晶格凝聚体的磁调控。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了电荷密度波畴动力学机制，证明了电场和磁场联合调控作为工程低功耗耗散器件和极端环境电子学策略的有效性，为二维电荷密度波系统的控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Controlling collective electronic phases in low-dimensional materials is a central challenge for developing technologies based on charge-density waves. Here, we report that perpendicular electric and magnetic fields can be used to tune charge-density-wave transport in the quasi-two-dimensional material 1T-TaS2. Using h-BN-encapsulated thin-film heterostructures with both top-gate and bottom-gate configurations, we find that electrical gating produces a non-monotonic shift in the depinning threshold, a behavior distinct from that of quasi-one-dimensional charge-density-wave systems. We further show that a perpendicular magnetic field increases the threshold voltage for domain depinning and can drive the nearly commensurate-to-incommensurate charge-density-wave phase transition, demonstrating magnetic control over a two-dimensional electron-lattice condensate. The obtained results shed light on mechanisms governing charge-density-wave domain dynamics and reveal combined electrical and magnetic-field control as a strategy for engineering low-power-dissipation devices and electronics for extreme environments.

</details>


### [153] [Shining light on short-range atomic ordering in semiconductors alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27876)
*Anis Attiaoui,Shunda Chen,Joseph C. Woicik,J. Zach Lentz,Liliane M. Vogl,Jarod E. Meyer,Kunal Mukherjee,Andrew Minor,Tianshu Li,Paul C. McIntyre*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 机器学习辅助的EXAFS分析揭示GeSn合金中短程有序度对带隙的显著影响，短程有序度可作为带隙工程的重要设计自由度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半导体性能调控主要依赖平均化学成分、应变状态和长程结构有序度，但理论预测表明短程有序度也能显著影响半导体带隙，需要实验验证这种影响

Method: 开发机器学习辅助的计算指导型EXAFS分析方法，结合光致发光测量，在应变和成分严格控制的GeSn合金单晶纳米结构中量化短程有序度与带隙的关系

Result: EXAFS和PL关联分析建立了GeSn合金带隙与Warren-Cowley短程有序度参数的直接关系，证明通过沉积后退火可在给定平均成分下大幅调控短程有序度

Conclusion: 短程有序度控制是带隙工程的重要设计自由度，与平均成分和应变同等重要，该方法可推广到其他半导体合金系统的原子级有序度测定与调控

Abstract: The functional properties of semiconductors are typically controlled by tuning their average chemical composition and their state of strain, and by controlling their long-range structural order, including the presence of extended defects such as dislocations and grain boundaries. In addition to these approaches, theoretical predictions suggest that short-range order (SRO) of atoms in group-IV semiconductor alloys can modify the bandgap, a defining property of any semiconductor. Herein, a new machine learning enabled, computation-guided methodology for extended X-ray absorption fine structure (EXAFS) analysis of SRO is used to quantify the effects of local atomic order on the band gap of germanium-tin (GeSn) alloy single crystal nanostructures with well-controlled strain and composition. Correlative analysis of EXAFS and photoluminescence (PL) measured from the nanostructures establishes the relationship between bandgap and the Warren-Cowley short-range order (WC-SRO) parameter of the GeSn alloys. It is further demonstrated that, for a given average as-deposited composition, SRO can be tuned readily and over a large range by post-deposition annealing of the alloy crystals. This work indicates that control of SRO can be an important design degree of freedom, along with factors such as average composition and strain, for band engineering, and suggests the potential for atomistic determination and tuning of SRO in other semiconductor alloy systems.

</details>


### [154] [Work-Function-Resolved Imaging of Relaxation Oscillations and Chemical Spillover in CO Oxidation over Platinum Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28354)
*Karel Vařeka,Michal Potoček,Adam Očkovič,Tomáš Šikola,Zhu-Jun Wang,Petr Bábor,Miroslav Kolíbal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过操作扫描电子显微镜与频率调制开尔文探针力显微镜联用，首次实现了铂表面CO氧化反应前沿的工作函数分辨成像，揭示了化学波传播的局部电子机制和时空不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铂表面CO氧化化学波表现出复杂的时空自振荡现象，但在操作条件下驱动其传播的局部电子机制仍不清楚。需要发展能够在操作条件下同时监测表面电子性质和反应状态的技术。

Method: 结合操作扫描电子显微镜与频率调制开尔文探针力显微镜，同时映射二次电子对比度和局部工作函数变化。利用KPFM尖端作为局部传感器，实现反应前沿的工作函数分辨成像。

Result: 首次明确物理分配了CO覆盖态和氧覆盖态。发现化学波溢出过程具有明显的时间不对称性和空间异质性过渡阈值。KPFM识别出氧覆盖的快速开始，随后是逐渐、弥散的弛豫回到CO覆盖态，即使在低压力下也表现出弛豫型振荡特征。

Conclusion: 高分辨率工作函数信号为工作催化剂表面的内部结构和动力学异质性提供了独特见解。相关反应-扩散模拟重现了波形态，证实了工作函数信号在揭示催化剂表面动态行为中的价值。

Abstract: Chemical waves of CO oxidation on platinum surfaces exhibit complex spatio-temporal self-oscillations, yet the local electronic mechanisms driving their propagation remain poorly understood under operando conditions. In this work, we combine operando scanning electron microscopy with frequency-modulated Kelvin probe force microscopy (FM-KPFM) to simultaneously map secondary electron contrast and local work-function variations during CO oxidation on Pt. By utilizing the KPFM tip as a localized sensor, we provide the first work-function-resolved imaging of reaction fronts, enabling an unambiguous physical assignment of CO- and oxygen-covered states. Our results demonstrate that the spillover process of chemical wave-the transition and expansion of adsorbate phases-is characterized by a pronounced temporal asymmetry and spatial heterogeneity transition thresholds. KPFM identifies a rapid onset of oxygen coverage followed by a gradual, diffuse relaxation back to the CO-covered state, indicative of relaxation-type oscillations even at low pressures (10^-2 Pa). Correlative reaction-diffusion simulations reproduce this wave morphology, confirming that the high-resolution work-function signal provides unique insights into the internal structure and kinetic heterogeneity of the working catalyst surface.

</details>


### [155] [On a relationship between grain boundary free energy, grain boundary segregation, and grain boundary diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27878)
*Yuri Mishin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 重新推导并修正Borisov晶界自扩散系数与自由能关系式，明确其物理假设，扩展至杂质扩散及间隙机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Borisov模型虽被广泛用于基于扩散数据预测晶界能，但其物理假设和近似条件未被充分理解，常被误用超出适用范围

Method: 从基本原理重新推导Borisov方程，识别其基本假设，修正错误和不一致之处，并将模型扩展至杂质扩散、间隙机制和间隙-哑铃机制

Result: 明确了Borisov模型的关键假设（活化复合体自由能），提出了验证这些假设的方法，建立了更完善的晶界扩散-能量关系理论框架

Conclusion: 通过系统分析Borisov模型，澄清了其适用范围和限制，为正确使用晶界扩散数据预测晶界能提供了理论指导

Abstract: We present a detailed analysis of the universal relationship between grain boundary (GB) free energy and GB self-diffusion coefficient derived by Borisov et al. (1964). This relationship was expressed by a simple equation that was used in many publications to predict GB energies on the basis of experimental diffusion data. Meanwhile, the physical assumptions and approximations underlying the Borisov model are poorly understood. As a result, the Borisov equation was often used outside its intended limits. Here, we re-derive the Borisov equation from ground up, identifying its underlying assumptions, correcting some errors and inconsistencies, and extending the original model to the case of impurity diffusion and diffusion by the interstitialcy and interstitial-dumbbell mechanisms. The meaning of the key assumption of the Borisov model, related to the free energy of the activated complex, is discussed, and ways to test this assumption are proposed.

</details>


### [156] [Oxygen as a dual function regulator in MoS2 CVD synthesis: enhancing precursor evaporation while modulating reaction kinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28461)
*Keerthana S Kumar,Abhijit Gogoi,Madhavan DK Nampoothiri,Bhavesh Kumar Acharya,Manvi Verma,Ananth Govind Rajan,Akshay Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了氧辅助化学气相沉积中氧的双重作用机制，通过调控硫氧比实现了大面积高质量单层MoS2的可控制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoS2作为有前景的二维过渡金属二硫化物，在可扩展合成和缺陷工程方面面临挑战。虽然氧辅助CVD显示出解决这两个问题的潜力，但氧的作用机制尚不明确，需要深入理解以实现可控合成。

Method: 采用氧剂量实验、密度泛函理论计算、计算流体动力学模拟和从头算分子动力学模拟相结合的方法，系统研究了氧在O-CVD中的双重作用机制。

Result: 研究发现氧具有双重作用：1) AIMD显示氧增加MoO3升华并增强Mo3O9供应；2) DFT表明硫氧化物因其体积大于纯S2而限制反应性MoS6中间体形成。通过调控硫氧比，实现了大面积高质量单层MoS2的制备。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了氧在MoS2合成中的双重作用机制，提出了基于硫氧比调控的动力学相图，为可扩展、缺陷可控的单层MoS2合成提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) is a promising 2D transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) for optoelectronics and quantum technology applications, but faces challenges in scalable synthesis and defect engineering. Oxygen-assisted chemical vapor deposition (O-CVD), which introduces in-situ oxygen during growth, shows excellent potential in resolving both issues at once. Although co-flowing oxygen shows improvement in growth, the underlying mechanistic role of oxygen remains unclear. In this work, a combination of oxygen dosing experiments, density functional theory (DFT) calculations, computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations, and ab initio molecular dynamics (AIMD) simulations, uncover the dual role of oxygen in O-CVD. Firstly, AIMD reveals that oxygen increases MoO3 sublimation and enhances Mo3O9 supply. Concomitantly, DFT reveals that sulphur oxides, due to their bulkier nature than pure S2, limit the formation of reactive MoS6 intermediates. Subsequently, by experimentally varying the oxygen flow-interval, flow-rate, and flow-time, and correlating them with CFD simulations, we decouple oxygen's roles in source-poisoning prevention (i.e. MoO3 evaporation) and growth regulation. We find that maintaining a low sulphur-to-oxygen (S:O2) ratio at the MoO3 boat and substrate during nucleation, and a high S:O2 ratio at the substrate during growth is the key to obtaining large-area high-quality monolayer MoS2, confirmed by our optical measurements. Based on our understanding, we present a kinetic phase diagram for MoS2 synthesis, which will enable controlled oxygen dosing as a tuning parameter for scalable, defect-controlled monolayer MoS2 synthesis.

</details>


### [157] [Coexistence of ferromagnetism and ferroelectricity in the van der Waals multiferroic CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27892)
*Subrata Ghosh,Rosalin Mohanty,Yuwei Sun,Soumi Mondal,Chandan De,Jose G. Jimenez,Weiwei Xie,Cheng Gong,Zhiqiang Mao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 报道了一种新型单相二维范德华多铁材料CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6，该材料同时具有铁磁性和室温铁电性，并表现出磁电耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维范德华多铁材料是下一代多功能器件的有前景平台。虽然人工异质结构可以结合双重铁性序并表现出强磁电耦合，但其性能常受限于界面质量和长期稳定性。实现具有共存铁磁性和铁电性的本征单相材料一直是该领域的长期挑战。

Method: 研究团队开发了单相二维范德华多铁材料CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6。通过铁电隧道结探测其本征铁电性质，测量了磁滞回线和剩余磁化强度来表征铁磁性，并通过磁介电响应分析磁电耦合效应。

Result: CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6表现出室温铁电性，铁电隧道结在295K下显示出107的大隧穿电阻比。材料具有铁磁有序，居里温度为14.6K，并表现出明显的磁滞回线和相对较大的剩余磁化强度。在居里温度以下观察到磁介电响应，证实了铁磁和铁电序之间的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了具有共存铁性序的单相范德华多铁材料，为开发高性能多功能器件提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) multiferroics have emerged as a promising platform for next-generation multifunctional devices. Although recent studies have demonstrated that artificial heterostructures can combine dual ferroic orders and exhibit strong magnetoelectric coupling, their performance is sometimes limited by poor interface quality and inadequate long-term stability. By contrast, the realization of intrinsic single-phase materials with coexisting ferromagnetism and ferroelectricity remains a longstanding challenge in the field. Here we report the realization of a single-phase 2D vdW multiferroic system, CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6, which exhibits both ferromagnetism and room-temperature ferroelectricity. The intrinsic ferroelectric nature of CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6 was probed using ferroelectric tunnel junctions, which exhibit a large tunneling electroresistance with an ON/OFF ratio of 107 at 295 K. CuIn0.2V0.8P2S6 develops ferromagnetic ordering with the Curie temperature (TC) of 14.6 K, as evidenced by pronounced magnetic hysteresis and a relatively large remanent magnetization. Notably, the appearance of a magnetodielectric response below TC is consistent with the anticipated interplay between the ferromagnetic and ferroelectric orders. These results highlight a promising route toward single-phase van der Waals multiferroics with coexisting ferroic orders.

</details>


### [158] [Localization-driven exchange contrast in diffusion exchange spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27907)
*Teddy X Cai,Nathan H Williamson,Peter J Basser*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DEXSY信号分析通常假设高斯扩散和一阶交换动力学，但本文证明在具有反射边界的单维区间中，即使没有弛豫也能产生类似交换对比，这源于边缘增强效应导致的表观交换率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEXSY通常用于探测不同限制域之间的交换，但分析常假设高斯扩散和一阶交换动力学。然而，其他情况也可能产生与混合时间相关的DEXSY信号对比，导致潜在的错误解释。本文旨在探究在最小系统中（无弛豫、反射边界）是否会产生类似交换的对比信号。

Method: 采用数值方法求解离散时空中的Bloch-Torrey方程，使用矩阵算子生成DEXSY信号。考虑扩展场梯度和相同编码的情况，研究具有反射边界的单维区间系统。

Result: 在定位化区域，即使在这个最小系统中也能从DEXSY信号中提取出表观一阶交换速率常数k。测量得到的k近似正比于D/L²，通常k ≈ π²D/L²，其中D是扩散系数，L是域尺寸。这种定位驱动的交换归因于空间磁化模式随混合时间的弛豫。

Conclusion: DEXSY及相关方法（如FEXSY）可能对真正的屏障渗透不具有特异性，因为即使在无交换的最小系统中，边缘增强效应也能产生类似交换的信号对比，这可能导致对实验结果的错误解释。

Abstract: Diffusion exchange spectroscopy (DEXSY) is a method to probe exchange between domains of varying confinement. Analyses of DEXSY signals typically assume Gaussian diffusion within distinct compartments and first-order exchange kinetics between them. Other situations can yield DEXSY signal contrast with respect to mixing time, however, leading to potentially erroneous interpretation. Here, we demonstrate that a one-dimensional compartment with reflecting boundaries and without relaxation can by itself produce such contrast in certain experimental regimes. The origin of this contrast is the diffusive mixing of spin isochromats initially near versus far from either boundary, as the former can be relatively coherent in an effect known as edge enhancement or signal localization. We consider DEXSY signals in the case of extended field gradients and identical encodings. Signals were generated via a numerical approach that solves the Bloch-Torrey equation in discrete space and time using matrix operators. We find that in the localization regime, an apparent first-order rate constant of exchange, $k$, can be extracted from DEXSY signals even in this minimal system. The measured $k$ is approximately proportional to $D/L^2$, where $D$ is the diffusivity and $L$ is the domain size. Typically, $k \approx π^2 D/L^2$. We attribute this localization-driven exchange to the relaxation of spatial magnetization modes with mixing time, noting that $π^2 D/L^2$ is the first non-zero eigenvalue of the Laplacian basis. These results demonstrate that DEXSY and related methods such as filter exchange spectroscopy (FEXSY) may not be specific to genuine barrier permeation.

</details>


### [159] [Raman and Terahertz Spectroscopy of Low-Frequency Chiral Phonons in Amino Acids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27920)
*Rahul Rao,Won Jin Choi,Joseph M. Slocik,Thuc T. Mai,Michael A. Susner,Kelsey A. Collins,Michael J. Newburger,Petr Bouř,Nicholas A. Kotov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过圆偏振低频拉曼光谱和拉曼光学活性光谱，结合太赫兹圆二色性测量，在氨基酸晶体中发现了手性声子的光谱特征，这些特征比指纹区信号更强，为生物分子振动特性提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子是原子扭转和旋转运动的镜像对称振动，在生物分子中对应着涉及数十个原子的低频太赫兹振动，其能量对分子手性和局部原子几何结构敏感。研究旨在揭示手性声子在生物分子中的光谱特征，理解其与圆偏振光的相互作用。

Method: 采用圆偏振低频拉曼光谱和拉曼光学活性光谱（ROA）测量多种氨基酸晶体的不同对映体形式，同时进行互补的太赫兹圆二色性（TCD）测量。结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算对L-丙氨酸进行模式分析。

Result: 在缬氨酸、丙氨酸、酪氨酸和脯氨酸的1-4.5 THz范围内发现两组双符号峰，这些ROA峰比指纹区信号更强。DFT计算表明这些模式对应分子的扭转和剪切运动。ROA和TCD数据高度一致，验证了手性声子的存在。

Conclusion: 圆偏振低频拉曼光谱和太赫兹圆二色性作为互补的振动光谱技术，能够有效识别生物分子中的手性声子，为理解生物分子的振动特性和与圆偏振光的相互作用提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Chiral phonons are mirror-symmetric vibrations that correspond to twisting and rotational motions of atoms. In chiral biomolecules, they correspond to low-energy terahertz (THz)-range vibrations of the molecular segments involving dozens of atoms whose energies are sensitive to the chirality of the molecules and local atomic geometries. Here we present spectral signatures of chiral phonons in circularly polarized low-frequency Raman and Raman optical activity (ROA) spectra from crystals of several amino acids in different enantiomeric forms. Along with complementary THz circular dichroism (TCD) measurements, our ROA data reveal two sets of bisignate peaks in valine, alanine, tyrosine and proline between 1 and 4.5 THz that are more intense than the ROA peaks in the fingerprint region. Density functional theory (DFT) calculations on L-alanine attribute these modes to twisting and shearing molecular motions. The strong agreement between the ROA and TCD data demonstrates the power of these complementary vibrational spectroscopy techniques to identify chiral phonons in biomolecules, and offers new insights into their vibrational properties and interactions with circularly polarized light.

</details>


### [160] [Graphitic-C3N4/TiO2(B) S-scheme Heterojunctions for Efficient Photocatalytic H2 Production and Organic Pollution Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27989)
*Xiaoyi Zhou,Min Zhang,Qiushi Wang,Shiwen Du,Xuedong Jing,Zhenyi Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 构建TiO2(B)/g-C3N4 S型异质结光催化剂，实现宽光谱吸收与高效电荷分离，同时进行光催化产氢和有机废水处理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体光催化剂在环境修复和能源转换中需要同时具备宽光谱吸收和强氧化还原能力，但单一材料难以兼顾这些特性

Method: 通过耦合TiO2(B)纳米棒与g-C3N4纳米片构建S型异质结光催化剂，利用其匹配的能带结构扩展光吸收范围并促进电荷分离

Result: 40 wt% g-C3N4/TiO2(B)异质结在模拟太阳光下产氢速率达1.98 mmol g-1 h-1，比纯组分分别提高1.5和2.0倍；在阿莫西林废水中90分钟内降解98.2%污染物并产氢20.70 μmol g-1

Conclusion: S型异质结光催化剂在光催化产氢与有机废水协同处理方面展现出巨大潜力，为环境修复和能源转换提供了新策略

Abstract: Achieving both broad solar-spectrum absorption and strong redox capability is critical for semiconductor photocatalysts in environmental remediation and energy conversion. Herein, an S-scheme heterojunction photocatalyst is constructed by coupling TiO2(B) nanorods with g-C3N4 nanosheets. Its well-matched band structure extends light absorption from the UV to the visible region and enables efficient charge separation. Under simulated sunlight irradiation, the 40 wt% g-C3N4/TiO2(B) heterojunction delivers a H2 evolution rate of 1.98 mmol g-1 h-1 for water reduction with methanol as the sacrificial agent, which is 1.5 and 2.0 times higher than those of pure g-C3N4 and TiO2(B), respectively. When exposed to amoxicillin wastewater instead of methanol solution, the heterojunction degrades 98.2% of amoxicillin and produces 20.70 umol g-1 of H2 within 90 min. Moreover, the heterojunction shows excellent photodegradation activity toward various organic antibiotics and dyes, owing to the S-scheme charge separation mechanism. This work highlights the promising potential of S-scheme heterojunctions for photocatalytic H2 production coupled with organic wastewater treatment.

</details>


### [161] [A Comparative Study of Molecular Dynamics Approaches for Simulating Ionic Conductivity in Solid Lithium Electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28012)
*Dounia Shaaban Kabakibo,Félix Therrien,Yoshua Bengio,Michel Côté,Hongyu Guo,Homin Shin,Alex Hernandez-Garcia*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 比较DFT和MACE机器学习势在21种锂固体电解质离子电导率预测中的性能，发现两者精度相当但MACE计算速度快350倍以上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测离子电导率对于设计下一代电池中的高性能固态电解质至关重要，需要评估不同计算方法在预测固体电解质离子电导率方面的性能

Method: 通过分子动力学模拟计算21种锂固体电解质的离子电导率，对比密度泛函理论和通用机器学习原子间势（MACE基础模型）两种驱动方法

Result: DFT和MACE在预测离子电导率方面表现出相当的性能，但MACE在单个GPU上的计算速度比DFT在64个CPU节点上快350倍以上

Conclusion: MACE机器学习势在保持与DFT相当精度的同时，大幅提高了计算效率，为系统比较其他通用机器学习势和微调模型提供了框架

Abstract: Accurate prediction of ionic conductivity is critical for the design of high-performance solid-state electrolytes in next-generation batteries. We benchmark molecular dynamics (MD) approaches for computing ionic conductivity in 21 lithium solid electrolytes for which experimental ionic conductivity has been previously reported in the literature. In particular, we compare simulations driven by density functional theory (DFT) and by universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs), namely a MACE foundation model. We find comparable performance between DFT and MACE, despite MACE on one GPU more than 350 times faster than DFT on a 64-CPU node. The framework developed here is designed to enable systematic comparisons with additional uMLIPs and fine-tuned models in future work.

</details>


### [162] [Self-Limiting Mechanism of Anti-Stokes Optical Cooling in Diamond NV Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28017)
*Haruki Manaka,Yasuhiro Yamada*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验和数值模拟研究了金刚石氮空位中心的反斯托克斯光学冷却，发现低于零声子线激发会引发电荷态转换，从而抑制冷却通道，揭示了电荷态转换是缺陷基光学制冷的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究金刚石氮空位中心的反斯托克斯光学冷却机制，探索其在光学制冷领域的应用潜力，并识别影响冷却效率的关键因素。

Method: 采用光致发光激发光谱测量低于零声子线激发下的声子辅助反斯托克斯发射，通过时间分辨光致发光测量分析有效PL寿命随激发密度的变化，使用最小速率方程模型拟合纳秒和毫秒级PL动力学，提取有效光泵浦和电荷转换速率，进行定量冷却性能模拟。

Result: 实验揭示了低于零声子线激发会驱动NV-和NV0之间的光致电荷态转换，从而抑制NV-介导的冷却通道；时间分辨PL测量显示有效PL寿命随激发密度增加而增加，反映了NV0贡献的增加；模拟预测了反斯托克斯冷却的自限制行为，并明确了维持净冷却的激发条件；估算的每个NV中心的冷却功率在微观尺度上与半导体量子点和稀土光学冷却器相当。

Conclusion: 电荷态转换是缺陷基光学制冷的关键瓶颈，限制了金刚石氮空位中心的反斯托克斯冷却效率，这一发现为优化光学制冷材料设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Anti-Stokes optical cooling in diamond nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers is experimentally and numerically investigated. Photoluminescence-excitation spectroscopy reveals pronounced phonon-assisted anti-Stokes emission under excitation below the zero-phonon line (ZPL). However, the below-ZPL excitation drives photoinduced charge-state conversion between negatively-charged NV- and neutral NV0, thereby suppressing the NV- mediated cooling channel. Time-resolved photoluminescence (PL) measurements reveal an increase in the effective PL lifetime with excitation density, reflecting an increasing NV0 contribution. By fitting nanosecond and millisecond PL dynamics with a minimal rate-equation model, we extract effective optical pumping and charge-conversion rates, which enables us to quantitatively simulate the cooling performance. The simulations predict a self-limiting behavior of anti-Stokes cooling and clarify the excitation conditions under which net cooling can be sustained within this effective model. The estimated cooling power per NV center is comparable, on a microscopic basis, to values discussed for semiconductor quantum dots and rare-earth optical coolers. These results identify charge-state conversion as a key bottleneck for defect-based optical refrigeration.

</details>


### [163] [Fractional Modeling of Thermoelastic Fracture Behavior in a Cracked PZT-4 Strip under Transient Thermal Loading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28025)
*Diksha,Soniya Chaudhary,Pawan Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究横观各向同性压电条带中含垂直绝缘裂纹在瞬态热冲击和预存应力场下的热弹性断裂响应，采用广义分数阶热传导Ezzat模型，分析分数阶、热松弛时间、预应力和几何参数对温度分布、热应力场及应力强度因子的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究压电陶瓷在严酷热环境下的热弹性断裂行为，传统傅里叶热传导模型无法准确描述热波传播和记忆效应，需要采用分数阶热传导理论来更精确地分析智能结构在瞬态热冲击下的可靠性。

Method: 基于广义分数阶热传导Ezzat模型建立混合边值问题，采用拉普拉斯变换获得变换域的温度和耦合场解，使用Lobatto-Chebyshev配置法求解奇异积分方程组确定裂纹尖端位移间断和热应力强度因子，通过Stehfest算法数值反演拉普拉斯变换获得时域瞬态响应。

Result: 数值结果表明分数阶、热松弛时间、预应力和几何参数对温度分布、热应力场和应力强度因子有显著影响，与经典傅里叶预测存在明显偏差，揭示了分数阶热传导相关的波动热行为和固有记忆效应。

Conclusion: 该研究为压电陶瓷热弹性断裂分析建立了统一框架，揭示了分数阶热传导模型在描述瞬态热冲击响应中的重要性，为在严酷热条件下工作的智能结构设计和可靠性评估提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This paper investigates the thermoelastic fracture response of a transversely isotropic piezoelectric strip containing a vertical insulated crack under transient thermal shock loading and pre-existing stress fields. The analysis is conducted within the framework of generalized fractional heat conduction using the Ezzat model, which incorporates thermal relaxation and memory-dependent effects. The problem is formulated as a mixed boundary value problem governed by fractional thermoelastic equations. The Laplace transform technique is employed to obtain temperature and coupled fields in the transform domain. The resulting system of singular integral equations is solved using the Lobatto-Chebyshev collocation method to determine the displacement discontinuity and the associated thermal stress intensity factors at the crack tips. The transient response in the time domain is recovered through numerical inversion of the Laplace transform using the Stehfest algorithm. Numerical results for PZT-4 are presented to examine the influence of fractional order, thermal relaxation time, pre-existing stresses, and geometric parameters on temperature distribution, thermoelastic stress fields, and stress intensity factors. The results demonstrate significant deviations from classical Fourier predictions, revealing wave-like thermal behavior and inherent memory effects associated with fractional heat conduction. The present formulation establishes a unified framework for the analysis of thermoelastic fracture in piezoelectric ceramics and provides insights into the design and reliability of smart structures operating under severe thermal conditions.

</details>


### [164] [Unconventional views on orbitronics supported by experimental results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28075)
*Melissa Yactayo,A. Pezo,J. L. Ampuero,M. Tian,L. Badie,J. Quispe-Marcatoma,C. V. Landauro,Y. Xu,Sébastien Petit-Watelot,Michel Hehn,A. Fert,J. -C. Rojas-Sánchez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了轨道电子学中长期程轨道电流传输的假设，通过实验证明轨道极化和轨道-自旋转换具有局域特性，而非长程传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道电子学假设存在类似自旋电流的长程轨道电流传输，但近期理论和实验对此提出质疑，认为轨道极化和轨道-自旋转换具有局域特性。本研究旨在通过实验验证轨道电流传输的本质。

Method: 研究采用Ni/(Pt)Ti/Au异质结构，通过铁磁共振和热梯度产生角动量，测量产生的电荷电流。通过改变Ti层厚度（最高达60nm）来检验轨道电流传输特性。

Result: 观测到的电荷电流在Ti厚度高达60nm时保持不变，这与Ti中轨道传输的预期不符。电流大小取决于Ti的两个界面，表明自旋在界面之间传输，并在界面处发生局域的轨道-自旋相互转换。

Conclusion: 实验结果不支持长程轨道电流传输模型，而是支持自旋介导的传输机制，其中轨道极化和轨道-自旋转换是局域过程，发生在界面处。

Abstract: Emerging orbitronics assumes long-range orbital current transport, analogous to spin currents. However, recent theory and experiments challenge this view, showing rather local characters for orbital polarization and orbit-spin conversions. We study angular momentum generated by ferromagnetic resonance and thermal gradients in Ni/(Pt)Ti/Au heterostructures. The observed charge current produced is independent of Ti thickness up to 60 nm, incompatible with orbital transport in Ti. Instead, its magnitude depends on both Ti interfaces, evidencing spin-mediated transport in between after and before local orbit-spin interconversions.

</details>


### [165] [Quantification of magnetic interactions in van der Waals heterostructures using Lorentz transmission electron microscopy and electron holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28136)
*Joachim Dahl Thomsen,Qianqian Lan,Nikolai S. Kiselev,Eva Duft,Arslan Rehmat,Zdeněk Sofer,Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 使用截面洛伦兹透射电镜和电子全息术量化Fe₃GeTe₂/石墨/FGT异质结构中的层间磁耦合，确定偶极耦合长度尺度为34±4 nm，表面效应导致磁矩倾斜，畴壁宽度约9 nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面测量难以分辨堆叠层中磁织构的耦合，因为磁信号在样品厚度上积分。需要量化vdW磁体异质结构中的层间相互作用，为器件设计提供指导。

Method: 采用截面洛伦兹透射电子显微镜和电子全息术，对Fe₃GeTe₂/石墨/FGT异质结构进行测量，重建层内和层间的局部磁场分布。结合微磁模拟验证观察到的磁织构。

Result: 1. 畴对齐随FGT间距增加而减弱，偶极耦合长度尺度λ=34±4 nm；2. 该长度尺度对应层间磁场相对于块体FGT降低约50%；3. 表面效应导致磁矩在表面附近~100 nm范围内偏离易轴；4. 畴壁宽度约9 nm；5. 微磁模拟无需Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用即可重现观察到的织构。

Conclusion: 该研究量化了堆叠vdW磁体的内部和杂散场，为需要可控磁织构耦合的器件设计提供了关键参数和指导，特别是偶极耦合长度尺度对器件间距设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: Magnetic van der Waals (vdW) materials are promising for memory and logic applications because of their highly tunable magnetic properties and compatibility with vdW heterostructure devices. However, in conventional plan-view measurements, coupling between magnetic textures in stacked layers is difficult to resolve because the magnetic signal is integrated over the sample thickness. Here, these interactions are quantified in Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ (FGT)/graphite/FGT heterostructures using cross-sectional Lorentz transmission electron microscopy and electron holography, enabling reconstruction of the local magnetic field within and between the layers. Domain alignment weakens with increasing FGT separation, yielding a dipolar coupling length scale of $λ= 34 \pm 4$ nm for the cross-sectional geometry studied here, corresponding to the average separation at which domain misalignment first emerges. This length scale coincides with an approximately 50\% reduction in the interlayer magnetic field relative to bulk FGT. Surface effects result in canting of the magnetic moments away from the easy axis up to $\sim$100 nm from a surface. Finally, the domain walls are narrow ($\sim$9 nm), while micromagnetic simulations reproduce the observed textures without invoking Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. These results quantify the internal and stray fields in stacked vdW magnets and guide the design of devices that require controllable coupling between magnetic textures.

</details>


### [166] [Hematite Thin Films Grown on Z-Cut and Y-Cut Lithium Niobate Piezoelectric Substrates by Pulsed Laser Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28175)
*Maximilian Mihm,Stephan Glamsch,Christian Holzmann,Matthias Küß,Helmut Karl,Manfred Albrecht*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究展示了在钽酸锂衬底上外延生长赤铁矿薄膜，利用衬底压电特性控制反铁磁奈尔矢量，为压电/交变磁体混合器件开发奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赤铁矿作为新发现的交变磁体材料，具有高奈尔温度、温度依赖的自旋重取向转变和低磁阻尼等优异特性。钽酸锂作为压电衬底能有效激发表面声波，不同切割方向的衬底可控制奈尔矢量取向，为开发高性能自旋电子器件提供平台。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积技术在y-切割和z-切割钽酸锂衬底上外延生长赤铁矿薄膜。利用衬底的不同切割方向控制薄膜的晶体取向和磁畴结构，研究温度依赖的自旋重取向转变对反铁磁奈尔矢量的调控。

Result: 在y-切割钽酸锂上生长的赤铁矿薄膜为单晶单相结构，而在z-切割衬底上则形成两个面内磁畴，相互旋转60°。两种衬底上的薄膜均表现出温度依赖的自旋重取向转变，能够有效控制反铁磁奈尔矢量。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了在压电衬底上高质量赤铁矿薄膜的外延生长，为开发压电/交变磁体混合系统提供了重要基础，有望推动磁子学和自旋电子学领域的高性能器件发展。

Abstract: Altermagnets are a newly identified class of materials that combine advantageous characteristics of both ferro- and antiferromagnets, making them highly promising for spintronic applications. Hematite has recently been identified as an altermagnetic material and exhibits several noteworthy properties, including a high Néel temperature, a temperature dependent spin reorientation transition (SRT) at the Morin temperature ($T_\mathrm{M}$), and low magnetic damping. In this work, we demonstrate the epitaxial growth of hematite thin films on y- and z-cut lithium niobate (LiNbO$_3$) substrates using pulsed laser deposition (PLD). LiNbO$_3$ as piezoelectric substrate is of particular interest as it enables the efficient excitation of surface acoustic waves (SAWs) with interdigital transducers. The different substrate cuts allow for different orientations of the Néel vector. Films grown on y-cut LiNbO3 are single-crystalline and single-phase, while those deposited on z-cut LiNbO$_3$ exhibit two distinct in-plane (ip) domains rotated 60° relative to each other. On both substrates, the hematite thin films exhibit a temperature dependent SRT which allows the antiferromagnetic Néel vector to be controlled. This study paves the way for the development of high-quality piezoelectric/altermagnetic hyprids for magnonics and spintronics.

</details>


### [167] [Maskless Electron Beam-Induced Etching of Diamond in Air: A Secondary Electron-Driven Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28180)
*Duc-Duy Tran,Cedric Mannequin,Fabrice Donatini,Masahiro Sasaki,Etienne Gheeraert*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文报道了一种在空气中直接、无掩模的电子束诱导刻蚀（EBIE）工艺，用于金刚石的高精度图案化，无需光刻或等离子体处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种无掩模、无损伤的纳米加工方法，用于金刚石等化学惰性材料的图案化，克服传统光刻和等离子体处理的局限性。

Method: 通过综合分析扫描电镜环境中的电子-气体、电子-金刚石和气体-表面相互作用，研究低能二次电子驱动气体解离和自由基生成的机制。利用氧和氮基自由基在金刚石表面化学吸附，形成挥发性含碳物种并在电子辐照下脱附，实现可控材料去除。

Result: 刻蚀深度达212 nm，横向分辨率低至200 nm。观察到时间依赖性各向异性，(100)表面转变为(111)面形态，提高了刻蚀产率。刻蚀过程呈现两种不同机制：受气体通量控制的分子限制机制和受电流密度控制的电子限制机制。

Conclusion: 建立了气体环境中EBIE的一般二次电子驱动机制，为金刚石半导体和其他化学惰性材料提供了一种无掩模、无损伤的纳米加工途径。

Abstract: We report a direct, maskless electron beam-induced etching (EBIE) process for diamond in air, enabling high-precision patterning without lithography or plasma processing. Through a comprehensive analysis of electron-gas, electron-diamond, and gas-surface interactions in the SEM environment, we demonstrate that etching is predominantly governed by low-energy secondary electrons, which drive gas dissociation and radical generation. The resulting oxygen- and nitrogen-based radicals chemisorb on the diamond surface, form volatile carbon-containing species, and desorb under continued electron irradiation, enabling controlled material removal. The process exhibits two distinct regimes: a molecule-limited regime governed by gas flux and an electron-limited regime controlled by current density. Etch depths up to 212 nm and lateral resolution down to 200 nm are achieved. Time-dependent anisotropy is observed, with (100) surfaces transitioning to (111)-faceted morphologies, enhancing etch yield. These results establish a general secondary electron-driven mechanism for EBIE in gas environments, providing a maskless, damage-free nanofabrication route for diamond semiconductor and other chemically inert materials.

</details>


### [168] [Grain boundary defects induced Tc increment in MnSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28187)
*Adrian Benedit-Cardenas,Tobias Fox,Stéphanie Bruyère,Christoph Pauly,Flavio Soldera,Sylvie Migot,Frank Mücklich,David Horwat,Alexandre Nominé*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过激光退火控制MnSi薄膜晶体尺寸，将居里温度从30K提升至120K，实现4倍增长，为微型化器件制造提供新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字技术快速发展需要功能性材料进步，但现有铁磁材料多依赖稀缺稀土元素，且微型化趋势对小型化组件需求增加。MnSi作为铁磁材料居里温度较低（30K），需要提升其性能以满足应用需求。

Method: 结合磁控溅射和激光退火两种非平衡技术制备MnSi薄膜。通过调整激光束扫描参数（能量密度、脉冲数、脉冲频率）控制晶体尺寸，实现局部空间可控的结晶过程。

Result: 当晶体尺寸约为20nm时，MnSi薄膜的居里温度提升至120K，相比原始材料提高4倍。通过低能量密度和高脉冲数条件获得最佳性能，实现约100μm的空间分辨率，并展示了结晶区与非结晶区界面。

Conclusion: 激光退火作为一种多功能且有前景的方法，通过精确控制晶体尺寸显著提升MnSi铁磁性能，为微型化器件制造提供了有效途径。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of digital technologies necessitates significant progress in functional materials, which are often derived from scarce elements and involve complex manufacturing processes. Additionally, the trend towards miniaturization in high-tech devices has heightened the demand for extremely small components with tailored functionalities. In the domains of ferromagnetic materials, the market is mostly dominated by rare-earth elements-based structures, which are also limited in abundance. In this work, we focus on the microstructure and properties of MnSi. It is a ferromagnetic material with a relatively low Curie temperature (TC) of 30 K. However, our study demonstrates that Tc can be increased by a factor of 4 through careful control of the crystal size. MnSi thin films were synthesized by combining two non-equilibrium techniques: magnetron sputtering and laser annealing. Laser annealing provoked the crystallinity evolution, by heat accumulation, of the barely crystallized films deposited by magnetron sputtering. The laser beam scanning parameters were adjusted to achieve different fluence values, pulse numbers, and pulse frequencies at each point of the film. Films with a crystal size of around 20 nm exhibited a TC of up to 120 K. These properties were obtained under conditions of low fluence and a high number pulse. Local laser impacts were applied to as-deposited samples, enabling spatially controlled crystallization. The interface between the poorly and well-crystallized regions was showcased using high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HR-TEM). A spatial resolution of approximately 100 μm was achieved. These results demonstrate the strong potential of laser annealing as a versatile and promising approach for the fabrication of miniaturized devices.

</details>


### [169] [Intrinsically ultralow thermal conductivity in all-inorganic superatomic bulk crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28267)
*Mingzhang Yang,Yuxi Wang,Jun Deng,Tianping Ying,Qinghua Zhang,Nianjie Liang,Xiaobing Liu,Bai Song,Jian-gang Guo,Xiaolong Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 超原子化合物Re6Se8Te7和Re6Te15单晶具有极低热导率，室温下分别为0.32和0.53 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，在所有无机块体晶体中处于最低水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超原子化合物由原子簇通过弱化学键交织而成，具有大的非谐振动，是超低热导率材料的理想候选。然而，由于复杂的化学成分和化学键，生长块体超原子单晶具有挑战性，对其本征热性质的研究很少。

Method: 生长高质量的Re6Se8Te7和Re6Te15超原子单晶，测量其热导率，分析Grüneisen参数、平均声速、玻色峰等特征，并使用经典Debye-Callaway模型描述热导率的温度依赖性。

Result: 室温下Re6Se8Te7和Re6Te15的热导率分别为0.32和0.53 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，在所有无机块体晶体中处于最低水平。这主要归因于大的Grüneisen参数(1.93)和低的平均声速(<1482 m/s)。玻色峰的出现证实了无序声子输运的存在。在350 K以上，热导率值接近玻璃态扩散模型的上限。

Conclusion: 超原子化合物因其极低的热导率和独特的结构特性，成为寻找超低热导率和能量管理材料的有前景的家族。

Abstract: Superatomic compounds, composed of atomic clusters interwoven by weak chemical bonds exhibit large anharmonicity vibrations, are excellent candidates for ultralow thermal conductivity (\k{appa}) materials. However, growing bulk superatomic single crystals is challenging due to complex chemical composition and chemical bonds, and studies on their intrinsic thermal property are scarce. Here, we grew high-quality superatomic single crystals of Re6Se8Te7 and Re6Te15, both of which are narrow band gap semiconductors that change into metals under external physical pressure. At room-temperature, the \k{appa} are 0.32 W m-1 K-1 and 0.53 W m-1 K-1 in Re6Se8Te7 and Re6Te15, respectively, ranking among the lowest value reported in all-inorganic bulk crystals. It is mainly attributed to the large Grüneisen parameter (1.93) and low average sound speed (< 1482 m/s), which are due to soft Te7 nets weakly embedded among the rigid Re6Se8 (Re6Te8) quasi-cubic clusters. The appearance of boson peak, i. e., hump of C(T)/T3, verifies the existence of disordered phonon transports. Besides, the temperature dependence of \k{appa} can be described by classic Debye-Callaway model. Notably, above 350 K, the \k{appa} values of Re6Se8Te7 and Re6Te15 are remarkably close to the upper limit derived from glassy-like diffusion model. This finding sets the superatomic compounds as a promising family for searching ultralow-\k{appa} and energy management materials.

</details>


### [170] [Quantitative Analysis of Light Induced Ion Segregation in Mixed-Halide Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28289)
*Petr Machovec,Lukáš Horák,Milan Dopita,Neda Neykova,Lucie Landová,Jakub Holovský,Václav Holý*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过X射线漫散射分析混合卤化物钙钛矿在光照下的卤化物离子分布及弛豫过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合卤化物钙钛矿具有通过化学计量比调节带隙的能力，这对制造多结太阳能电池至关重要。然而，光照下卤化物离子会发生偏析，形成富碘和富溴区域，这会降低潜在太阳能电池的效率。

Method: 引入了一种详细研究混合卤化物钙钛矿在光照期间和之后卤化物离子分布的方法。计算了卤化物偏析产生的应变场，利用获得的原子局部位移计算X射线漫散射。通过拟合在FA0.83Cs0.17Pb(I0.6Br0.4)3多晶薄膜上测量的实验数据，确定了光照下混合卤化物钙钛矿中Br-和I-离子的分布，并追踪了随后在黑暗中的偏析弛豫过程。

Result: 观察到在光照期间，在轻微富碘的体积内形成了高度富溴的区域。成功确定了卤化物离子在光照下的分布，并跟踪了黑暗中的弛豫过程。

Conclusion: 该方法能够详细研究混合卤化物钙钛矿中卤化物离子的分布动态，为理解光照诱导的卤化物偏析及其弛豫行为提供了重要工具，有助于优化混合卤化物钙钛矿太阳能电池的性能。

Abstract: Mixed-halide perovskites (MHPs) offer good band gap tunability by stoichiometry changes, which is an essential property for the creation of multijunction solar cells. However, under illumination, halide ions in MHP segregate and create I- and Br-rich regions, which decreases the efficiency of potential solar cells. In this work, a method for a detailed investigation of the distribution of halide ions within the MHP during and after illumination is introduced. Calculations of the strain field created by the halide segregation were performed, and the obtained local displacement of atoms was used to calculate the x-ray diffuse scattering. By fitting the experimental data measured on the thin polycrystalline layer of FA0.83Cs0.17Pb(I0.6Br0.4)3 the distribution of Br- and I- ions within an illuminated MHP was determined and the subsequent relaxation process of the segregation in the dark was tracked. Creation of highly Br-rich regions within slightly I-rich volume during the illumination was observed.

</details>


### [171] [Role of spatiotemporal nonuniformities in laser-induced magnetization precession damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28364)
*P. I. Gerevenkov,Ia. A. Filatov,L. A. Shelukhin,P. A. Dvortsova,A. M. Kalashnikova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 激光诱导磁化进动测量在铁磁体中常显示阻尼时间在磁场诱导的二阶自旋取向转变附近异常减小，这种行为无法用线性化的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程描述。研究表明这种异常不是材料特性，而是由非均匀激发区域内局部磁化进动的干涉效应引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释铁磁体中激光诱导磁化进动测量中观察到的异常现象：在磁场诱导的二阶自旋取向转变附近，阻尼时间异常减小，这与线性化的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的预测不符。需要理解这种异常行为的物理起源。

Method: 结合泵浦-探测实验、考虑泵浦和探测光斑有限尺寸的解析建模以及微磁模拟。通过分析非均匀激发区域内局部磁化进动的干涉效应，揭示标准宏观自旋方法的局限性。

Result: 研究表明异常阻尼行为不是材料固有特性，而是由非均匀激发区域内局部磁化进动的干涉效应导致。激发区域内磁参数的非均匀弛豫会扭曲测量的进动包络，而偶极场会在其频率中产生时间非单调项。标准宏观自旋方法无法捕捉观察到的动力学行为。

Conclusion: 在临界场附近，激发局域性起着关键作用。观察到的异常阻尼行为源于非均匀激发区域内局部磁化进动的干涉效应，而非材料特性。这强调了在接近临界场的条件下考虑激发空间分布的重要性。

Abstract: Laser-induced magnetization precession measurements in ferromagnets often reveal an anomalous decrease in the damping time near a field-induced second-order spin-orientation transition, a behavior that cannot be described by the linearized Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation. Here we demonstrate that this anomaly is not a material property but results from interference of precessing local magnetizations within the inhomogeneously excited region. By combining pump-probe experiments, analytical modeling that accounts for the finite sizes of the pump and probe spots, and micromagnetic simulations, we show that the standard macrospin approach fails to capture the observed dynamics. The inhomogeneous relaxation of magnetic parameters within the excitation area distorts the measured precession envelope, while dipole fields give rise to a temporally non-monotonic term in its frequency. Our results highlight the critical role of excitation locality in a vicinity of critical fields.

</details>


### [172] [Stable Asymmetric Magnetization Reversal in Epitaxial Co(001)/CoO(001) Bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28424)
*Maik Gaerner,Judith Bünte,Finn Peters,Inga Ennen,Hermann Tetzlaff,Johannes Fiedler,Tomasz Blachowicz,Luana Caron,Andreas Hütten,Andrea Ehrmann,Martin Wortmann*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究外延fcc-Co(001)/CoO(001)薄膜中交换偏置引起的磁滞回线不对称性，发现与多晶体系不同，该不对称性在多次训练循环后保持稳定，且与交换偏置大小直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究铁磁/反铁磁双层系统中交换偏置引起的磁滞回线不对称性，特别是外延单晶体系与多晶体系在不对称性行为上的差异。

Method: 使用分子束外延在MgO(001)衬底上生长外延fcc-Co(001)/CoO(001)薄膜，在不同温度下进行场冷却（沿易轴和难轴方向），通过纵向和横向磁光克尔效应测量磁化反转过程。

Result: 室温测量显示不同磁场方向下通过稳定中间态的磁化反转过程；低于阻塞温度冷却后观察到明显的非对称磁化反转；与多晶双层不同，不对称性在多次训练循环后保持恒定，且不对称性大小与交换偏置大小直接相关。

Conclusion: 外延fcc-Co(001)/CoO(001)薄膜中的非对称磁化反转行为与多晶体系存在显著差异，其不对称性具有稳定性且与交换偏置直接相关，这为理解单晶体系中交换偏置效应提供了新见解。

Abstract: The exchange bias (EB) in ferromagnetic/antiferromagnetic (FM/AFM) bilayer systems causes a shift of the magnetic hysteresis curve after field cooling through the Néel temperature of the AFM. In some cases, this shift is accompanied by an asymmetry between ascending and descending branches. In the past, this asymmetric magnetization reversal has been studied in different bilayer systems, including polycrystalline Co/CoO thin films. Here we investigate the asymmetric magnetization reversal in an epitaxial fcc-Co(001)/CoO(001) thin film grown on MgO(001) by molecular beam epitaxy at varying temperatures up to room temperature after field cooling along the easy and the hard axes. Room temperature measurements of the longitudinal and transverse magneto-optic Kerr effect show different magnetization reversal processes via stable intermediate states for different angles between external magnetic field and magnetic easy axes. Once the sample is cooled below the blocking temperature, a pronounced asymmetric magnetization reversal can be observed. We show that in contrast to polycrystalline bilayers, the loop asymmetry stays constant after multiple training cycles and that the magnitude of the asymmetry is directly correlated with the magnitude of the EB.

</details>


### [173] [Pentagonal PdTe2 Monolayer for Sustainable Solar-driven Hydrogen Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28726)
*Narender Kumar,Shambhu Bhandari,Dario Alfè,Nacir Tit,Ravindra Pandey*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 五边形PdTe2单层是一种可调谐的二维光催化剂，通过拉伸应变工程（+2%和+3%）实现自发水分解，在pH=7时获得20.40%的太阳能-氢转换效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高效、可持续的太阳能驱动制氢催化剂，特别是寻找性能优于现有二维材料的新型光催化剂。

Method: 使用HSE06泛函的密度泛函理论计算，研究拉伸应变（特别是+2%和+3%双轴应变）对五边形PdTe2单层电子结构、能带位置、载流子迁移率和水分解反应路径的影响。

Result: +3%拉伸应变下，价带顶和导带底跨越水氧化还原电位，带隙1.75 eV，高空穴迁移率，在pH=7时获得ηHER=0.70 V和ηOER=0.72 V的过电位，太阳能-氢转换效率达20.40%。

Conclusion: 五边形PdTe2单层通过应变工程成为性能优异的二维光催化剂，其太阳能-氢转换效率超过多种已报道材料，是可持续太阳能制氢的有力候选材料。

Abstract: This investigation demonstrates that the pentagonal PdTe2 (penta-PdTe2) monolayer is a highly tunable two-dimensional (2D) photocatalyst, characterized by the bandgap of 1.75 eV and high hole mobility. Using density functional theory calculations with the HSE06 functional, we show that tensile strain engineering (particularly at +2% and +3%) is essential for enabling spontaneous water splitting. At these strain values, the valence-band maximum and conduction-band maximum straddle the water redox potentials (H+/H2 and O2/H2O) in both acidic (pH=0) and neutral (pH=7) conditions. The monolayer's low hole effective mass facilitates rapid charge extraction, mitigating recombination and driving the oxygen evolution reaction (OER) more effectively than many hexagonal and pentagonal counterparts. The Gibbs free energy (ΔG) pathways indicate that overpotentials for the hydrogen evolution reaction (HER) and OER are highly sensitive to mechanical deformation, specifically biaxial strain, through which +3% tensile strain, yielding an optimized balance of overpotentials of ηHER = 0.70 V and ηOER = 0.72 V at pH=7. Finally, integrating optical absorption with thermodynamic driving forces results in a Solar-to-Hydrogen (STH) efficiency of 20.40% at pH 7. This exceeds the performance of several previously reported 2D catalysts, positioning penta-PdTe2 as a superior candidate for sustainable, solar-driven hydrogen production.

</details>
