# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Tunable anharmonicity in Sn-InAs nanowire transmons beyond the short junction limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26895)
*Amrita Purkayastha,Amritesh Sharma,Param J. Patel,An-Hsi Chen,Connor P. Dempsey,Shreyas Asodekar,Subhayan Sinha,Maxime Tomasian,Mihir Pendharkar,Christopher J. Palmstrøm,Moïra Hocevar,Kun Zuo,Michael Hatridge,Sergey M. Frolov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: Sn-InAs纳米线transmon量子比特中实现了从Ec到小于Ec/10的广泛电调谐非谐性，突破了多通道短结模型预测的Ec/4下限，为量子电路设计提供了新自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究超导-半导体混合约瑟夫森结transmon量子比特的非谐性电调谐特性，探索突破传统理论模型限制的可能性，为量子电路设计提供更灵活的参数调控手段。

Method: 使用Sn-InAs纳米线构建transmon量子比特，通过栅极电压同时调控约瑟夫森能量和弱连接透明度，采用双音微波光谱技术测量非谐性变化范围。

Result: 实验测得非谐性绝对值范围从充电能量Ec到小于Ec/10，突破了多通道短结模型预测的Ec/4下限，在最低非谐性点仍能实现量子比特的相干操作。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了超导-半导体混合transmon量子比特具有广泛电调谐非谐性的潜力，为开发受益于可调非谐性的新型量子电路提供了重要基础。

Abstract: The anharmonicity of a transmon qubit, defined as the difference in energy level spacing, is a key design parameter. In transmons built from hybrid superconductor-semiconductor Josephson elements, the anharmonicity is tunable with gate voltages that control both the Josephson energy and the weak link transparency. In Sn-InAs nanowire transmons, we use two-tone microwave spectroscopy to extract anharmonicity ranging in absolute value from the transmon charging energy $E_c$ to values smaller than $E_c/10$. This behavior contrasts with the predictions of the multi-channel short-junction model, which sets a lower limit on anharmonicity at $E_c/4$. Coherent operation of the qubit is still possible at the point of the lowest anharmonicity. These findings demonstrate the potential of quantum circuits that benefit from widely electrically tunable anharmonicity.

</details>


### [175] [The role of polarization field terms in a model for a cavity quantum material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26917)
*Arwen Lloyd,Adam Stokes,Alessandro Principi,Ahsan Nazir*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文分析了腔量子材料建模中光-物质耦合的处理方法，重点探讨了Peierls替代在不同规范下的有效性及其局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在腔量子材料建模中，需要仔细处理光-物质耦合。通常需要指定由材料波函数构造的矩阵元，但在紧束缚框架中这些参数往往未知。Peierls替代常被用于避免在多中心偶极规范中引入这些额外参数，但该方法的有效性需要系统验证。

Method: 采用规范变换的被动视角推导Peierls规范描述；构建具有两个位点的多带系统玩具模型；在Coulomb规范、偶极规范和Peierls规范下将模型耦合到均匀场；分析不同规范下子系统的相对性及其对物理可观测量和轨道截断性能的影响。

Result: Peierls替代在一维低能有效单带描述中可以成立，但在完整的Peierls规范理论中会遗漏自极化修正和描述带间跃迁所需的直接耦合。Coulomb、偶极和Peierls规范定义了复合系统中光与物质子系统的不同划分方式，这种子系统相对性对物理可观测量和轨道截断性能有重要影响。

Conclusion: Peierls替代在特定条件下可作为有效的低能近似，但在完整的多带理论中存在局限性。不同规范下子系统划分的相对性对腔量子材料的建模和计算有重要启示，需要在选择规范时考虑其对物理结果和数值计算效率的影响。

Abstract: Constructing models for cavity quantum materials requires a careful treatment of the light-matter coupling. In general, one must specify matrix elements constructed from the material wavefunctions, which are often unknown in a tight-binding framework. The Peierls substitution is often used to avoid introducing these additional parameters in the multi-center dipole (or Peiels) gauge, under the assumption that contributions from intraband and interband dipole moments can be neglected in the low-energy theory. We present the derivation of the Peierls gauge description in the passive view of canonical transformations. We construct a toy model for a multi-band system with two sites, which we couple to a uniform field in the Coulomb, dipole, and Peierls gauges. We find that the Peierls substitution can be justified as a low-energy, effectively single-band description in one dimension, but it misses both self-polarization corrections and the direct coupling needed to describe interband transitions in the full Peierls gauge theory. Moreover, the Coulomb, dipole, and Peierls gauges define distinct partitions of the composite system into the light and matter subsystems. We illustrate the implications of this subsystem relativity for physical observables and on the performance of orbital truncations in each gauge.

</details>


### [176] [Frustrated out-of-plane Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and the onset of atomic-scale 3$q$ magnetic textures in 2D Fe$_{3}$GeXTe (X = Te, Se, S) monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26951)
*Caglayan Rabia,Desplat Louise,Nikolaev Sergey,Ibrahim Fatima,Li Jing,Mogulkoc Yesim,Mogulkoc Aybey,Chshiev Mairbek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该理论研究二维Fe₃GeXTe（X=Te, Se, S）单层中面内和面外Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）对磁基态的影响，发现DMI可诱导原子尺度的3q磁结构和类纳米斯格明子晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维磁性材料中DMI对磁基态的影响，特别是Janus结构（X=Se, S）具有非零面内DMI，探索通过应变或电场调控DMI来设计原子尺度磁结构。

Method: 采用基于第一性原理计算参数化的扩展海森堡哈密顿量进行原子自旋模拟，研究DMI强度增加时系统基态的演化。

Result: 所有系统的本征DMI太弱无法稳定非共线态，但受挫的面外DMI倾向于在布里渊区边缘形成原子尺度的3q磁结构；DMI放大3倍即可稳定非平面3q态，更大DMI则稳定类似纳米斯格明子晶格的原子尺度结构。

Conclusion: 二维Fe₃GeXTe单层中DMI可调控产生原子尺度的复杂磁结构，为通过应变或电场调控DMI设计新型二维磁体中的拓扑磁结构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We theoretically study the effect of in- and out-of-plane Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) on the magnetic ground states of two-dimensional (2D) Fe$_3$GeXTe (X=Te, Se, S) monolayers, where X=Se, S correspond to antisymmetric Janus structures with nonvanishing in-plane DMI. We perform atomistic spin simulations with the extended Heisenberg Hamiltonian parametrized by first principles calculations. While we find that the base DMI in all systems is too weak to stabilize noncollinear states, we show how the frustrated out-of-plane DMI tends to favor atomic-scale $3q$ magnetic textures at the edge of the Brillouin zone. Owing to the ability to tune the DMI in 2D magnets via applied strain or electric field, we study the evolution of the systems' ground state with increasing DMI amplitude. We find that nonplanar $3q$ states are favored under scaling factors as low as 3, while larger DMI tends to stabilize states reminiscent of nanoskyrmion lattices at the atomic-scale.

</details>


### [177] [Spin--valley--resolved tunneling through magnetic barriers in WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27384)
*Rachid El Aitouni,Clarence Cortes,David Laroze,Ahmed Jellal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究磁场对WS₂电子性质的影响，重点关注自旋轨道耦合、自旋与谷极化以及电导特性，发现K谷传输概率高于K'谷，存在克莱因隧穿效应，磁场通过塞曼效应调控费米子能级和输运通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁场如何调控WS₂中的电子输运特性，特别是自旋轨道耦合、自旋与谷极化对电导的影响，旨在为谷电子学和谷滤波器件的信息存储应用提供理论基础和控制手段。

Method: 通过解析求解本征值方程，利用连续性方程基于电流密度确定传输概率，采用Büttiker公式计算电导，并进行数值计算分析磁场对传输特性的影响。

Result: 数值结果表明：K谷传输概率高于K'谷；两个谷均观察到明显的克莱因隧穿效应；磁场通过塞曼效应改变费米子能级，影响电导并实现费米子在势垒内的局域化；磁场强度增加可调控自旋和谷极化，决定哪些通道能够穿越势垒。

Conclusion: 磁场为控制WS₂中费米子输运提供了有效工具，通过调控自旋和谷极化特性，为谷电子学和谷滤波器件在信息存储等领域的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the influence of a magnetic field on the electronic properties of WS$e_2$ with a focus on spin-orbit coupling, spin and valley polarization, and conductance. We solve the eigenvalue equation analytically and use the continuity equation to determine the transmission probability based on current densities. We calculate the conductance using Büttiker formula. Our numerical results indicate that transmission through the $K$ valley is more likely than through the $K'$ valley. For both valleys, the Klein tunneling effect is clearly observed. The conductance is affected by an increase in the magnetic field because it alters the energy levels of fermions via the Zeeman effect. These modifications enable the confinement of fermions within the barrier. Spin and valley polarization are also influenced by the magnetic field. As the field intensity increases, it steers the fermions and determines which channel can cross the barrier. This adds another tool of controlling fermions, paving the way for relevant applications in valleytronics and valley filtering for information storage.

</details>


### [178] [Nonreciprocal transverse currents in Rashba metal junctions under out-of-plane Zeeman fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27642)
*Megha Bera,Bijay Kumar Sahoo,Abhiram Soori*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究正常金属与Rashba金属结在塞曼场作用下的电荷输运，发现该结表现出固有的非互易横向电导，无需面内磁场或铁磁接触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Rashba系统中非互易横向电荷输运的新机制，避免传统方法需要面内磁场或铁磁接触的限制，为半导体异质结构中的实验提供理论基础。

Method: 研究正常金属与Rashba金属结在垂直塞曼场作用下的电荷输运，分析哈密顿量对称性破缺、横向动量贡献抵消机制，以及衰减模式在结区的关键作用。

Result: 发现结区存在有限横向电导，具有非互易性（依赖偏压方向），在塞曼场能量尺度处出现峰值，空间分布由贡献模式性质决定，吸引势垒强度下存在增强电导的束缚态。

Conclusion: 揭示了Rashba系统中无需面内磁场或铁磁接触的非互易横向电荷输运机制，该效应源于塞曼场破缺哈密顿量对称性及衰减模式的有限自旋极化，可在半导体异质结构中实验观测。

Abstract: We study charge transport across a junction between a normal metal and a Rashba metal in the presence of a Zeeman field applied to the spin--orbit coupled region. While an out-of-plane Zeeman field does not generate a transverse response in a homogeneous Rashba system, we show that such a junction exhibits a finite transverse conductivity that is inherently nonreciprocal, i.e., it depends on the direction of the applied bias. We demonstrate that this effect originates from the breaking of the $k_y \to -k_y$ symmetry of the Hamiltonian in the presence of the Zeeman field, which prevents cancellation of transverse current contributions from opposite transverse momenta. We further show that evanescent modes in the spin--orbit coupled region play a crucial role by carrying a finite spin polarization that gives rise to a transverse current localized near the junction. The transverse conductivity exhibits a peak at an energy scale set by the Zeeman field, displays distinct behavior for opposite bias directions, and shows spatial dependence governed by the nature of the contributing modes. We also identify bound states at the junction for attractive barrier strengths, which enhance conductance when their energies lie near the transport window. Our results reveal a mechanism for nonreciprocal transverse charge transport in Rashba systems without requiring in-plane magnetic fields or ferromagnetic contacts, and should be experimentally accessible in semiconductor heterostructures.

</details>


### [179] [Benzo-bis(imidazole) self-assembled monolayers molecular junctions in meta or para conformation: effects of protonation on the electrical and thermal conductances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27760)
*Sergio Gonzalez-Casal,Simon Pascal,Olivier Siri,Dominique Vuillaume*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究苯并双咪唑分子自组装单分子层结的热导和电导特性，发现间位连接相比对位连接的热导更低，且间位连接的热导对质子化敏感，而电导则受量子干涉效应和分子排列影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分子结中连接位置（间位vs对位）和质子化状态对热导和电导的影响，验证理论预测的声子干涉效应和量子干涉效应，并探索分子排列对界面热/电传输的调控机制。

Method: 使用自组装单分子层技术构建苯并双咪唑分子结，比较无侧基和带有苯胺侧基的分子，分别研究间位和对位硫醇锚定连接的热导和电导特性，并通过质子化/去质子化过程进行可逆调控。

Result: 间位连接的热导（16-29 nW/K）比对位连接（37-40 nW/K）低约50%，验证了声子干涉效应；质子化使间位连接热导增加50%，而对位连接不敏感；电导方面，间位连接因量子干涉效应比对位连接低，质子化进一步降低电导，但分子轨道能级不变。

Conclusion: 分子连接位置通过声子干涉效应显著影响热导，而质子化通过改变分子排列调控界面热耦合；电导受量子干涉效应和分子排列共同影响，为分子热电器件设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We report the thermal conductances of molecular junctions made of self-assembled monolayers of benzo-bis(imidazole) molecules, without side groups or functionalized with two phenylamine side groups. In the two cases, when the molecules are connected to the electrodes by thiol anchoring groups in the meta-position, the thermal conductance is decreased compared to the same molecules connected in the para-position (ca. 16-29 nW/K and ca. 37-40 nW/K, respectively) in agreement with the theoretically predicted phonon interference effect in molecular junctions. Upon protonation, the thermal conductances of the meta-connected molecular junction increase by about 50% (reversible behavior upon deprotonation). The fact that only the thermal conductance of the meta-connected molecular junction is sensitive to the protonation/deprotonation is tentatively related to modifications of the structural organization of the molecules in the monolayer, which modifies the thermal conductance at the molecule/electrode interfaces. The electrical conductance is lower for the meta-connected molecule than for the para-connected one, due to destructive quantum interferences, as expected and reported for other molecular junctions. The conductance further decreases (reversibly) upon protonation. The energy position of the molecular orbital involved in the electron transport is not modified by the protonation and the decrease in current is related to changes in the molecule organization in the monolayer, which modulate the electronic coupling energy at the molecule/electrode interfaces.

</details>


### [180] [Ground-State Selection by Pure Energy Relaxation in Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27834)
*D. A. Saltykova,A. V. Yulin,I. A. Shelykh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究耗散性激子极化激元凝聚体在纯能量弛豫下的非平衡模式选择，发现能量弛豫会破坏激发态凝聚，促进基态选择


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在纯能量弛豫存在下，通过激子库非相干泵浦的耗散性激子极化激元凝聚体中的非平衡模式选择问题

Method: 研究受限系统中存在纯能量弛豫时，激子极化激元凝聚体的模式演化，分析从涡旋凝聚态到旋转混合态再到基态凝聚态的转变过程

Result: 随着泵浦强度增加，渐近态从涡旋凝聚态演化为旋转混合态，最终转变为基态凝聚态；纯能量弛豫会破坏激发态凝聚，促进基态选择

Conclusion: 纯能量弛豫会定性改变凝聚场景，使激发态凝聚变得不稳定，促进基态选择，这对非平衡凝聚体的模式控制具有重要意义

Abstract: We study nonequilibrium mode selection in dissipative exciton-polariton condensates incoherently pumped through an excitonic reservoir in the presence of pure energy relaxation. For a confined system in which a vortex mode is selected at threshold, we show that energy relaxation qualitatively changes the condensation scenario: as the pump increases, the asymptotic state evolves from a vortex condensate to a rotating mixed state and then to a ground-state condensate. Pure energy relaxation thus destabilizes condensation into excited states and promotes ground-state selection.

</details>


### [181] [Frequency Comb of Electric-Polarization Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27947)
*Xiyin Ye,Tao Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出在铁电材料中利用电极化波的非线性动力学实现太赫兹频率梳，其效率与铁子模式携带的静态电极化成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频率梳在光学和微波频段已广泛应用，但在太赫兹频段实现频率梳具有重要价值。论文旨在利用铁电材料中电极化波（铁子）的非线性动力学特性，在太赫兹频段实现频率梳，为直接观测和应用铁子的本征特性提供新途径。

Method: 通过铁电材料中电极化波（铁子）的非线性动力学实现太赫兹频率梳。利用铁子模式携带的静态电极化特性，构建频率梳系统。

Result: 提出的太赫兹频率梳效率与铁子模式携带的静态电极化成正比。这一特性使得该频率梳能够直接反映铁子的本征特性，为观测和应用铁子提供了新的机会。

Conclusion: 在铁电材料中利用电极化波非线性动力学实现太赫兹频率梳是可行的，其效率与静态电极化直接相关。这一方法为太赫兹频率梳的实现提供了新途径，同时为铁子的直接观测和应用开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: Frequency combs are a spectrum of equally spaced frequency components with very high time-frequency accuracy, which have been widely used in the optical and microwave frequency ranges. We propose the realization of a frequency comb operating at the terahertz regime in terms of the nonlinear dynamics of electric-polarization waves, or ferrons as their quanta, in the ferroelectric materials. The efficiency of the frequency comb of the electric-polarization waves is exactly proportional to the static electric polarization carried by the ferron modes, which thereby offers new opportunities for the direct observation and application of the intrinsic properties of ferrons.

</details>


### [182] [Ferromagnetic resonance modulation in topological materials with bulk--boundary coexistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28056)
*Shun Muto,Yuya Ominato,Takeo Kato,Mamoru Matsuo,Ai Yamakage*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 将铁磁共振调制理论扩展到体态和边界态共存的拓扑材料系统，应用于d波超导体(110)表面研究吉尔伯特阻尼增强，发现零能附近的边缘-边缘激发峰和能隙处的边缘-体激发峰等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拓扑材料中体态和边界态共存系统的铁磁共振响应，特别是d波超导体表面零能安德列夫束缚态与体态共存时对吉尔伯特阻尼的影响。

Method: 扩展铁磁共振调制理论框架，应用于d波超导体(110)表面系统，分析表面零能安德列夫束缚态（一维边界态）与体态共存情况下的激发特性。

Result: 发现两个特征峰：零能附近的边缘-边缘激发峰和超导能隙处的边缘-体激发峰；观察到低温区幂律衰减和中间温度区指数衰减；证明体态和边界态对铁磁共振响应有可比贡献。

Conclusion: 体态和边界态共存系统表现出独特的铁磁共振特征，理论框架为分析拓扑材料提供了广泛适用的工具。

Abstract: We extend ferromagnetic resonance (FMR) modulation theory to describe systems in which bulk and boundary states of topological materials coexist, with both appearing at the same energy. As an application of the formulation, we investigate the enhancement of the Gilbert damping constant on the $(110)$ surface of a $d$-wave superconductor where nodal quasiparticles coexist with edge states, which are one-dimensional boundary states, known as surface zero-energy Andreev bound states. We find two characteristic features: a pronounced edge-to-edge excitation peak near zero energy, and an additional edge-to-bulk excitation peak at the superconducting gap energy. We also observe power-law decay at low temperatures and exponential decay at intermediate temperatures in the low-energy regime. These features demonstrate the comparable contributions of the bulk and boundary states to the FMR response. Our theory provides a broadly applicable framework for the analysis of topological materials.

</details>


### [183] [Dynamical diffraction formalism for imaging time-dependent diffuse scattering from coherent phonons with Dark-Field X-ray Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28071)
*Darshan Chalise,Brinthan Kanesalingam,Dorian P. Luccioni,Daniel Schick,Aaron M. Lindenberg,Leora Dresselhaus-Marais*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用暗场X射线显微镜（DFXM）通过时间依赖性强度振荡边带来研究GHz频率相干声子动力学的新方法，克服了传统布拉格峰追踪方法在频率分辨率上的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干声子阻尼决定了声学谐振器的品质因数上限，在先进通信和量子信息技术中至关重要。然而，使用传统表面技术探测GHz频率范围内的声子衰减仍然具有挑战性。虽然DFXM提供了通过深度、非破坏性、全场成像应变场和位错的能力，但之前基于布拉格峰位移的方法在可研究的最高声子频率上存在空间分辨率限制。

Method: 采用Takagi-Taupin动力学衍射理论，建立了一种通过时间依赖性强度振荡边带来研究相干声子动力学的方法。该方法允许同时研究实空间和倒易空间中的相干声子动力学，克服了布拉格峰追踪方法在实空间分辨率上施加的频率分辨率限制。论文还提出了优化激发带宽和倒易空间选择性的实验策略。

Result: 建立了研究相干声子动力学时可实现的空间和倒易空间分辨率，并评估了观察长寿命强度振荡的条件。该方法能够定量、频率分辨地测量体晶材料中的声子衰减和声子-缺陷相互作用。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的形式化方法使DFXM实验设计能够用于定量、频率分辨的声子衰减测量，为研究体晶材料中的声子动力学和声子-缺陷相互作用提供了新的实验框架，克服了传统方法的频率分辨率限制。

Abstract: Coherent acoustic phonons, whose damping sets the upper bound of quality factors in acoustic resonators, play a critical role in advanced telecommunication and quantum information technologies. Yet, probing their decay in the GHz regime remains challenging using conventional surface-based techniques. Dark-field X-ray microscopy (DFXM) offers a solution by enabling through-depth, non-destructive and full-field imaging of strain fields and dislocations inside bulk materials with high spatial and angular resolution. We previously used kinematic diffraction theory to describe DFXM signals based on how the Bragg peak shifts due to the strain wave, allowing us to reconstruct the frequency spectrum of coherent phonons as a function of depth through the sample. The approach of tracking the Bragg peak shifts to study phonon dynamics, however, places an upper-bound to the highest phonon frequency that can be studied, determined by the spatial resolution of the measurement. In this work, we discuss how coherent phonon dynamics can be studied with DFXM from time-dependent intensity oscillation sidebands. This approach simultaneously allows studying coherent phonon dynamics in real and reciprocal space, overcoming frequency resolution limits imposed by the real-space resolution of Bragg-peak tracking. Using Takagi-Taupin dynamical diffraction formalism, we establish the spatial and reciprocal space resolution achievable for studying the coherent phonon dynamics and evaluate conditions for observing long-lived intensity oscillations. We close by proposing experimental strategies to optimize excitation bandwidths and reciprocal-space selectivity. The formalism in the paper enables the design of DFXM experiments for quantitative, frequency-resolved measurements of acoustic phonon decay and phonon-defect interactions in bulk crystalline materials.

</details>


### [184] [Emergent Magnetic Monopole in Artificial Polariton Spin Ice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28384)
*Junhui Cao,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于极化激元的人工自旋冰实现方案，利用圆偏振作为伊辛自由度，通过损耗性顶点模式动态实施冰规则约束，在非平衡条件下实现规范电荷的创建、操控和观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工自旋冰为涌现规范场和磁单极子激发提供了灵活平台，但传统实现方式在操控和观测方面存在限制。本研究旨在开发一种基于极化激元的驱动-耗散实现方案，利用光子平台的优势实现更可控的规范电荷操作和实时观测。

Method: 提出极化激元人工自旋冰架构：1）将每个连接模式的圆偏振作为伊辛自由度；2）引入辅助损耗性顶点模式动态实施局部冰规则约束；3）通过顶点模式的绝热消除获得有效的自旋冰惩罚项；4）在扩展自旋冰几何中，通过偏振分辨实空间成像直接重建顶点电荷及其动力学。

Result: 1）局部偏振翻转产生单极子-反单极子缺陷对；2）连续翻转使缺陷在晶格中传输并定义狄拉克弦；3）在稳态中实现两进两出流形；4）建立了极化激元晶格作为创建、操控和观测非平衡自旋冰系统中涌现规范电荷的可控光子平台。

Conclusion: 极化激元晶格为研究非平衡自旋冰系统中的涌现规范电荷提供了高度可控的光子平台，能够实现缺陷的生成、传输和直接观测，为探索非平衡拓扑物态开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Artificial spin ice provides a versatile setting for emergent gauge fields and magnetic monopole excitations. Here we propose a driven-dissipative polariton realization of artificial spin ice, in which the circular polarization of each link mode plays the role of an Ising degree of freedom, while an auxiliary lossy vertex mode dynamically enforces a local ice-rule constraint. Adiabatic elimination of the vertex mode yields an effective spin-ice penalty, favoring the two-in two-out manifold in the steady state. We show that local polarization flips generate monopole-antimonopole defects, and that sequential flips transport these defects across the lattice while defining a Dirac string. In an extended spin-ice geometry, the vertex charges and their dynamics can be directly reconstructed from polarization-resolved real-space imaging. Our results establish polariton lattices as a controllable photonic platform for creating, manipulating, and observing emergent gauge charges in nonequilibrium spin-ice systems.

</details>


### [185] [Comparison of Origins of Re-Entrant Supercurrents at High In-Plane Magnetic Fields in Planar InAs-Al Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28530)
*S. R. Mudi,S. Anupam,V. Mourik,S. M. Frolov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文研究了InAs/Al平面约瑟夫森结在高面内磁场下的重入开关电流现象，探讨了这些现象是否表明拓扑或0-π相变，但也提出了无序导致的模式干涉作为替代解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有强自旋轨道耦合的混合超导-半导体系统中的平面约瑟夫森结，旨在探索这些系统是否能够通过磁场驱动的0-π相变进入拓扑态，这对于实现拓扑或三重态超导性具有重要意义。

Method: 实验测量了多个InAs/Al平面约瑟夫森结在高面内磁场下的开关电流特性，观察重入现象。同时进行了模拟研究，分析在存在无序的情况下超电流干涉效应，以区分拓扑相变和模式干涉机制。

Result: 实验观测到多个器件在高磁场下出现重入开关电流现象，其中一些符合预期的拓扑或0-π相变特征。但模拟表明，这些重入现象也可以由结中无序导致的模式干涉效应解释，无需涉及塞曼效应或拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 虽然观测到的重入开关电流现象可能表明拓扑或0-π相变，但研究显示这些现象同样可以用无序导致的模式干涉机制解释。这强调了在解释此类实验数据时需要谨慎，不能仅凭重入现象就断定拓扑相变的发生。

Abstract: Hybrid superconductor-semiconductor systems with large spin-orbit coupling are important platforms for realizing topological or triplet superconductivity. Planar Josephson junctions made using these materials are predicted to enter the topological state by tuning the phase difference between the two superconductors from 0 to $π$. The 0-$π$ transition can be driven by magnetic field through Zeeman splitting of subbands in the semiconductor. It is expected to manifest as a node, or a re-entrance, in the critical current. Here we present re-entrant switching currents from several InAs/Al planar Josephson junctions in high in-plane magnetic fields. We find that re-entrances in some devices conform with expected signatures for topological or 0-$π$ transitions. However, we show that the data can also be explained in terms of mode interference in the junction in the presence of disorder. We also present simulations of supercurrent interference under in-plane fields that can reproduce re-entrances due to corrugated weak link without invoking the Zeeman effect or topology.

</details>


### [186] [Geometry-controlled competition between axis centering and detwinning in fivefold-twinned gold nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28399)
*Silvia Fasce,Diana Nelli,Luca Benzi,Georg Daniel Förster,Riccardo Ferrando*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过分子动力学模拟研究金Marks十面体中五重轴接近表面时的几何控制竞争：凹几何促进表面扩散恢复五重对称，凸几何导致快速去孪晶，轴位于表面下两原子层时恢复稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 五重孪晶金属纳米粒子中的中心楔形向错对其力学和催化性质有重要影响，但控制这种拓扑缺陷稳定性、迁移和湮灭的原子机制尚未完全理解。

Method: 采用系统分子动力学模拟研究金Marks十面体，通过受控几何修饰将五重轴人工靠近表面，生成具有不同轴深度的凹形和凸形形态。

Result: 凹几何促进表面扩散，通过重新居中原始向错或通过集体原子重排成核新的亚表面轴来恢复五重对称；凸结构中的浅轴在最初几纳秒内通过表面滑移快速去孪晶；将轴定位在表面下仅两原子层可抑制去孪晶并恢复稳定性。

Conclusion: 表面曲率和缺陷深度对向错迁移率和孪晶稳定性起关键调节作用，为理解多孪晶纳米粒子的结构演化和指导缺陷工程纳米材料的受控设计提供了机制框架。

Abstract: Fivefold-twinned metal nanoparticles host a central wedge disclination that strongly influences their mechanical and catalytic properties. Yet the atomistic mechanisms governing the stability, migration, and annihilation of this topological defect remain incompletely understood. Here we present a systematic molecular dynamics study of gold Marks decahedra in which the fivefold axis is artificially brought close to the surface by controlled geometric modifications. By generating concave and convex morphologies with varying axis depth, we uncover a geometry-controlled competition between axis centering and detwinning. Concave geometries promote surface diffusion that restores fivefold symmetry, either by recentering the original disclination or by nucleating a new subsurface axis through collective atomic rearrangements. In contrast, convex structures with a shallow axis undergo rapid detwinning within the first nanoseconds via surface glide, leading to single-twin or fully FCC configurations. Remarkably, positioning the axis just two atomic layers beneath the surface suppresses detwinning and restores stability. Our results demonstrate that surface curvature and defect depth critically regulate disclination mobility and twin stability, providing a mechanistic framework to understand the structural evolution of multi-twinned nanoparticles and to guide the controlled design of defect-engineered nanomaterials.

</details>


### [187] [Exciton Polariton-Polariton Interactions in Transition-Metal Dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28409)
*Jonas K König,Jamie M Fitzgerald,Daniel Erkensten,Ermin Malic*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过材料特异性方法分析了MoS₂单层和双层中的极化子-极化子相互作用，揭示了交换、饱和和偶极-偶极相互作用对极化子能级的影响机制，为极化子器件开发提供微观见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖唯象模型，忽略了重要的多体过程。需要基于材料特异性和预测性方法，深入理解极化子-极化子相互作用的微观机制，以推动极化子器件的发展。

Method: 采用材料特异性和预测性方法，研究嵌入法布里-珀罗腔中的单层和双层MoS₂，系统表征交换、饱和和偶极-偶极相互作用对极化子-极化子相互作用的贡献。

Result: 1. 交换相互作用导致失谐腔中上下极化子支的不对称能移，由激子特性差异驱动；2. 温度和电子-光子耦合通过平衡极化子分布决定能量重整化；3. 双层中偶极-偶极相互作用由层间特性介导，可实现电控和电场诱导反交叉闭合。

Conclusion: 获得的极化子-极化子相互作用微观见解对于开发超紧凑极化子电路至关重要，揭示了材料特异性相互作用机制和电控可能性。

Abstract: Microscopic insights into nonlinear interactions are essential for advancing polaritonic devices. Existing studies often rely on phenomenological models that overlook important many-body processes. Based on a material-specific and predictive approach, we investigate monolayer and homobilayer MoS$_2$ embedded in a Fabry-Pérot cavity to characterize the exchange, saturation, and dipole-dipole contributions to polariton-polariton interactions in these technologically promising materials. A key finding is that the exchange interaction induces asymmetric energy shifts of the lower and upper polariton branches in a detuned cavity, a behavior driven by the difference in their excitonic character. Furthermore, we demonstrate that temperature and electron-photon coupling determine the energy renormalization through the equilibrium polariton distribution. In homobilayers, the dipole-dipole interaction is mediated by the interlayer character, enabling electrical control and facilitating the electric-field-induced closing of anti-crossings due to dipolar-interaction shifts. The gained insights on polariton-polariton interactions are important for the development of ultra-compact polaritonic circuitry.

</details>


### [188] [Resonant-enhanced tunneling electroresistance in sliding ferroelectric tunnel junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28482)
*Ruixue Wang,Jiangang Chen,Er Pan,Wunan Wang,Zefen Li,Fan Yang,Hongmiao Zhou,Zhaoren Xie,Qing Liu,Xiao Luo,Junhao Chu,Wenwu Li,Fucai Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于动量守恒共振隧穿的石墨烯电极滑动铁电隧道结，实现了高达225.65%的隧道电阻比，具有多态可编程性、高耐久性、快速开关和低能耗等优异特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着存储器尺寸不断缩小，需要寻找在原子厚度下仍能可靠工作且能耗极低的开关机制。滑动铁电性虽然具有优异的疲劳抗性、超快开关速度和超低矫顽场，但其固有极化强度较弱，限制了可用信号窗口，需要新的物理机制将微弱的极化变化转化为显著的电阻对比。

Method: 引入动量守恒共振隧穿机制，在晶格对齐的石墨烯电极之间构建共振滑动铁电隧道结。通过石墨烯电极的晶格对齐实现动量匹配，增强隧穿过程中的共振效应，从而显著放大微弱的滑动铁电极化变化对隧穿电流的影响。

Result: 实现了高达225.65%的隧道电阻比，远超传统滑动铁电隧道结。器件还展现出可调的TER比、多态可编程性、高电流密度、优异的耐久性（变异系数<0.69%）、快速开关（20 ns）、低开关能量（310 fJ）和低读取电压（<0.2 V）。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了滑动铁电性在连接存储器技术性能与微型化之间桥梁的独特作用，为下一代非易失性存储器技术开辟了新途径。共振隧穿机制成功解决了滑动铁电体极化强度弱的问题，实现了高性能的存储器器件。

Abstract: The escalating demand for memory scaling requires switching mechanisms that remain reliable at atomic thickness while operating with minimal energy consumption. Sliding ferroelectricity provides a promising platform for this challenge: the spontaneous interfacial polarization emerging at superlubric, atomically thin van der Waals interfaces endows exceptional fatigue resistance, ultrafast switching and ultralow coercive fields. Nevertheless, the intrinsically weak polarization of sliding ferroelectrics limits the available signal window, necessitating new physical mechanisms that can transduce subtle polarization variations into pronounced resistance contrasts. Here, we address this challenge by introducing momentum-conserving resonant tunneling between lattice-aligned graphene electrodes. The resulting resonant sliding ferroelectric tunnel junction achieves a tunneling electroresistance (TER) ratio of up to 225.65%, substantially exceeding that of conventional sliding ferroelectric tunnel junctions. In addition, the device delivers a tunable TER ratio, multistate programmability, high current density, robust endurance with a small coefficient of variation (<0.69%), fast switching (20 ns), low switching energy (310 fJ), and low read voltage (<0.2 V). Collectively, these results establish a unique role for sliding ferroelectricity in bridging the gap of memory technology between performance and miniaturization, and open a new pathway toward next-generation nonvolatile memory technologies.

</details>


### [189] [Random fine structure and polarized luminescence of triplet excitons in semiconductor nanocrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28552)
*D. S. Smirnov,E. L. Ivchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出半导体纳米晶系综中三重态激子极化光致发光的理论，考虑电子-空穴交换和载流子-核超精细相互作用导致的随机精细结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究半导体纳米晶系综中三重态激子的极化光致发光特性，这些系综具有由电子-空穴交换和载流子-核超精细相互作用贡献的随机精细结构

Method: 建立理论模型，假设相互作用参数服从正态和各向同性分布，交换相互作用用高斯正交随机矩阵系综描述，计算发光强度、光学取向和光学排列作为精细结构分裂参数和激子寿命的函数

Result: 计算了发光强度、光学取向和光学排列随精细结构分裂参数和激子寿命的变化，分析了纵向磁场下光学排列的抑制和光学取向的增强

Conclusion: 建立了半导体纳米晶系综中三重态激子极化光致发光的完整理论框架，能够描述随机精细结构对光学特性的影响，并揭示了磁场对光学取向和排列的调控作用

Abstract: We present a theory of polarized photoluminescence of triplet excitons in semiconductor nanocrystal ensembles with the random fine structure contributed by the electron-hole exchange and carrier-nuclear hyperfine interactions. The interaction parameters are assumed to be normally and isotropically distributed. In particular, the exchange interaction is described by the Gaussian orthogonal ensemble of random matrices. The intensity of luminescence as well as the optical orientation and alignment are calculated as functions of the fine structure splitting parameters and the exciton lifetime. We have also analyzed the suppression of optical alignment and enhancement of optical orientation in an external longitudinal magnetic field.

</details>


### [190] [Robust Floquet-induced gap in irradiated graphite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28724)
*Fei Wang,Xuanxi Cai,Wanying Chen,Jinxi Lu,Tianshuang Sheng,Xiao Tang,Jiansong Li,Hongyun Zhang,Shuyun Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究利用强中红外泵浦和时间-角分辨光电子能谱，在体相石墨中观测到稳健的Floquet诱导能隙，为操纵狄拉克费米子和实现光工程量子相提供了平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet工程为通过时间周期驱动调控量子材料电子态提供了新途径。关键步骤是通过电子与相干光场的有效耦合，在Floquet布里渊区边界实现光诱导的动力学电子结构修饰（如避免交叉能隙）。

Method: 使用强中红外泵浦和时间-角分辨光电子能谱技术，直接观测体相石墨中的Floquet诱导能隙和相干Floquet边带。

Result: 在价带和导带的共振点都观测到Floquet诱导能隙，并伴随相干Floquet边带。这些能隙和边带与光激发载流子共存，但通过不同的时间尺度可以区分它们的起源。

Conclusion: 在石墨中实现稳健的Floquet诱导能隙，为相干操纵狄拉克费米子和实现光工程量子相建立了平台。

Abstract: Floquet engineering provides an emerging pathway for tailoring the electronic states of quantum materials through time-periodic drive. A critical step along this direction is achieving light-induced modifications of the dynamical electronic structure, such as avoided-crossing gap at the Floquet Brillouin zone boundary, via efficient coupling of electrons with the coherent light-field. Here, we report robust Floquet-induced gap in bulk graphite that persists despite the presence of interlayer coupling and photo-excitation. Using time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy with intense mid-infrared pumping, we directly reveal Floquet-induced gaps at resonance points both in the valence and conduction bands, accompanied by coherent Floquet sidebands. The gap and sidebands coexist with photo-excited carriers, yet their distinct timescales allow us to disentangle their origins. Our demonstration of robust Floquet-induced gaps establishes graphite as a platform for coherent manipulation of Dirac fermions and realization of light-engineered quantum phases.

</details>


### [191] [Observation of Floquet-induced gap in graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28725)
*Fei Wang,Xuanxi Cai,Xiao Tang,Jinxi Lu,Wanying Chen,Tianshuang Sheng,Runfa Feng,Haoyuan Zhong,Hongyun Zhang,Pu Yu,Shuyun Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 实验首次在单层石墨烯中直接观测到Floquet工程诱导的杂化能隙，证实了光场调控量子相的理论预言


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet工程为通过时间周期性驱动创造非平衡相提供了强大途径，但石墨烯中Floquet诱导的杂化能隙（避免交叉能隙）这一关键光谱特征一直缺乏实验证据

Method: 使用强光场共振驱动单层石墨烯，通过时间和角度分辨光电子能谱技术观测Floquet能带交叉处的能隙打开和相干Floquet边带

Result: 直接观测到Floquet诱导的杂化能隙，能隙表现出明显的动量各向异性，具有两个由时空对称性保护的狄拉克节点，且可通过光偏振调控

Conclusion: 该研究首次实验证实了石墨烯中的Floquet能带工程，为石墨烯及相关材料中光场工程量子相的研究开辟了新机遇

Abstract: Floquet engineering provides a powerful pathway for creating non-equilibrium phases of matter with tailored electronic structures and properties through time-periodic driving. As the original theoretical prototype, graphene established the framework in which the Floquet topological insulator with light-induced anomalous Hall effect was proposed. However, the defining spectroscopic signature of Floquet engineering in graphene--light-induced hybridization (avoided-crossing) gap at Floquet band crossings, has remained experimentally elusive. Here, we report direct observation of Floquet-induced hybridization gap in monolayer graphene under resonant driving by a strong light field. Time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy reveals gap opening at Floquet band crossings, accompanied by coherent Floquet sidebands. The gap exhibits pronounced momentum anisotropy, featuring two Dirac nodes protected by the spatiotemporal symmetry and tunable by light polarization. These results provide long-sought experimental demonstration of Floquet band engineering in graphene, opening up opportunities for light-field engineered quantum phases in graphene and related materials.

</details>


### [192] [Topological Optical Chirality Dichroism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28752)
*Wojciech J. Jankowski,Giandomenico Palumbo,Robert-Jan Slager*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文报道了三维手性系统中对手性光的普适拓扑二色性，揭示了手性拓扑不变量导致整数量子化的二色性激发率差异，并提出利用超手性光探测三维材料中手性能带拓扑的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维手性系统对手性光的响应特性，建立光学探测手性电子能带拓扑的新途径，特别是针对迄今为止尚未观测到的手性电子能带拓扑结构。

Method: 通过理论分析手性拓扑不变量与光学手性之间的耦合，证明这种耦合导致整数量子化的二色性激发率差异。进一步将光学手性与高阶张量贝里曲率和Dixmier-Douady不变量（包括Hopf指数）的耦合联系起来。最后提出利用超手性光作为实验探测方案。

Result: 发现手性拓扑不变量导致整数量子化的二色性激发率差异，证明光学手性与高阶张量贝里曲率和Dixmier-Douady不变量的耦合产生拓扑效应，建立了探测三维材料中手性能带拓扑的光学途径。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了光学探测三维手性系统拓扑特性的新范式，特别是利用超手性光作为探测手性能带拓扑的"确凿证据"探针，为观测迄今未发现的手性电子能带拓扑提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We report on a universal topological dichroism of chiral three-dimensional systems in response to the chirality of light. We show that chiral topological invariants result in integer-quantized dichroic excitation rate differences. Moreover, we demonstrate that such topological effects arise more generally from coupling optical chirality to higher tensor Berry curvatures and Dixmier-Douady invariants of quantum states, including Hopf indices. We finally propose an experimental setup that leverages superchiral light as a smoking-gun probe of chiral band topologies in three-dimensional materials. Our findings establish an optical route for probing to date unobserved chiral electronic band topologies.

</details>
